# Completed Milestones — La Ideal

Finished work, split out of [`progress_tracker.md`](progress_tracker.md) so the tracker stays a short, actionable list (current status + open issues). This file is the project history behind the changelog; the customer-facing summary of each release lives in `releases_notes.txt`.

**Rules**:
- Add new entries at the **top** of the current `### Post-<X.Y> development — <Month Year>` section. `X.Y` is the latest *released* version when the work was done (see "Latest release" in the tracker's Current Status). If a release has shipped since the top section was created, start a new section above it. When a release ships, suffix its section header with `(shipped in X.Y)`.
- Format: `- [x] **Title**: what was done, why, key files.` Write fresh prose about the actual change, with the rationale that is not obvious from the diff. Do not paste the original issue text.
- Never delete entries. Entries older than ~6 months with no further action move to **Archive** at the bottom.

---

## Completed Milestones

### Post-11.2 development — October 2026
- [x] **Contabilidad's IVA is the sum of the invoiced cuotas** (pre-12.0 review, contabilidad Medium; owner's decision 20-10-2026): the quarter's IVA repercutido was computed from the summed gross, unrounded, while each AEAT record carries its cuota rounded per invoice. Over a quarter, modelo 303 could differ from what was registered by a few cents. The income and regularisation listings now keep each payment event's gross (`invoices`, per `verifactu_invoice_seq`), and `figuresFromDetails` sums each one's `splitGross` cuota. The base is the gross minus that IVA, and a gasto is split as one supplier invoice. The detail tables use the same split, so their totals still equal the summary to the cent. Test: `test_ivaIsTheSumOfTheInvoicedCuotas`, where two invoices of 10,00 give 1,74 + 1,74 instead of the 3,47 of 20,00.
- [x] **Backups to an optional second folder** (pre-12.0 review, backup Medium "same disk"; owner's decision 20-10-2026): every backup sat next to the live database, on the same disk. Configuración now has *Segunda carpeta de copias* (`backup.second_folder`), for a USB stick or another drive. When it is set, each verified backup is also copied there, written as `.part`, size-checked and renamed, and that folder is pruned with the same retention. A copy that fails, say the stick is out, leaves the main backup successful and says so in the result panel. Test: `test_secondFolderGetsTheCopy`.
- [x] **Configuración says when the gateway refuses the key** (follow-up of the P2 batch): `testConnection` already told a refused key (401 / 403 or a key error in the body) apart from a network failure, but *Probar conexión* still said "No se pudo conectar al servidor" for both. A refused key now gets "Clave rechazada": the server answers but refuses the service key. The status mapping is pinned by `test_testConnectionReportsARefusedKey`. The message box itself is not driven by a test.
- [x] **Anular follows the whole chain of rectificativas** (follow-up of the P2 batch's audit): the guard against cancelling an invoice that a standing rectificativa corrects looked one level down. With a rectificativa R1 substituted by R2 (R1 RECTIFICADA, R2 pointing at R1), cancelling the original was allowed, and R2's amount stayed in the books. The lookup moved from the dialog into `sql_lite::liveRectificationOf`, a recursive query that finds the accepted rectificativa at any depth. Test: `test_liveRectificationOfFollowsTheChain`.
- [x] **No endless duplicate check in the direct client** (follow-up of the P2 batch's audit): when AEAT answered "duplicado" but its query never returned the record (another date or period), the record went resend -> duplicate -> query every retry delay, for ever. `verifyDuplicate` now counts those rounds. After 5, the record is an ERROR (refused, in the refused count) with "compruébela con Consultar en AEAT". The fake AEAT gains `hideFromQuery`. Test: `test_duplicateAeatNeverShowsEndsInError`.
- [x] **No gateway rectificativa in production** (follow-up of the compliance decision on the gateway's missing `RectificationItems`): the gateway's rectificativas do not say which invoice they rectify (`FacturasRectificadas`). That is acceptable in AEAT's pre-production, where the gateway stays for 12.0, but not for a real invoice. `VerifactuIntegration::submitRectificationAsync` now refuses one when the gateway is in PRODUCTION, with a message to use the direct connection, which sends the field. Test: `test_gatewayRectificativaRefusedInProduction`.
- [x] **No rebuilt QR on a reprint with the direct client** (follow-up of the direct-client hardening): an accepted row without a stored `verifactu_url_qr`, which only older gateway rows have, still fell back to `generateQRAsync`. With the direct client that rebuilt the URL from today's environment and IVA rate, which may not be the invoice's. `Imprimir::resolveQrCode` now prints such a row without QR when the connection is direct, and logs it; the gateway still answers its own `/GetQrCode`. Test: `test_reprintWithoutStoredUrlHasNoQr`.
- [x] **The chain verifier also catches a deleted record** (follow-up of the direct-client hardening): `AeatChainCheck` took any link to a huella this database does not hold as a restored copy's continuation, so a record deleted from `aeat_records` went unnoticed. A sync that moved the head back to an older stored record would also have raised a false alarm. Each head a chain sync adopts (`adoptTip`) is now stored as an anchor (`aeat_chain_anchors`). `verifyStoredChain` accepts a continuation, or a link to an older stored record, only when it is an anchor. A store from before the anchors takes the continuations it has on that day as its first anchors, once, so an existing copy raises no false alarm. The Req. 1 wording now says deletions are detected. Tests: `test_anchorsMakeADeletedRecordVisible`, `test_anchoredHeadMovedBack`.
- [x] **The start-up recovery never resends an invoice that is still on its way** (follow-up of the Cobrar store-first change): the recovery opens 4 s after start-up and lists every `PENDIENTE` event. A Cobrar pressed in those seconds stores its rows `PENDIENTE` before sending, so Reintentar could resend an invoice in flight: a duplicate with the gateway, a re-queue with the direct client. `VerifactuIntegration` now tracks each invoice it sent until its answer (`isInFlight`). `PendingSubmitsDialog` does not list such an invoice, and its Reintentar refuses one with "se está enviando ahora mismo". Test: `test_pendingRecoverySkipsAnInvoiceBeingSent`.
- [x] **A rectificativa por diferencias keeps the invoice above zero** (pre-12.0 review, app A11): the I-type difference had no lower bound, so -500 on a 20 € ticket gave a negative corrected invoice. The dialog now adds the original, the differences already issued (`sql_lite::rectifiedDifferences`: accepted or pending, refused and cancelled ones left out) and the new one. It refuses the difference unless the result stays above 0, before anything is numbered, stored or sent, and points to Anular factura for an invoice that should go away. Tests: `test_rectifiedDifferences`, `test_rectify_differenceCannotMakeTheTotalNegative`.
- [x] **No row rewrites without a copy of the database** (follow-up of the pre-12.0 backup fix): when the backup a new version takes before migrating failed, `migrateDatabase()` still ran its data repairs (duplicate-hash repair, rounding, comma decimals) on the only copy. Now `MainWindow` then calls `migrateDatabase(db, false)`, which runs the schema steps (tables, columns, closures, guards), so the app works, and leaves every row rewrite for a later start that does take the copy. The red message says the repairs wait. Test: `test_migrateDatabase_schemaOnlyLeavesRowsAlone`.
- [x] **Pre-12.0 P2 / P3, group 7: connections, layering, facade and leaks** (pre-12.0 review; 00 X1 / X2 / X3 / X5 / X7, sql_lite F13, P3). About 40 `sql_lite` functions opened and closed the shared default connection themselves, closing it under other windows' models (one cause of the 256-row cuts) and getting early returns wrong. The RAII `ConnectionScope` replaces all 57 open / close pairs: a connection the caller holds open stays open, and every return closes only what was opened (`test_connectionScope_leavesTheConnectionAsFound`). `sql_lite`, `add_garment`, `facturas` and `printing` included `../verifactu/verifactutypes.h` by relative path without linking anything. They now link the header-only `verifactu_types` INTERFACE target. `sql_lite`'s two reads of AeatStore's `aeat_records` table are named helpers, and `test_sqlLiteReadsTheRealTable` runs them against the table AeatStore builds. The windows reached past the facade into the direct backend. `VerifactuIntegration` now answers what they asked (`isDirect`, `holdsPendingRegistration`, `storedRegistrationOutcome`, `environmentName`, `sendingError`, `certificateExpiryWarning`, `verifyStoredChain`, `syncDirectChain`), and only the self-test, the export, the self-check and the tests still use `directBackend()`. `verifactu` is a STATIC library with the keyword link signature, and the appsettings -> printing edge is documented. Leaks: the windows opened from the menus close with `WA_DeleteOnClose`, the `Imprimir` instances are deleted after printing, `Imprimir` reuses its model, and Listado / Recogida delete the models they replace. The leftovers (delegates, colours, `tr()`, the call sites outside `sql_lite`) are filed. Drafted by two Sonnet subagents in their own worktrees, merged and reviewed here.
- [x] **Pre-12.0 P2, group 6: one split of the gross for every invoice** (pre-12.0 review; 00 X4). The base and cuota of an invoice were computed in four places, three different ways. The facade sent the gateway an unrounded base, with the cuota as base x rate. The rectificativa dialog took gross minus base. The printed factura rounded the base through `QString::number`. So the record's base + cuota and the printed figures could differ from the amount charged by a cent. Now `splitGross` (header-only, `verifactutypes.h`) rounds the base to cents, half away from zero, and takes the cuota as the rest. The facade, both rectificativa amounts, `TicketRenderer` and the gastos window use it, so base + cuota is always the gross. The callers keep passing gross / (1 + rate), unrounded, and the facade splits it back in cents (a rounded base would lose a cent). `test_splitGrossAddsUpForEveryAmount` checks every cent from -20,00 to 1.000,00. Left for the accountant: whether modelo 303 carries the sum of the per-record cuotas (tracker). Docs: the direct-client investigation is marked as a historical report, and the Verifactu README shows both backends, the amount split and the restore limit. The compliance-auditor step was skipped by the owner's instruction (until `/release`).
- [x] **Pre-12.0 P2, group 5: the test gaps** (pre-12.0 review; 00 §5). The post-update self-check gains four automatic checks on the shop's own copy. M1a: the settings file is readable and points at an existing database. M1b: the newest backup is at most 3 days old. M1d: a second `migrateDatabase` changes nothing, by a SHA-256 of the copy. M1e: counts of unreadable amounts and unrecognised IVA rates, counts only. The e2e bench gains the gateway's failure modes: HTTP 500, 401, malformed JSON, a dropped connection and "success" without CSV all leave the payment `PENDIENTE` and never `ERROR`, and a stalled reply ends as NETWORK_ERROR at the 30 s timeout. It also gains Recogida's remaining search modes (Anulación dates, accents, not found) and Cobrar pressed twice while AEAT answers. That last test found that the slot could be re-entered: `PayDialog::onCobrarClicked` now returns while a reply is pending. Each new test was shown to fail on a temporary break of the code it pins. Drafted by the test-engineer subagent and reviewed here.
- [x] **Pre-12.0 P2, group 3b: a sturdier migrateDatabase, a silent data layer** (pre-12.0 review; sql_lite F8 / F12 / F18, 00 X8). `migrateDatabase` never checked that the database opened, and it closed a connection its caller had open. Its multi-row repairs (split relink, new hashes, rounding) ran row by row without a transaction. The "review manually" warnings repeated at every start until real problems drowned. The app could not start on an empty file, since nothing created `ingresos` or the other base tables. And uniqueness of the `(n_recibo, hash)` key every write uses was only repaired, never enforced. Now: the open is checked and a caller's connection stays open. Each repair loop is all or none. A notice is logged once (`migration_notes`). An empty file gets the base tables (`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`, the shop's own columns). After the hash repair a unique index keeps `(n_recibo, hash)` unique; it is not built while a sent row still shares its hash, which is logged for review. `sql_lite` also showed eight `QMessageBox`es, which popped up in windows that promise no pop-ups and in the self-check, and blocked failure-path tests. They are gone: failures are logged and returned, and `sql_lite` links Qt Gui, not Widgets. Tests: `test_migrateDatabase_baseSchemaNoticesOnceAndUniqueKey`, with `test_migrateDatabase_reportsAFailedStep` and the duplicate-hash test updated. Still open: the `ConnectionScope` RAII (X7 / F13).
- [x] **Pre-12.0 P2 / P3, group 4: invoice dialogs, estado, gateway and direct-client edge cases** (pre-12.0 review; app A8-A10 / A21, verifactu-direct, verifactu-gateway, 00 P3). Anular: one press cancelled an invoice at AEAT. Now the first press says what will be cancelled and the button turns into *Confirmar anulación*. Anular is also refused while an accepted rectificativa points at the ticket, which would leave its delta in the books. Rectificar read an arbitrary group of a mixed ticket and now uses its paid seq-0 invoice. Both dialogs are on UiKit, with AEAT's text escaped. An estado string no version wrote fell back to PENDIENTE and could be resent. It is now `VerifactuEstado::Unknown`: logged, never resent, cancelled, rectified or voided, and printed without QR or leyenda. The gateway's `testConnection` reported OK on a 401; a refused key is now its own answer, and the unused tax-item / invoice-type enums are gone. Direct client: `NombreRazonEmisor` is cut to the XSD's 120 characters, and a qrcodegen exception gives no QR instead of a crash. Two cases left a waiting request unanswered; it is now always answered. WinHTTP resolves and connects within 10 s on a private thread pool, so closing never waits on AEAT. A SOAP fault on a whole submission used to refuse up to 1000 records AEAT never judged. Those records now stay pending and are resent one by one, not counted as reached. A duplicate answer was adopted blindly. AEAT's own record is now queried first: another amount is an ERROR (another invoice under our number), an unreadable one stays pending, and it is ours only if an earlier send reached AEAT with our huella. With `LAIDEAL_TEST=1`, set for every CTest test, both transports refuse any host but this machine. The compliance audit passed and approved the four decisions (Anular refusal, duplicate-amount ERROR, one-by-one resend for now, the gateway's missing `RectificationItems` as a documented 12.0 limit). Its notes were applied (Unknown prints no QR, an unreadable amount stays pending, a faulted batch is not reached) or filed. Drafted by an Opus subagent and reviewed here.
- [x] **Pre-12.0 P2, group 3a: amounts read strictly, invoice rows guarded by the database** (pre-12.0 review; sql_lite F4 / F6 / F16 / F21, app A13). `moneyText` turned unreadable text ("1.234,50", "abc") into 0.00, so a garbage amount was stored as a free garment. The new `moneyParse` reads an amount strictly, and the amount writers and `insertGarmentRow` refuse anything else. The Verifactu feeds summed `importe` as text, so a legacy "5,50" counted as 5 and a resend went out short. They now sum `INGRESOS_IMPORTE_VALUE` and round the totals to cents. The client autofill took the first name starting with what was typed: "Ana" got "Ana Maria"'s phone and address, and the save wrote them onto Ana. `searchItemFromClient` now matches the exact name, and the generic prefix search takes `%` and `_` literally. Req. 1 rested on application code alone. `migrateDatabase` now adds two triggers. One refuses deleting a row registered at AEAT (a CSV, or ENVIADA / ANULADA / RECTIFICADA). The other refuses changing an ENVIADA row's invoice identity (`n_recibo`, `fecha_pago`, `pagado`, CSV, XML, `verifactu_hash`, InvoiceID, seq) unless the write leaves it equal. The estado, the cancellation date, the pickup and the amount (Separar keeps the invoice total over the two parts) still move. The test helpers that shape data by hand drop the triggers first, and the app's own paths run with them in every e2e test that builds a MainWindow. Tests: `test_moneyParse_strict`, `test_pendingEventsSumParsedAmounts`, `test_clientLookupExactAndWildcardsEscaped`, `test_ingresosGuardTriggers`.
- [x] **Pre-12.0 P2, group 2: updater, gastos invoices, report files, Listado** (pre-12.0 review; updater, facturas, reporthtml, listado, tableview and contabilidad rows). Updater: startup and manual checks shared one "silent" flag. Silent is now carried per request, and a second check while one runs is ignored. During the download the dialog offers Cancelar (the reply is aborted) and ignores Esc / the window close. `TMP` / `TEMP` are set only in the installer process's environment, never the app's. A tag that is not exactly X.Y fails the check instead of reading as newer. Facturas (gastos): the amount accepted `inf`, `nan`, exponents, zero and negatives. `Facturas::parseGross` now takes only a plain positive amount. The same supplier and number is refused with the existing invoice shown, fields are trimmed, and the id comes from the insert itself, which removes the `readMax...` pop-up. Contabilidad: regenerating a closed quarter replaced the filed PDF. It now writes `<name>_regenerado_YYYYMMDD.pdf`, and every PDF is written as `.part` and renamed, so a locked or failed write keeps the old one. Reports: the business identity and the listing cells are HTML-escaped, and the prendas PDF reports a failed write in red. Listado: `n_recibo` sorted as text, so 100000 came before 99999. It now sorts numerically, as do the amounts, and `fecha_anulacion` sorts as a date. The search filters once per keystroke and covers up to Observaciones. Searching or sorting first loads every row, and ingresos is read-only at the model level too (the proxy drops `ItemIsEditable` and refuses `setData`, Req. 1). Tests in `test_updater`, `test_facturas`, `test_contabilidad`, `test_reporthtml` and `test_mysortfilterproxymodel`. Drafted by a subagent and reviewed here. Open: the first 256-row ingresos batch is still ordered by text in SQL until the user sorts or filters.
- [x] **Pre-12.0 P2 / P3, group 1: printing, backups, log, CI and installer** (pre-12.0 review; printing F3-F5 / F17, backup and logging Medium rows, 00 C1 / C3 / C5 / G11). Printing: the CP858 table mapped Ì to 0xDD, the broken bar. It is now 0xDE, and a test checks the table against hard-coded CP858 values rather than the preview decoder, which had the same error. C0 control characters in printed text become a space, so a name can no longer carry a printer command. The RAW path said "impreso" for a queue the spooler held offline, which would print later as a duplicate. It now refuses an offline, worked-offline or unavailable queue; paper-out and an open cover are left to the Epson Status API, whose spooler flags can be stale. The vendored qrcodegen is pinned by SHA-256 with a notice. Backups: the newest 3 copies are always kept. Each month keeps its last copy, not its earliest. The boundaries use `addDays(30)` / `addYears(4)`, and a clock that is wrong prunes nothing. `backup.last_error` records a failure, and every start warns in the result panel when backups are off or none was correct for 3 days (`BackupManager::currentBackupWarning`). The log rotates during a session too, never loses `.old` on a failed rename, and falls back to the temp folder without crashing (`installAt` test seam, new `test_applogger`). CI and release: the UI smoke also runs on pull requests to master, and `release.ps1` runs ctest and zips like CI. The installer drops the dead `.myp` association, removes the previous version's Qt DLLs and plugin folders before copying, and offers *Comprobar la instalación* (`--self-check`), unchecked. `.gitignore` covers `*.sqlite`, `*.pfx` and `*.p12` (test fixtures excepted). The unused icons are removed. Drafted by a subagent; reviewed here, with accents added to its messages and the offline check narrowed. Open: backup's second folder (a product option) and the stale remote branch.
- [x] **Documentation drift of the pre-12.0 review** (00 §4 G1-G6, G8-G10): the docs described the app before the direct AEAT client. `architecture.md` now has both Verifactu backends, the `aeat*` classes and the `aeat_chain` / `aeat_records` / `aeat_chain_sync` tables, with settings in the AppSettings JSON (not a QSettings INI). The README configuration table listed keys that do not exist (`db.path`, `app.ivaRate`, `verifactu.serviceKey`, ...) and missed most real ones. It is rebuilt from `appsettings.cpp`, keys only, no values. `ai_agent_instructions.md` (Qt 6.4.3, the DB path, Ninja, the file map), `INDEX.md` (version, the six estados, test count), `docs/README.md` and `docs/testing/README.md` (the fake AEAT server, the self-check layer) are updated. The release skill now points to CONTRIBUTING as the source of truth. `rest_api.md` loses its advice to log the ServiceKey and gains an "outdated, the code is authoritative" banner, and the printer 04 / 05 notes are marked Excel-era. The edits were drafted by a subagent and reviewed here.
- [x] **Compliance-audit follow-ups of the blocker fixes** (pre-12.0 review): a gateway rectificativa answered "success" without AEAT's CSV, now PENDING, was stored as ERROR. AEAT may hold it, though, and the original stayed counted until it was redone. It is now stored PENDIENTE with that reason, for Consultar en AEAT. The start-up orphan rule (`markRectificationUnsent`) only turns a placeholder with no answer at all, as after a crash or a window closed unanswered, into an ERROR to redo. A settings save that fails, such as the one storing the direct client's NumeroInstalacion and chain marker, was only logged. `AppSettings::lastSaveError` now feeds a red start-up message, since a new installation number after a restart would change the records. The audit's other points: Anular during the first chain sync now says why (the facade gate, item 4). Whether AEAT ever answers a duplicate on a live cancellation is checked in the M6 pre-production run at `/release`. The legacy reprint limitation is by design. Tests: `test_markRectificationUnsent_onlyUnanswered`, `test_lastSaveErrorReported`.
- [x] **Every ingresos writer is a guarded sql_lite function** (pre-12.0 review; 00 X6, sql_lite F2 / F3 / F5 / F7): six `UPDATE` / `INSERT ingresos` statements still lived in PayDialog, RectifyInvoiceDialog and PendingSubmitsDialog, each without some of the usual guards. They are now named `sql_lite` functions. `storeChargedGarments` is Cobrar's all-or-none store, now refused in a closed quarter. `markPendingEventUnreconciled` is the recovery's *Marcar como error*. The rectificativa row has four: `insertRectificativaRow`, refused in a closed quarter and with its amount through `moneyText`; `resetRefusedRectificativa`, which only rewrites a row still in ERROR; `applyRectificativaResult`, where a refusal or unknown outcome never rewrites an ENVIADA row (the missing settled guard: a late duplicate rejection turned an accepted rectificativa into ERROR); and `markRectificativaUnanswered`. The dialogs keep their own decisions, such as the estado of an unknown outcome. `applySettledVerifactuCancellation` left the connection open on four of its five paths and stamped today's date even in a quarter closed meanwhile. It now closes on every path and dates the cancellation through `openPeriodDate`. The 10.12 void-date backfill re-matched voided rows with no date to move at every start, so its "idempotent" claim was false. It now skips them. Tests: `test_storeChargedGarments_allOrNone`, `test_rectificativaWritersKeepTheSettledRow`, `test_markPendingEventUnreconciled`, `test_applySettledVerifactuCancellation_closesAndDatesOpenPeriod`, `test_migrateDatabase_voidDateStepSkipsDatelessRows`.
- [x] **Direct client hardening: the chain is verified, the QR reprinted as issued, the certificate watched** (pre-12.0 review; 00 §2.18 and G7, verifactu-direct, imprimir F6): four gaps of the direct AEAT client before production. (a) The chain sync skipped any query record whose `SistemaInformatico` block was missing as "another system's". If AEAT ever omitted the block, a first sync with no local records would restart the chain with `PrimerRegistro` over our own records. Such a record is now unusable, like one without its huella, and the first sync fails. (b) A reprint asked the backend for the QR, and the direct client rebuilt the URL from the current environment and today's IVA rate. After the production switch every older test invoice would print a production QR with the leyenda. Reprints of an accepted invoice now draw the QR from the `verifactu_url_qr` stored with AEAT's answer, for the gateway and the direct client alike, with no request. (c) Nothing warned before the certificate expired, and one expiring mid-run gave an endless generic retry. The start now warns under 30 days (`AeatDirectBackend::expiryWarning`), and a failed send with an expired certificate stops the sending with that reason. (d) Req. 1 claimed tamper detection that no code did (art. 8.1). The new pure `AeatChainCheck::verify` recomputes every stored record's huella from its XML and checks the links per issuer, failing a reordering or a restarted chain. It runs at every start (`verifyStoredChain`, red result-panel message, `qCritical`) and in the post-update self-check (M1c). A link to a huella only AEAT holds, as in a restored copy, is counted, not reported. The requirements doc now says what is and isn't detected: deletions and `ingresos` edits are not. Tests: `test_aeat_chaincheck` (new suite: valid chain, edited XML, reordering, restart, continuation, separate issuers), `test_firstSyncFailsWhenAeatOmitsTheSystemBlock`, `test_certificateExpiryWarning`, `test_reprintUsesTheStoredVerificationUrl`, `test_checkChain`. The AeatChainCheck core was drafted by a subagent and reviewed and reworked here (per-issuer chains, continuations). The audit follow-ups are filed in the tracker.
- [x] **Cobrar stores the payment before it submits the invoice** (pre-12.0 review; 00 §2.9, recog_prendas #4 / #6 / #7): PayDialog sent the invoice to AEAT first and only marked the garments paid when the reply came. A crash, kill or power cut during the round trip therefore left an invoice at AEAT, or queued in `aeat_records`, for a ticket the database showed unpaid. That was the only invoicing path that did not store first, unlike `saveTicket`. `PayDialog::storePayment` now writes the selected garments as paid + `PENDIENTE`, with their seq and InvoiceID, in one transaction before anything is sent. It is all or none: a garment paid from another window meanwhile stops the whole charge before anything goes out. Only then is the invoice submitted, and the reply (`applyReply`) writes `ENVIADA` / `Error` through `updateTicketVerifactuFields`. A timeout, a transport failure or a reply-time `INVALID_CONFIG`, which used to leave the paid rows with an empty estado, now keeps them `PENDIENTE` for the recovery. Cobrar now refuses a 0,00 total and a missing Verifactu integration, which booked an invoice with no record. A local refusal to send keeps the payment `PENDIENTE` and shows the reason in Recogida (`notSentReason`) instead of a message box. Because the payment is now stored before any close can happen, two earlier paths became impossible and were removed: the "quarter closed while AEAT answered" warning and the "garments not stored after sending" report. The `e2e_app` suite gets its own 300 s ctest timeout: it takes about 105 s on the CI runner and passed the 120 s default. Tests: `test_payDialog_storesThePaymentBeforeSubmitting`, `test_payDialog_garmentPaidMeanwhileStopsTheCharge`, `test_payDialog_refusesZeroTotalAndNoIntegration`.
- [x] **Searches return every row; dates, garments and quarter closes are bounded** (pre-12.0 review; recog_prendas #2 / #5, add_garment, contabilidad, app A17, sql_lite F14): `QSqlQueryModel` fetches 256 rows at a time. Recogida's phone, ticket and date searches closed the connection before the rest came, so a large client or a busy day showed 256 rows and an understated Importe total. Every search now goes through `loadEveryRow`. The payment date, which is the invoice date AEAT gets, had no bounds in Cobrar or Añadir prendas. It is now between the reception date and today, the pickup date too, and the MainWindow reception date and the rectification date are capped at today. Añadir prendas looked up the price of whatever was typed, so a free-typed garment opened a "no se ha encontrado la prenda" pop-up and could be saved as a 0,00 EUR invoice. It now looks up only an exact garment-list entry, and save refuses an unknown garment, a quantity under 1 and a 0,00 importe. `garmentImporte` now also clamps a negative size. Contabilidad could close the quarter that contains today, which refuses every payment of the day. It now closes a quarter only from its last day (`Contabilidad::quarterLastDay`), with a warning on that day; before that it writes the report and leaves the quarter open. A gastos IVA stored as text such as "21,0" or "abc" parsed silently to 0 and was summed as SIN IVA. `sql_lite::expenseIvaRate` now maps anything that is not a whole number to -1, which is left out, flagged in the detail and counted in the red warning. Tests: `test_recogida_searchReturnsEveryRowBeyondTheFirstBatch` (300 garments: phone, ticket, date, name), `test_addGarment_refusesAGarmentNotInTheList`, `test_paymentDatesBoundedByReceptionAndToday`, `test_contabilidad_unfinishedQuarterIsNotClosed`, `test_expenseIvaRate_strict`, `test_quarterLastDay`, `test_unrecognisedIvaCounted` and the negative-size case. The lower bound of an I-type rectification amount (app A11) is still open.
- [x] **The factura prints its date of issue; a cancelled or rectified invoice reprints marked as such** (pre-12.0 review; printing F1 / F2, imprimir F3): the factura simplificada printed only "Recepción" and an unlabelled print time. Its date of issue, required by art. 7 RD 1619/2012 and the `FechaExpedicion` in the QR and at AEAT, was nowhere on the paper. It now prints "Fecha de expedición": the paid rows' `fecha_pago`, set by `Imprimir::buildTicket` into `TicketData::issueDate`. A reprint of an invoice AEAT holds as ANULADA or RECTIFICADA used to come out as a normal factura, with a panel saying it was "not confirmed". It now carries `*** FACTURA ANULADA ***` / `*** FACTURA RECTIFICADA ***` and "Factura anulada el <fecha>. Esta copia no es una factura en vigor.", with no QR or leyenda, and the renderer enforces that too. The panel says the copy is marked. The "Factura completa" heading stays FACTURA SIMPLIFICADA: the AEAT record is F2, and art. 7.2 RD 1619/2012 allows the recipient's NIF and address on a simplificada. A completa now requires the DNI / NIF, since art. 7.2 is about a recipient who asks for one, and the renderer prints no empty "DNI:" line. The "Todas las facturas" panel names any copy printed as anulada or rectificada. The QR date and the printed date now come from the same paid-row scan. Tests: `test_renderFacturaIssueDate`, `test_renderVoidedFacturaMarked`, `test_renderCompleteInvoiceWithoutDni`, and the e2e reprint of a cancelled invoice.
- [x] **The updater runs only a verified installer; the deploy check covers the SQLite driver and TLS** (pre-12.0 review; updater High, 00 C2): the downloaded installer was launched with no integrity check at all, so a tampered release or a cut-off download would run as the shop user. The release now publishes `laideal_setup_X.Y.exe.sha256`, written by both `ci.yml` and `releases/release.ps1`. `Updater` reads the asset's size, takes the SHA-256 from GitHub's asset `digest` or from that file, and refuses a release that offers neither. It accepts only https URLs and redirects on github.com / `*.githubusercontent.com`. The installer is written as `.part`, and `downloadFinished` fires only when the size and SHA-256 match. The download gives up after 60 s without data instead of hanging. The windeployqt assertion in CI, now also in `release.ps1`, did not list `sqldrivers\qsqlite.dll` or `tls\qschannelbackend.dll`. Without them the installed app has no database, and every HTTPS call fails silently. Pure helpers (`findInstallerAsset`, `isTrustedDownloadUrl`, `parseChecksumFile`, `installerMismatch`) are unit-tested in `test_updater.cpp`.
- [x] **Backups that cannot destroy a good copy, and a backup before each version's migration** (pre-12.0 review; backup High, app A14): `VACUUM INTO` wrote straight into the final `laideal_<ts>.db`, which caused two problems. A copy cut off by a crash looked like a valid backup, so it could survive as a month's copy and lead to the good ones being pruned. And a second run in the same second failed and then deleted the existing good copy. Copies are now written as `.part`, checked with `integrity_check` and renamed. A run removes only files it created, and stale `.part` files are cleaned up. An atomic guard allows one run at a time, since the automatic worker and the manual action share the connection names. The snapshot connection waits 30 s on a busy database. `performBackup()` no longer writes `AppSettings` from the worker thread: the caller records the result with `BackupManager::recordSuccess()` on the GUI thread. `~MainWindow` waits for the worker, and a last-backup time in the future counts as due. `migrateDatabase()` used to run on a new version before any backup of it. Now the first start of a new version (`backup.last_version`) takes a synchronous backup first. `migrateDatabase()` returns `false` when a step failed, the result panel says so, and the self-check uses the return value. Tests: real backups of a throwaway DB in `test_backup_manager` (rename, stale `.part`, same second, concurrent run, future last time), and `test_migrateDatabase_reportsAFailedStep`.
- [x] **The chain gate lives in the Verifactu facade; Enter presses the focused button** (pre-12.0 review; app A5 / A6, uikit): the "first chain check must finish" gate was only in the windows. Anular and the start-up *Reintentar* skipped it, so a cancellation or resend could be chained on a stale record. `VerifactuIntegration::submitSimplifiedInvoiceAsync`, `cancelInvoiceAsync` and `submitRectificationAsync` now refuse while the chain is unchecked, whoever asks, and Anular shows the reason. *Reintentar* emitted a signal and always said "reenviado", even when nothing was sent. `PendingSubmitsDialog::retry` now returns why nothing was sent, and the row stays listed with that reason. In every UiKit dialog, Enter on a focused secondary button, Cerrar included, pressed the primary action, possibly an AEAT send, because secondary buttons are not auto-default. The Enter filter now clicks the dialog's focused button first. Tests: the chain-gate e2e extended to Anular and the facade, `test_pendingRetryKeepsTheRowWhenNothingWasSent`, `test_enterOnFocusedSecondaryButtonPressesIt`.
- [x] **Settings safety** (pre-12.0 review, appsettings #2-#6): (1) Switching from the direct client to the gateway with its hidden PRODUCCIÓN box still ticked saved `aeat_direct_production = true`, so coming back skipped the production confirmation. Only an active direct production is saved now. (2) The gateway's PRODUCCIÓN box switched to the real AEAT without a question; it is now confirmed going in and coming out. (3) `save()` removed the old file before renaming the new one, leaving a window with no settings at all; it now uses `QSaveFile`. (4) A settings file that failed to parse was overwritten with defaults by the next save; it is now kept aside as `.corrupt-<timestamp>`. (5) The plaintext legacy `~/.verifactu_key` stayed on disk after migration; it is now removed once the encrypted copy is saved. Tests in `test_settingsdialog` and `test_appsettings`.
- [x] **No personal data in the log** (pre-12.0 review, logging P1): `~/.laideal.log` is what the shop sends to support, but it carried client names, phones and the address of every new client (`addNewClient`, `updateItemToClient`, `readClientPhones`, the AddGarment insert line). Those messages now keep only the operation and the SQL error text, and `/coding-guidelines` gains a section on what never goes into the log: secrets and clients' personal data. Unit `test_clientWritesLogNoPersonalData` captures every message of the client writes, the error paths included.
- [x] **Direct client: the installation number is saved at once; a lost cancellation reply settles as cancelled** (pre-12.0 review blocker 10; appsettings #1, verifactu-direct High): `aeatInstallationNumber()` generated the NumeroInstalacion every record carries, and `setAeatChainMarker()` stored the chain marker, but neither saved the settings. A crash before an unrelated save gave the next run a different number. Both now `save()` at once. When the reply to a cancellation was lost and the stored record was resent, AEAT's answer "duplicate, invoice Anulada" went through the alta branch and became an ERROR: the books kept the income and Anular looped. A cancellation reported as a duplicate, in any state, is now accepted, and claimed as ours only when an earlier send reached AEAT. The fake AEAT now answers a repeated anulación as AEAT does, so `test_cancellationLostReplyResentIsAccepted` exercises it; it fails without the fix. Still to confirm against real AEAT pre-production (M6). Unit `test_installationNumberAndChainMarkerPersistAtOnce`.
- [x] **Gateway: no request waits forever, and an unreadable or CSV-less reply is never a final state** (pre-12.0 review blocker 9, verifactu-gateway; the shop runs 12.0 on the gateway): only *probar conexión* had a timeout, so a stalled submit, cancel, query or QR never answered. The pending ticket got no late reply and Consultar en AEAT waited forever. `createNetworkRequest` now gives every request a 30 s transfer timeout. `parseVerifactuResponse` is told what it answers (`VerifactuReply::Submit` / `Cancel` / `Qr`). A body that is not JSON, or has no `ResultCode` (a proxy page, a truncated reply), is now a NETWORK_ERROR: an unknown outcome, the row stays PENDIENTE and is checked. Before, it was a definitive AEAT refusal that cleared the InvoiceID and joined the red count. A submit "success" without AEAT's CSV is now PENDING instead of ENVIADA, which had no CSV, QR or Huella and was never checked again. A cancellation may still succeed without a CSV. Unit tests replace the one that locked the old behaviour.
- [x] **A reprint is never blank nor a merge of payment events** (pre-12.0 review blocker 8; imprimir F1 / F2): Recogida's *Imprimir factura* with no row selected loaded every row of the ticket. It printed all its payment events as one factura, with a QR whose amount even included unpaid garments: an invoice AEAT never received. It now prints the ticket's only payment event, or asks to select a garment of the one to reprint when there are several (`submittedInvoiceEvents`). The engine guards itself too: `Imprimir::resolveQrCode` sums only paid rows and draws no QR for rows of more than one event. The dialog searched a trimmed number but loaded the untrimmed one, and a ticket whose garments were all voided passed the existence check. Both printed a blank recibo reported as "impreso". The number is now trimmed, `buildTicket` builds nothing without printable rows, and the dialog says the ticket has nothing to print. e2e `test_reprintNeverBlankNorMerged`.
- [x] **A failed database read is never an empty period nor ticket number 1** (pre-12.0 review blocker 7; sql_lite F1 / F10, contabilidad High): the Contabilidad listings returned an empty list when their query failed (a locked database, a broken column). The report then showed zero income and, with *Bloquear*, closed the quarter on it. `nextTicketNumber` read a failed MAX as 0 and handed out ticket number 1. The collectors now return whether the query ran, and the public listings, `readMaxValueInColumnFromTable` and `nextTicketNumber` take an optional `bool *ok`. Contabilidad writes no report and closes nothing on a failed read and says so. `nextTicketNumber` returns 0: MainWindow shows no number and refuses to save; Rectificar, Facturas and the self-check refuse too. `readMaxValueInColumnFromTable` no longer pops a modal box from the data layer; its callers report in their panel. Unit `test_failedReadsAreReportedNotEmpty`, e2e `test_contabilidad_unreadableDataNeitherReportsNorCloses`.
- [x] **One copy of the app per database** (pre-12.0 review blocker 6; app A4): nothing stopped two copies running on the same database. Both would hand out the same next ticket number (MAX + 1 twice) and, with the direct client, fork the AEAT hash chain. `main()` now takes `InstanceLock::acquire(dbPath)`, a `QLockFile` keyed on the normalised database path in the user's temp folder; it is not next to the database, because that folder may be synced by OneDrive to other PCs. A second start waits up to 3 s for a copy that is closing, then says "La Ideal ya está abierto" and exits. A lock left by a crashed copy is taken over, since Qt checks its process. `--self-check` works on a copy and is exempt. New unit suite `test_instancelock` (path normalisation, refusal until release, another database free, a crashed copy's lock).
- [x] **Cobrar, Anular and Rectificar cannot be closed while AEAT answers** (pre-12.0 review blocker 5; recog_prendas #1, app A7): Esc or the window X during "Enviando a AEAT..." rejected the dialog. Cobrar's garments then stayed unpaid while AEAT could already hold the invoice, and nothing adopted the reply. Anular and Rectificar could be closed the same way. Each dialog now overrides `done()`, the exit Esc, X and Cerrar all go through, and while a request is pending it refuses with a notice in the result panel. The dialogs still close themselves on the reply, and Cobrar also after its bounded 5 s wait, which hands the request to Recogida. A reply that never comes is bounded by the gateway timeout (blocker 9). e2e `test_dialogsCannotCloseWhileAeatAnswers`.
- [x] **Anular / Rectificar: the AEAT reply applies to what was sent; no rectificativa without its row** (pre-12.0 review blocker 4; app A1-A3): a second search while a cancellation or rectificativa waited for AEAT made the reply mark the ticket shown by then. That could mark an invoice AEAT never cancelled as ANULADA, or another ticket as RECTIFICADA. Both dialogs now lock the ticket field and Buscar while a request is pending and apply the reply to a snapshot taken at click time (`CancelInvoiceDialog::m_pendingTicket` / `m_pendingEvent`, `RectifyInvoiceDialog::m_submittedTicket`). Rectificar used to send to AEAT even when its placeholder row failed to insert, and then reported "enviada" with no local row. `insertPlaceholderRow` now returns false and nothing is sent, and a reply UPDATE that changes no row is a local failure. e2e `test_cancelInvoice_replyAppliesToTheTicketItWasSentFor`, `test_rectify_placeholderNotStoredSendsNothing`, `test_rectify_replyAppliesToTheTicketItWasSentFor`.
- [x] **The shop's Verifactu connection for 12.0 decided: the gateway on TEST** (pre-12.0 review blocker 2, Germán 19-10-2026): 12.0 ships with the shop on the IreneSolutions gateway in its TEST environment, so the shop can use every change and send the self-check report while bugs are fixed. The direct AEAT client stays for the later production switch, once AEAT answers the two written requests. The tracker's *Next* line, the production-switch row and the Verifactu README contradicted each other and now say this; the gateway fixes are the 12.0 blockers.
- [x] **Private data removed from the repository's current files** (pre-12.0 review blocker 1, Germán 19-10-2026): the issuer's NIF, her full name (a header comment) and the CSVs of the two INFORMA+ written requests are gone from every tracked file. A CSV lets anyone open the official document at AEAT's sede. The QR-size test now uses AEAT's test NIF; the request references stay. The past commits still hold them: the owner chose not to rewrite the history of the public repository (tags, Releases and clones would all change). The shop's public street address stays as the first-run default, and the tracker no longer claims it is never in the source. Marked fixed in `docs/reviews/2026-10-19-pre-12.0/`.
- [x] **Refused invoices: the red count leaves out pre-Verifactu tickets and opens the list** (Germán 19-10-2026, on a copy of the shop's DB):
  - **Why the count was misleading:** it showed 33 refused invoices. 27 were tickets paid in 2020 / 2022 that the start-up recovery sent in May 2026, before the recovery floor date existed. AEAT refuses any invoice dated in an earlier year, so they can never be resent and kept the warning up forever. The count also gave no ticket numbers to look for in Recogida.
  - **The floor date:** `refusedInvoiceCount` / the new `refusedInvoices` (`sql_lite`) now take the recovery floor date (`verifactu.pending_recovery_floor_date`, Configuración → *Solo tickets desde*, default 01-09-2026) and compare it with the **payment date**, the invoice date AEAT judges. The start-up pending check compares it with the reception date.
  - **The list:** the status-bar text is a link that opens **Facturas rechazadas** (`RefusedInvoicesDialog`): ticket (marked when it is a rectificativa), payment date, amount and AEAT's reason. *Abrir en Recogida* (or a double click) opens Recogida on that ticket with its refused row selected (`RecogPrendas::showTicket`). The list and the count refresh when that Recogida closes.
  - **The rows are untouched:** they stay `ERROR` and can still be resent from Recogida.
  - **Tests:** e2e `test_mainWindow_refusedInvoicesStayInView` (floor, link, list, Recogida on the ticket) and the floor in `test_refusedRectificativaRedoneUnderItsNumber`.
- [x] **Comprobar tras actualizar: the shop user's post-update self-check** (tracker item, design chosen by Germán 19-10-2026):
  - **Why:** the manual smoke test needed someone to copy the newest backup and the settings, edit JSON by hand and compare each result with a table.
  - **How it runs:** the installer now adds *Start → La Ideal - Comprobar tras actualizar*, which starts `laideal.exe --self-check`. `SelfCheckDialog` runs instead of MainWindow and works in a temporary folder:
    - It copies the newest `backups/laideal_*.db` and a settings copy (`SelfCheck::testSettings`: the database copy, the direct client in pre-production, no gateway key, backups off).
    - It runs M1 (`checkSchema`) and M2 (`checkData`).
    - It asks M2b: the last closed quarter's figures recomputed without closing anything, against the filed PDF, which it can open.
    - It asks M3 after printing the recibo and the factura of the newest invoice AEAT holds. The QR is drawn from the stored verification URL.
    - It asks M5: the installer pages.
    - It writes `comprobacion_laideal_<version>_<date>.txt` to the Desktop, with statuses and reasons but no shop data, and deletes the copies.
  - **M1 in detail:** the migrations run on the copy while warnings are captured; any "failed" statement fails the check. It also checks `integrity_check`, the column count, that `accounting_closures` exists, and that the next number is the highest + 1.
  - **Safety:** the live DB and `~/.laideal_settings.json` are only read. The Verifactu client is never started, so nothing can reach AEAT or the gateway.
  - **Compliance review:**
    - The factura is reprinted only if every paid row of the invoice is `ENVIADA` in the copy and the live DB, opened read-only, still holds it as sent. The backup can be a day old.
    - The QR is drawn at the size the direct client uses.
    - The question says the papers are test prints.
    - The certificate password is stripped from the settings copy.
    - Copies left behind by a crashed run are deleted at the next start.
  - **Delivery:** chosen over a PyInstaller script driving the UI (large, fragile at the shop, antivirus flags) and a PowerShell script. It needs nothing installed and is testable in ctest.
  - **Docs:** `smoke_test.md` is now part 1 (M4 look and M6 AEAT pre-production, before the release) and part 2 (the self-check). `/release` step 1, README, CONTRIBUTING and the testing overview follow.
  - **Key files:** `src/app/selfcheck.{h,cpp}`, `src/app/selfcheckdialog.{h,cpp}`, `src/app/main.cpp`, `releases/laideal.iss`.
  - **Tests:** new `test_selfcheck`, including a whole run that leaves the live files byte for byte unchanged.
  - **For the 12.0 release notes:** "New: 'La Ideal - Comprobar tras actualizar' in the Start menu checks the new version with your data and printer, on a copy, and saves a report to send." / "Nuevo: 'La Ideal - Comprobar tras actualizar' en el menú Inicio comprueba la nueva versión con sus datos y su impresora, sobre una copia, y guarda un informe para enviarlo."
- [x] **Decimals shown with the comma in every window and on the printed tickets, whatever the language** (Germán 18-10-2026):
  - **Before:** three formats were in use. Reports, Listado and some messages were always Spanish. The Recogida importe column followed the Windows locale. The ticket table, the Recogida / Añadir prendas fields, the Cobrar, Anular, Envíos pendientes and Rectificar windows and the printed recibo and factura used the stored dot. `app.language` changes none of it.
  - **The helpers:** every display now goes through `displayMoney()` / `displayDecimal()` (`sql_lite`, next to the storage format `moneyText`). They give two decimals with the comma and no thousands separator, so "1.234" is never ambiguous in a field. The reports keep `formatEuro` ("1.234,56 €").
  - **Tables:** `NumberFormatDelegate` no longer reads the view's locale, and an empty cell stays empty. The new `DecimalCommaDelegate` shows the size columns of Recogida and Listado de ingresos.
  - **Printed ticket:** `TicketRenderer` prints the comma and computes base / IVA from numbers, not from the printed text.
  - **Rectificar factura amount:** a `UiKit::AmountSpinBox`, which shows the comma and takes "," or "." as the decimal key, so the numeric keypad works either way.
  - **Reading values back:**
    - PayDialog keeps each amount as data instead of parsing its text.
    - The size writer stores a comma-typed size with the dot.
    - Recogida no longer rewrites a typed comma in the size field to a dot.
  - **Unchanged:** the database, the maths and the AEAT XML keep the dot.
  - **Coding guidelines:** new rule in `/coding-guidelines`.
  - **Tests:** `test_sql_lite` `test_displayMoney_andDisplayDecimal`, `test_uikit` `test_amountSpinBox_commaShownEitherKeyTyped`, `test_textcolordelegate` `test_decimalDelegates_showTheComma`. The e2e, escpos and ticket-preview expectations now read "7,00" / "100,00" / "42,20".
  - **For the 12.0 release notes:** "Amounts are shown with a decimal comma everywhere, also on printed receipts and invoices." / "Los importes se muestran con coma decimal en todas las ventanas y también en los recibos y facturas impresos."
- [x] **Main window menu bar reorganised by kind of work** (tracker item, layout chosen by Germán 18-10-2026):
  - **Why:** *Herramientas* mixed daily work, invoice corrections, accounting and maintenance. *Archivo* was built in two places, with Configuración and the log inserted by position (`actions().at(1)`), which was fragile.
  - **The new layout:**
    - **Archivo:** Configuración…, Hacer copia de seguridad ahora…, Cerrar.
    - **Tickets:** Recogida, Añadir nuevas prendas, Imprimir ▸.
    - **Facturas:** Anular, Rectificar, Exportar registros AEAT.
    - **Contabilidad:** Añadir factura de gastos, Generar, Revertir contabilidad….
    - **Ver:** unchanged.
    - **Ayuda:** updates, release notes, Log de depuración…, Acerca de Verifactu.
  - **Built in one place:** every menu is built in `buildUi()`. `actionConfiguracion` is created there and connected once the database is migrated.
  - **Shortcuts:** all kept, except Revertir contabilidad, which lost Alt+Shift+R and gained "…", so a closed quarter is never reopened by one key press. Anular and Rectificar got Alt+A / Alt+T. The three quick buttons stay.
  - **"..." used one way everywhere** (Germán's follow-up): it marks an entry that opens a window to fill in or choose from before anything happens.
    - Gained it: Recogida, Añadir nuevas prendas, the three Imprimir entries, Añadir factura de gastos, Generar contabilidad.
    - Lost it: Hacer copia de seguridad ahora, Buscar actualizaciones, Notas de la versión, Log de depuración and Acerca de Verifactu, which act at once or only show something.
  - **Messages naming a menu path follow it:** "Ayuda → Log de depuración", and "Contabilidad → Añadir factura de gastos" in Listado. Three messages said "Ayuda → Mostrar log", an entry that never existed.
  - **Tests:** the e2e checks the whole layout and the shortcuts. The `tests/ui` scripts open the new menus.
  - **For the 12.0 release notes:** "The menu bar is organised by task: Tickets, Facturas and Contabilidad replace Herramientas; the backup is in Archivo and the log in Ayuda. 'Revertir contabilidad' no longer has a shortcut; 'Anular factura' and 'Rectificar factura' have Alt+A and Alt+T." / "La barra de menús se organiza por tareas: Tickets, Facturas y Contabilidad sustituyen a Herramientas; la copia de seguridad está en Archivo y el log en Ayuda. 'Revertir contabilidad' ya no tiene atajo de teclado; 'Anular factura' y 'Rectificar factura' tienen Alt+A y Alt+T."
- [x] **Dead-code sweep; the legacy Archivo utilities removed** (tracker item, Germán 18-10-2026):
  - **Limpiar base de datos** (Alt+L) is gone. It replaced the decimal comma in `gastos.importe`, `ingresos.importe` / `size` and the `prendas` prices, and `MainWindow::repopulatePrendas` also ran it silently after every Listado edit. Its `ingresos` branch rewrote `importe` on any row, sent invoices included, which is an edit path on invoice records.
  - **What replaces it:**
    - `NumberFormatDelegate::setModelData` stores a Listado edit with a dot (`storedNumber`).
    - `migrateDatabase` replaces a leftover comma at start in `gastos` and `prendas`. It skips a gasto of a closed quarter, whose report was filed without it, and a value such as "1.234,50", and never touches `ingresos`.
    - Its two messages now say where to correct an amount: Contabilidad's comma-decimal warning and `readGarmentPrice`'s.
  - **Crear hash en ingresos** is gone too. It filled empty hashes and gave a new hash to rows sharing one across the whole table, invoices included. `genHash16` has used a UUID for years and every INSERT sets a hash. A read-only check of the shop DB found no empty hash and no comma decimal left. It did find 33 groups (67 rows) of one ticket sharing a hash, all paid tickets from 2021-2023 never sent to AEAT. Every write keyed by `(n_recibo, hash)` hit both rows of such a group.
  - **What replaces it:** a `migrateDatabase` step that runs before the start-up rounding, which also writes by that key:
    - Every row of such a group but one gets a new hash; the row kept is one with a CSV when the group has one.
    - An empty hash is filled the same way.
    - A further row AEAT holds (a CSV) is only logged.
    - Each change is logged with the old and new value.
    - The same hash on two different tickets is no conflict and is left alone.
    - The compliance review asked for the order, the empty hashes, the log and the keeper.
  - **`dead-code-finder` sweep:** one dead method, `VerifactuInvoice::getTotalTaxAmount()` (its field still feeds the JSON), removed. Seven methods used only by tests are deliberate seams and were kept: `AeatQr::moduleCount`, `AeatCertificate::isLoaded`, `AeatDirectBackend::chainReady`, `AeatSelfTest::invoiceNumber`, `AddGarment::loadTicket`, `Imprimir::ticketBytes`, `ReportHtml::lastReportHtml`.
  - **Key files:** `src/app/mainwindow.{h,cpp}`, `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.{h,cpp}`, `src/tableview/numberformatdelegate.{h,cpp}`.
  - **Tests:** `test_sql_lite` `test_migrateDatabase_givesDuplicateAndEmptyHashesANewOne` / `..._replacesTheDecimalCommaOutsideInvoices`, `test_textcolordelegate` `test_numberFormatDelegate_storesADot`.
- [x] **Cobrar warns on screen when its quarter was closed during the AEAT round trip** (tracker item, from the compliance review of the `accounting_closures` change): Cobrar checks the quarter just before calling AEAT, and since that change a payment AEAT may already hold is stored even if Contabilidad closes the quarter while AEAT answers - but only a log line said so, and the filed report then missed that income. `PayDialog::paidIntoClosedQuarter()` now flags it and Recogida shows "Ticket N cobrado" with a warning naming the quarter, saying its report misses the payment and to revert and generate it again (result panel, no pop-up). Also dropped "bloqueado" from the unstored-garments message: since the change only a garment paid meanwhile can be left unstored. e2e `test_recogida_paymentIntoAQuarterClosedDuringTheSubmitIsShown`.
- [x] **Accounting lock moved from the per-row `edit_lock` flag to an `accounting_closures` table** (Germán 18-10-2026, after the cancellation-only quarter fix):
  - **Why:** the lock belongs to a period but lived on every row. A quarter's state was worked out from the rows dated in it, so a quarter without rows could not be closed; that is what forced the special case for a quarter holding only a cancellation. Closing also ran an `UPDATE` over every invoice row of the quarter. Every writer had to set the flag by hand. The TEXT `'0'` / `'1'` comparison had already caused the 11.0 bug.
  - **The table** (`migrateDatabase` → `migrateAccountingClosures`, `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.cpp`):
    - Columns: `year`, `quarter`, `closed_at`, `reopened_at`, `origin`, `report_file`.
    - A partial unique index allows one open closure per quarter.
    - It is append-only: triggers refuse a `DELETE` and every `UPDATE` other than setting `reopened_at` once. Revertir keeps the closure, so the table is the close / reopen history.
    - The first time, one transaction carries over every quarter any row's flag had closed (`origin = 'migration'`). After that `edit_lock` is never read or written, so stale flags cannot close a reopened quarter again. On failure the migration rolls back, logs a critical line and retries at the next start.
  - **Row lock:** a row is locked when its date is in a closed quarter: `fecha_pago` for ingresos, `fecha` for gastos. This is `kUnlockedWhere` / `closedQuarterSql` in SQL, and `quarterIsClosed` in Recogida (`rowClickedLocked`). Listado and GenListado gastos use `closedQuarterKeys`. Listado re-reads the closed quarters before a delete, a double click or an edit (`rowLockedNow`, with the validator now given the row's index), so a quarter closed while the listing is open is honoured.
  - **Contabilidad:** the flow and Comprobar bloqueo now read the closure:
    - "bloqueado (contabilidad realizada el …)" or "no está bloqueado (contabilidad revertida el …)".
    - The report's file name is stored with the close.
    - A cancellation-only quarter is closed like any other, so `quarterHasOnlyRegularizations`, `combinedLockState`, `readLockForQuarter` and `updateLockForMonth` are gone.
    - An empty quarter is still refused (`quarterHasMovements`).
  - **PayDialog:**
    - The `edit_lock` skip and the race guard went: the quarter check just before the AEAT call reads the live table.
    - A payment AEAT may already have registered is now stored even if the quarter was closed during the round trip, and logged as critical for review. Before, it was left unstored.
  - **Schema:** the `edit_lock` column stays in the schema (hidden), so the column indices do not move.
    - The shop's `ingresos.edit_lock` turned out to be `NOT NULL` without a default; the compliance review flagged this and `PRAGMA table_info` on the shop DB confirmed it. Every `ingresos` INSERT therefore still writes `'0'`.
    - `TestSchema` and the UI seed now declare the column the same way, so a missing value fails in the tests instead of at the shop. The test schema's old `DEFAULT '0'` had hidden this.
  - **Fails closed:** `quarterIsClosed` treats an unreadable `accounting_closures` as closed, like the SQL guards. Before, a failed lock query let writes through.
  - **Tests:** `test_sql_lite`, `test_contabilidad`, `test_e2e_app` and `test_e2e_verifactu` were ported to `closeQuarter`, and new cases cover the migration, the history, the triggers and the row lock. `TestSchema::resetClosures` drops and recreates the table between cases, because the triggers refuse `DELETE`.
- [x] **README §Build covers every build folder** (Germán 18-10-2026): a table of the four CMake trees (`build/` Release + tests, `build-debug/` VSCode, `build-uitest/` the UI-test app and tools, `build-release/` the installer via `release.ps1`), the toolchain `PATH` line once, then Release build, Debug in VSCode, and a new **UI-test build** subsection (configure with `-DLAIDEAL_UI_TEST=ON`, build the three targets, create the `.venv`). §Tests and `docs/testing/ui_smoke.md` (Running it locally) now point there instead of repeating the commands.
- [x] **Contabilidad: a quarter whose only movement is a cancellation gets its report** (tracker item, found by the UI smoke test): a quarter's lock state comes from the payments and gastos dated in it, so a quarter holding only the cancellation of an earlier quarter's invoice read as "no data" and Generar contabilidad wrote nothing - its "Anulaciones / rectificaciones del periodo" (the -12.10 of smoke step D2) was never reported. New `Contabilidad::quarterHasOnlyRegularizations` (no payments nor gastos, and `regularizationsBetweenDates` non-empty for the quarter, the same rows the report subtracts) makes `onGenerateClicked` write the report and say there is nothing of the quarter's own to lock (the cancelled row is locked with its payment quarter; Revertir on it still answers that there are no records to revert), and Comprobar bloqueo says the same. Rectifications needed nothing: the rectifying ticket is a payment of the quarter. Covered by `test_e2e_app::test_contabilidad_quarterWithOnlyACancellation` (fails without the fix with the old "No hay registros" warning); UI smoke step D2 lost its precondition on block B and runs alone too.
- [x] **CONTRIBUTING.md** (Germán 17-10-2026): the root README's Development workflow and Release procedure moved, unchanged, into a new root `CONTRIBUTING.md` (the file GitHub links from pull requests), headed by the rules for every change: branch, test (README §Tests), the Verifactu compliance review, docs, commit style; the release-ready list now also asks for green `ctest` + UI smoke and the manual smoke test. The README (271 → 154 lines) points to it; `/release`, `/update-docs`, `ai_agent_instructions.md` and `INDEX.md` follow. No CODE_OF_CONDUCT / CONTRIBUTORS / templates: one maintainer, no outside contributors.
- [x] **Manual smoke test cut down to what needs a person; test commands in the README** (Germán 17-10-2026): with blocks A-E automated, `docs/testing/smoke_test.md` is now a 15-minute checklist (M1-M6: the shop's real schema and data, the physical printer, how screens / PDFs / installer pages look, the AEAT pre-production script when the direct client changed), run after `ui-smoke.yml` on a backup copy with its own `--settings` and sending no invoice; the seeded tickets are described in `ui_smoke.md`. The root README §Tests is a table of the four suites (ctest, UI smoke in CI and locally, the AEAT pre-production run with or without `--dry-run`, the manual pass) with the command, options and the doc holding the steps. The pre-production settings file now lives outside the repo at `%USERPROFILE%/work/tintoreria/documents/prueba-aeat-settings.json`.
- [x] **Automated UI smoke test, phases 1-3** (tracker item, Germán 17-10-2026; decisions: a test-only build flag for the fake AEAT, the pre-production run local): smoke test blocks A-E now run by themselves on the installed app. **Fake AEAT in the installed app:** CMake option `LAIDEAL_UI_TEST` (OFF by default) compiles a `--fake-aeat http://127.0.0.1:<port>` switch into `main.cpp` (anything else refused; also no PDF viewer, and a title marker); `tests/tools/fake_aeat_server` runs the e2e suites' `FakeAeatServer` as a process. Because such a build could mark invoices sent without AEAT, the release job refuses an exe holding the UTF-16 `--fake-aeat` marker, `ui-smoke.yml` asserts its own build holds it (the guard cannot go blind), `release.ps1` configures it OFF, and no UI-test exe is uploaded; the compliance auditor reviewed it. **The blocks** (`tests/ui/block_a.py`…`block_e.py` on shared operator flows in `actions.py`, run by `smoke.py`): A start-up, migrations, the recovery dialog and the log's AEAT URLs; B the recovery dialog's buttons, Recogida (search, Añadir prendas, Cobrar accepted by the fake AEAT, Anular prendas), the refusals, columns, listados, Anular factura / Rectificar; C printing; D Contabilidad Q3 / Q4 / Anual from the PDFs' text, the lock box rules, Bloquear Q3 then the refused edit and expense, Revertir; E backup, Configuración, the live English / Spanish switch, the installer's language. Each step leaves a screenshot, a failed one every window's control tree; the report goes to the job summary. **Printing (P2):** a *Generic / Text Only* queue on a RAW TCP port to a local listener that keeps one file per job (a FILE: port overwrites its file and would lose the second copy), decoded by `tests/tools/escpos_preview` with the ESC/POS reader moved from `test_ticket_preview` into `tests/support/escpospreview.h`; the QR is read with OpenCV. **P3:** `tests/ui/aeat_preproduction.py` runs the `laideal-prueba-aeat` kit's flows against AEAT pre-production with the issuer's certificate (paid ticket, two payments, cancellation, R5 rectifications by differences and substitution, export), on a synthetic DB, with a safety stop unless the app is in PRUEBAS and every AEAT URL is prewww1; local only (the certificate cannot be a CI secret of a public repo), with a `--dry-run` against the fake AEAT that CI runs every time. Ticket numbers are 8 digits (`9DDDhhmm`) because Recogida reads a 9-digit search as a phone number. **What UIA showed about Qt** (recorded in `docs/testing/ui_smoke.md`): dialogs are nested windows, so lookups are scoped; cell widgets and checkable cells are invisible / without a toggle pattern, so they are clicked in place and checked through the DB; hidden columns keep zero-width headers; dates type only section by section. **Found:** a quarter whose only movement is the cancellation of an earlier quarter's invoice gets no Contabilidad report (filed in the tracker; D2 relies on block B's payment meanwhile). Whole run about 3-4 minutes locally (26 steps). `/release` step 1 now runs `ui-smoke.yml` before the manual pass, which keeps only the real printer, the shop schema and the look of screens and PDFs.
- [x] **Automated UI smoke test, phase 0 (spike)** (tracker item, Germán 17-10-2026): the installed app can be driven like a person over Windows UI Automation. Qt 6.4 publishes each widget's `objectName` as its plain AutomationId, so pywinauto finds `pb_save`, `lblResult`, `cb_client`, `le_nr_ticket` by the names the e2e bench already uses, and reads `lblResult`'s rich text as plain text; the only gap is `table_ticket`'s cell-widget combos (garment, service), which UIA does not expose, so the script clicks their cell (`DataItem`) and types. `tests/ui/seed.py` builds a synthetic DB (the `testschema.h` tables; the app migrates it) and an offline settings file passed with the existing `--settings` switch (no Verifactu credentials, no printing, update check, backup or recovery dialog), so no shop data and no AEAT call; `tests/ui/laideal_ui.py` holds the launch/find/read/screenshot helpers and `tests/ui/smoke_p0.py` saves one unpaid ticket by mouse and keyboard and checks the result panel, the stored row (`SIN COBRAR`, 3.50) and the form reset. New `.github/workflows/ui-smoke.yml` (`workflow_dispatch` + pushes touching `tests/ui/`) builds, packages with `windeployqt` + Inno Setup as the release job does, installs with `/VERYSILENT /LANG=spanish`, runs the script with the build's Qt off `PATH` (so the deployed DLLs are what loads) and keeps screenshots, control tree, logs and DB as the `ui-smoke` artifact. Passed locally against the Release build; not part of `ctest` (it needs a desktop session and takes the mouse). Docs: `docs/testing/ui_smoke.md`.
- [x] **A first sync reads every month** (tracker "remaining limits" 1, Germán 17-10-2026): AEAT's query searches by the invoice's month, and the sync stopped going back at the first months with records. The chain's newest record can carry an older date (Reintentar of an old invoice, a back-dated rectificativa). After a restore of a backup older than that record, the next record therefore chained to an older one, and the chain at AEAT forked. A first sync now reads all 24 months and takes the newest record by generation time. It runs on the first start, a restored database or a new PC, which the chain marker detects. A daily start, whose marker matches, still reads only this month and the previous one, so it is not slowed down. The other half of the restore question, cancellations (AEAT's query returns registrations only), stays with the AEAT questions. The fake AEAT server can now answer by month (`FakeAeatServer::setQueryByMonth`). From the compliance review of the change: on a first sync, a record of ours that AEAT returns without its huella now fails the sync instead of being skipped, because over 24 months it could be the chain's newest. A first sync that fails partway through adopts nothing and starts over. Tests `test_firstSyncReadsEveryMonth` (24 queries, the right chain end, then 2 queries on the next start), `test_firstSyncFailsOnARecordWithoutItsHuella`, `test_firstSyncFailingMidWalkAdoptsNothing` (`FakeAeatServer::queryWithoutHashFor`), mutation-checked.
- [x] **Numbering only from the real AEAT; gateway numbers count; no self-test beside the shop in pre-production** (tracker "remaining limits" 2-5, Germán 16-10-2026): AEAT pre-production holds test numbers far above the shop's for the issuer's NIF (90000001-90000004 from the 12-10 run, plus many gateway test tickets). The next ticket number therefore now uses AEAT's highest only when the direct client works in production (`VerifactuIntegration::highestNumberAtAeat`). In pre-production it always follows the database, and no restore warning shows. In production, the issuer's highest number counts from any system, the gateway's included. The chain still continues only our own records. This way a restore across the 12.0 switch never reissues a number the gateway registered after the backup. The self-test button is disabled while the shop's own direct client is in pre-production (the tickets are the test, and both would send the same outbox). Switching back to the gateway with direct records still pending was already refused (`test_leavingDirectRefusedWithPendingRecords`). The gateway-only case of a rectificativa accepted after its dialog closed is accepted as a gateway limit, since 12.0 replaces the gateway and Recogida's "Consultar en AEAT" shows the real state. Tests `test_mainWindow_preProductionNumbersFollowTheDatabase`, `test_selfTestOffWhileTheShopIsInPreProduction`, `test_highestNumberAtAeat` (the gateway's 9000 counts). The restore-numbering tests now run in production against the fake server. Mutation-checked.
- [x] **No invoice without Verifactu** (tracker decision, Germán 16-10-2026; compliance auditor note 13 of the 12.0 review): up to 11.2, a paid ticket was saved and printed with no Verifactu record when the issuer's NIF, name or service key was missing. That was documented as intended, and the auditor only ever reviewed diffs, so it was never flagged. With 12.0's own declaración responsable (e: "solo VERI*FACTU: Sí") it would contradict what the producer signs. The gate now has two levels. `VerifactuIntegration::numberingBlockedReason` (first chain check running or failed) refuses every save. `invoicingBlockedReason` (that, or `!isConfigured()`, with what is missing) refuses a paid ticket save, Cobrar, Rectificar and a paid garment in Añadir prendas, while unpaid tickets and garments are still saved. Añadir prendas is now checked when a paid garment is saved (`AddGarment::invoicingBlocked`), not when the window opens. That also closes the tracked limit of a window left open across a Configuración restart. The manual smoke test now runs on the direct client in AEAT pre-production instead of with the key removed (`docs/testing/smoke_test.md`, `/release`). New test `test_noInvoiceWhileVerifactuIsNotConfigured`; `test_gateOnlyWhileTheChainIsUnchecked` and `test_mainWindow_noSaveWhileTheFirstCheckRuns` now save through the real Añadir window. Mutation-checked.
- [x] **Declaración responsable: address and place** (tracker item, Germán's decision 16-10-2026): item j) reads the shop's address from the settings (`business.address`, `business.city`, then España), so no address is written into the public repository, and item l) is "Granada, España". The date stays pending until the version is signed at `/release 12.0`, which is now on the release checklist. Test `test_currentSignatureFromTheSettings`.
- [x] **One save at a time; no number refresh mid-save from the start-up sync** (compliance auditor 1-3, seventh round of the 12.0 review, verdict PASS): a second click on Guardar during the save's wait for AEAT re-entered the save and stored a second ticket under the same number. Guardar is now disabled and re-entry refused while a save runs. The start-up sync's restore warning no longer refreshes the number in the middle of a save either: the form reset does it. A rectificativa whose request could not even be made is marked ERROR to redo at once, instead of staying PENDIENTE until the next start. `test_mainWindow_syncDuringASaveKeepsItsNumber` extended, mutation-checked.
- [x] **The number holds during a save; Recogida's Añadir gated; a refused resend that cannot be prepared stops** (compliance auditor 1-3, test engineer 1-2, sixth round of the 12.0 review): since every finished chain sync refreshes the number on screen, a sync ending while a paid save waited for AEAT moved it to the next number. The factura and recibo were then printed under that next number. The refresh now waits for the save, which resets the form itself (`MainWindow::m_saving`). Recogida's Añadir, which opens the same window as Herramientas → Añadir nuevas prendas, is gated like Cobrar. A redo of a refused rectificativa now stops when its row cannot be read or updated (`reusePlaceholderRow` returns its result) instead of sending figures the books do not hold. Tests `test_mainWindow_syncDuringASaveKeepsItsNumber`, `test_gateOnlyWhileTheChainIsUnchecked` (also pins that an ordinary start never blocks saving), mutation-checked. Note 4 (an open Añadir window across a Configuración restart) is filed with the direct client's remaining limits.
- [x] **Numbering safe during the first chain check too; fifth review round** (compliance auditor 1-4, test engineer 1-5, guidelines auditor 1-10, dead-code finder, fifth round of the 12.0 review): the gate only refused tickets once the first chain check had failed. While that check was still running (up to two years of months asked one by one), a ticket could be saved under a number AEAT might hold. Now the gate also refuses while it runs (`AeatDirectBackend::firstSyncRunning`). Every finished sync, not only the gate's retry, now refreshes the number on screen (`AeatDirectBackend::chainSynced` -> `VerifactuIntegration::chainChecked`). Añadir nuevas prendas (a paid garment is invoiced) is gated too. A failed `markRectificationResent` stops the closed-quarter resend. The rectificativa helpers log failed updates. The start-up sweep moved to `MainWindow::settlePendingRectifications`, and the refused-resend branch to `RectifyInvoiceDialog::prepareRefusedResend`. Comment drift in `sql_lite.h` and `verifactuintegration.h` was fixed, the schema namespaces in `aeatrecord.h` are file-local now, and the module and Contabilidad docs were brought up to date. New tests `test_mainWindow_noSaveWhileTheFirstCheckRuns`, `test_mainWindow_numberRefreshedAfterAnyChainSync`, `test_noRedoOverAPendingRectificativa`, `test_storedOutcomeOnlyOnceAnswered`. Extended: invalid date in `openPeriodDate`, an accepted rectificativa never resent, a failed retry never reported as checked. All mutation-checked except the cosmetic "cadena comprobada" message.
- [x] **Rectificativa redo and start-up sweep** (compliance auditor 2, 4-7, fourth round of the 12.0 review): a refused rectificativa from a quarter closed since was resent with its date and amount pinned but in whatever S/I mode the radio showed. AEAT could then hold a rectificativa by differences while the books keep an S row, and whether the original was subtracted depended on which path settled it. Now it is resent only in its filed mode. Its row goes PENDIENTE while it is sent (`markRectificationResent`, nothing else changes), so the original cannot be cancelled or rectified again meanwhile. A redo is refused while the direct client still holds that rectificativa's record. At start, a PENDIENTE rectificativa whose record AEAT already answered is settled from the stored answer (`AeatDirectBackend::storedOutcome`) instead of being marked ERROR for a redo that would rewrite its figures. The original's subtraction date always falls in an open period (`openPeriodDate`: the date asked for, else today, else the next open quarter), including when today's quarter is closed. Tests `test_refusedRectificativaInAClosedQuarter` (extended), `test_startSettlesARectificativaFromItsStoredAnswer`, `test_openPeriodDateAndResentRectificativa`, mutation-checked. Note 10 (switching to the gateway with a pending direct rectificativa) is added to the tracked remaining limits.
- [x] **No ticket number until the chain is checked; number refreshed after the retry** (compliance auditor 1 and 3, test engineer 1-5, fourth round of the 12.0 review): while the direct client's first chain check cannot reach AEAT, unpaid tickets were still saved, and the retried check did not move the number on screen. A restored database could then save a ticket under a number AEAT already holds, and send it later at Cobrar. Now no ticket is saved, paid or not, and no rectificativa is issued until the check succeeds (`VerifactuIntegration::invoicingBlockedReason`). The retried check reports `chainChecked`, and the main window then takes the number after AEAT's highest. The self-test's chain rule is now one function (`VerifactuIntegration::selfTestSharesShopChain`).
