New tests `test_queuedRectificativaIsLeftToTheDirectClient` (a rectificativa the direct client holds stays PENDIENTE through its dialog closing and the start-up sweep), `test_payDialog_noInvoicingUntilTheChainIsChecked` (Cobrar and Rectificar refused), `test_selfTestSharesTheShopChainOnlyInPreProduction`. Recovery and the refreshed number were added to `test_mainWindow_noInvoicingUntilTheChainIsChecked`, and the hidden notice in production and with the gateway to `test_mainWindow_showsThePreProductionEnvironment`. All mutation-checked.
- [x] **The self-test and the shop's environment; pre-production in view** (compliance auditor 8 and note 12, test engineer 4, third round of the 12.0 review): the self-test always works in pre-production. While the shop's direct client is also in pre-production, the self-test now keeps the shop's chain marker (otherwise the next start would need AEAT) and settles the shop's records it sends. While the shop is in production or on the gateway, it touches neither: before, a pre-production outcome could mark a real production ticket ENVIADA. The main window's status bar now says when the direct client registers in AEAT's test environment (`lblAeatEnvironment`). Tests `test_selfTestKeepsTheChainMarker`, `test_mainWindow_showsThePreProductionEnvironment`.
- [x] **No invoicing until the chain is checked** (compliance auditor 6, third round of the 12.0 review): when the direct client's first check cannot reach AEAT (first start of 12.0, restored database, new PC), no record can be generated, and the local numbers may already be at AEAT. A paid ticket or a Cobrar then was saved anyway and later resent under a number AEAT holds for another sale. Now both are refused with the reason (`VerifactuIntegration::invoicingBlockedReason`, `AeatDirectBackend::chainSyncFailed`), each attempt starts a new check, and unpaid tickets are still saved. Test `test_mainWindow_noInvoicingUntilTheChainIsChecked` (mutation-checked).
- [x] **Record states the client relies on** (compliance auditor blocking 2, findings 3-5, 7 and 9, test engineer 1-2, third round of the 12.0 review):
  - "pending" means pending at AEAT (`holdsPendingRecord`): a refused record no longer makes a redone rectificativa or a retried cancellation look queued;
  - a send is marked "may reach AEAT" before it leaves and cleared only if the request never went out (`aeat_records.reached` 0/2/1). A send cut off by closing the app or by a new backend after Configuración counts as reached, so its duplicate answer is ours;
  - a rectificativa whose dialog closes without a pending record (gateway, or the direct client before its record exists) is an ERROR to redo;
  - at start, any rectificativa left PENDIENTE with nothing to send it is marked ERROR (`pendingRectificationNumbers` / `markRectificationUnsent`), so it never blocks its ticket for good;
  - a ticket whose cancellation is pending is not rectified;
  - a refused rectificativa counted in a quarter closed since is resent only with its own amount and date, with its row untouched, and the original is marked RECTIFICADA in an open period.
  Tests `test_holdsPendingRecordOnlyWhilePending`, `test_duplicateAfterAnInterruptedSendIsOurs`, `test_mainWindow_orphanedPendingRectificativaBecomesAnError`, `test_refusedRectificativaInAClosedQuarter`, and the rectify refusal in `test_cancelDirectQueuedIsPending`; each guard mutation-checked.
- [x] **Request ids unique across backends** (compliance auditor blocking 1, third round of the 12.0 review): every backend numbered its requests from `aeat-1` / `req-1`. After Configuración re-initialised the backend, a new request could take the id a window was still waiting on, and one ticket was settled with another invoice's answer (ENVIADA, never correctable). The counters are now shared by every backend of the run. Test in `test_mainWindow_reinitialisesOneBackend` (mutation-checked).
- [x] **Late outcomes nobody else would settle** (compliance auditor notes 10-12, second round of the 12.0 review):
  - with the gateway, a rectificativa whose dialog closes before the answer stayed PENDIENTE where nothing resends a rectificativa. It now becomes an ERROR to redo under its number (`~RectifyInvoiceDialog`);
  - the self-test's backend sends every pending record of the issuer and now reports those outcomes (`AeatSelfTest::recordSettled`), which the main window settles like any other (`MainWindow::onRecordSettled`);
  - payment sequence numbers skip only the direct client's records of its own environment, so pre-production records under real numbers leave no gaps in production (`nextVerifactuInvoiceSeq(..., aeatEnvironment)`).
  Tests `test_rectifyGatewayClosedBeforeTheAnswer`, `test_selfTestReportsOtherRecordsItSettles`, `test_nextVerifactuInvoiceSeq`; each mutation-checked.
- [x] **Tests from the second coverage review**: the main window's next ticket number starts above our system's higher numbers at AEAT after a restore, and says so (`test_mainWindow_numbersAboveAeatAfterARestore`). Our highest number at AEAT reads "7-1" as 7 and ignores the self-test's and other systems' numbers (`test_highestNumberAtAeat`). The R5 names the corrected invoice as its stored record has it (`test_rectifyNamesTheStoredRecordsInvoice`). The sql_lite helpers read the values the store really writes (`test_storeValuesSeenBySqlLite`). Each mutation-checked.
- [x] **A restored database needs AEAT before its first record** (compliance auditor should-fix 4, second round of the 12.0 review): a database restored from an older backup carries its own "synced" mark. If AEAT was out of reach at that start (no certificate on a new PC, no network), the sync fell back to its stale local chain. That forked the chain and re-issued numbers AEAT already holds. The direct client now keeps the chain's last record outside the database, in the settings (`verifactu.aeat_chain_marker`, written after every record and sync). A database whose chain differs, or no marker at all (new PC, first start of 12.0), must reach AEAT before any record, as on a first start. Test `test_chainMarkerOutsideTheDatabase` (mutation-checked); the e2e helper `markChainReady` sets both marks.
- [x] **Leaving production is guarded** (compliance auditor should-fix 7, second round of the 12.0 review): unticking *Enviar a la AEAT real* needed no confirmation. Production records still unsent would stay behind (the outbox is per environment), and the startup recovery would resend their rows to pre-production. It is now refused while production records are pending, and confirmed otherwise (default No). Test in `test_directProductionNeedsConfirmation` (mutation-checked).
- [x] **One Verifactu backend after Configuración** (compliance auditor must-fix 3, second round of the 12.0 review): Aceptar in Configuración created a new `VerifactuIntegration` without removing the old one. With the direct client, two backends then sent the same outbox, each with its own wait time, so AEAT's `TiempoEsperaEnvio` was no longer honoured, and a duplicate could be misread as another system's. Windows opened earlier also kept using the old one. The main window now keeps one integration (its handlers connected once, `connectVerifactu`) and re-initialises it, and `VerifactuIntegration::initialize` removes the previous backend first. Test `test_mainWindow_reinitialisesOneBackend` (mutation-checked).
- [x] **Pending rectificativas and cancellations, end to end** (compliance auditor must-fix 1 and 2, should-fix 6 and note 9, second round of the 12.0 review):
  - a rectificativa is "pending, it sends itself" only when the direct client really holds its record (`AeatDirectBackend::holdsRecord`). Before the first chain sync none is generated, so the row is an ERROR to redo under its number, never a PENDIENTE row nothing would send;
  - while a rectificativa of a ticket is PENDIENTE (`pendingRectificationOf`), Rectificar and Anular factura refuse the ticket, so there is no second R5 and no cancellation the late R5 would overwrite;
  - `markTicketRectified` never touches an invoice already ANULADA or RECTIFICADA;
  - a late acceptance whose date fell in a quarter closed meanwhile marks the original today;
  - a cancellation the direct client stored but could not send yet says it is pending, not an error.
  Tests `test_pendingRectificativaGuards`, `test_pendingRectificativaBlocksTheTicket`, `test_rectifyDirectWithoutRecordIsAnError`, `test_cancelDirectQueuedIsPending`; each guard mutation-checked.
- [x] **A refused rectificativa is redone under its own number** (test engineer and compliance auditor 5, second round of the 12.0 review): a refused R5 stayed a paid ERROR row, counted as income and as "refused", with no way to clear it. Recogida rightly refuses to resend it as a plain invoice, and Rectificar factura made a second R5 beside it. Following AEAT's answer (resend corrected under the same number), Rectificar factura on an original whose rectificativa was refused reuses that number and row (`refusedRectificationOf`, `RectifyInvoiceDialog::reusePlaceholderRow`). The direct client sends it with Subsanacion / RechazoPrevio, and once accepted it is ENVIADA and leaves the refused count. Test `test_refusedRectificativaRedoneUnderItsNumber`, which also checks Recogida's refusal (mutation-checked).
- [x] **Test: the chain sync follows AEAT's query pages** (test engineer gap 12 of the 12.0 review): the fake AEAT answered every query in one page, so following `ClavePaginacion` was never exercised. `FakeAeatServer::setQueryPageSize` now pages its replies, and `test_syncFollowsQueryPages` checks the chain continues after a record on the last page (fails when the pages are ignored).
- [x] **Tests: late outcomes settled by the main window and by Recogida** (test engineer gaps 4 and 9 of the 12.0 review): with a live fake AEAT in the e2e suite (`LiveAeat`, `FakeAeatServer` added to `test_e2e_app`). A record an earlier session left unanswered goes out when the main window starts and its rows become ENVIADA (`test_mainWindow_settlesAnOutcomeNobodyWaitedFor`). Recogida's Cobrar over the direct connection prints at once, and Recogida settles the rows when AEAT accepts, without asking for a reprint (`test_recogida_directCobrarSettledByRecogida`). Both wirings mutation-checked.
- [x] **Tests: the R5's reference, a reply without a line, the stored event XML** (test engineer gaps 6 and 7 of the 12.0 review): the rectificativa's stored record names the corrected invoice by its InvoiceID and payment date (`test_rectifyDirectQueuedWithoutAnswer`). A reply with no line for a record it carried leaves that record pending (never refused or accepted without a CSV); it is answered PENDING and settles once on its own (`test_replyWithoutALineForARecord`, with `FakeAeatServer::omitLineFor`). `verifactuEventRecordXml` reads the event's paid rows only (`test_verifactuEventRecordXml`).
- [x] **A duplicate is ours only if our earlier send reached AEAT** (test engineer gap 3 of the 12.0 review): AEAT's duplicate answer to a resend was claimed as ours whenever the record had been sent before (`attempts > 1`). If the first send never got there (no connection, TLS), the duplicate is another system's record (e.g. the gateway's), and the rows took an XML and huella AEAT never registered. The transport now says whether the request went out (`requestSent`: `WinHttpSendRequest` succeeded) and the store keeps it (`aeat_records.reached`, added to older tables). Only a duplicate after a send that reached AEAT is claimed. Test `test_duplicateOfAnotherSystemAfterAFailedSend` (fails with the old rule).
- [x] **A payment's InvoiceID is never handed out twice** (compliance auditor note 12 of the 12.0 review): `nextVerifactuInvoiceSeq` counted paid rows only. A Cobrar whose garments all failed to store as paid (the lock race) left no paid row, so the next Cobrar reused `<n>-<seq>`, and the direct client resent the old record or answered with its result for different amounts. It now also skips numbers the direct client already holds a record for (`aeat_records`). Test in `test_nextVerifactuInvoiceSeq`.
- [x] **Pending direct records counted per environment** (compliance auditor note 11 of the 12.0 review): leaving the direct connection is refused while it has unsent records, but `aeatPendingRecordCount` counted every environment. A pre-production self-test record left pending would block the switch back to the gateway for good, even in production. It now counts the direct client's own environment. Test in `test_aeatPendingRecordCount`.
- [x] **Tests for the direct client's QR guards and the main window's save** (test engineer gaps 5 and 8 of the 12.0 review): `test_queuedRecordQrOnlyForItsPendingRecord` (the early QR only from this issuer's pending record with the invoice's date and amount; none once AEAT accepted or refused it) and `test_mainWindow_directSavePrintsAtOnce` (a paid ticket saved in the main window takes the print-at-once path, rows PENDIENTE, never through the gateway). The e2e suite's direct endpoint is now a local server that closes every connection at once, with a 1 s retry delay. A closed port took up to 50 s to fail on Windows and held cases behind the 60 s retry.
- [x] **One system name in the records and the declaración** (compliance auditor 8 of the 12.0 review): the records sent `NombreSistemaInformatico` LAIDEAL while the declaración responsable's item a) said La Ideal. `AeatRecord::laIdealSystem` now says La Ideal, and the declaración reads its a) from the same value. Tests in `test_laIdealSystem` and `test_producerDeclaration`.
- [x] **Printed QR from a transparent image** (test engineer gap 11 of the 12.0 review): `TicketRenderer::printableQr` read transparent pixels as black, so a QR image with transparent light modules gave no readable grid and was printed unscaled. The image is now flattened onto white first. Test in `test_printableQr` (mutation-checked).
- [x] **Refused invoices stay in view** (compliance auditor 5 of the 12.0 review): a refusal after the customer took the factura with its QR was written to a Recogida window's panel, which closes, or to the main window's panel, which the next save overwrites. The main window's status bar now keeps "N factura(s) rechazada(s) por la AEAT" (`sql_lite::refusedInvoiceCount`, paid rows in ERROR) until they are resent. It refreshes at start, on every AEAT outcome and every 30 s for windows of their own. Test `test_mainWindow_refusedInvoicesStayInView` (mutation-checked).
- [x] **Direct client: the store looks invoices up per issuer** (test engineer gap 2 of the 12.0 review): `AeatStore::latest` matched the invoice number only. After the NIF changed in Configuración, the same number under the new issuer was answered with the old issuer's accepted outcome and CSV, or flagged as a resend of its refused record, or waited for a pending record of the old issuer that this backend never sends. Lookups from the backend now pass the issuer. Test `test_sameNumberUnderAnotherIssuer` (mutation-checked).
- [x] **A database restored from an older backup** (test engineer gap 1 and compliance auditor 7 of the 12.0 review; AEAT: a restored DB is the same SIF and continues the chain): a later sync whose second month failed threw away what AEAT had already answered for the first, so the chain could fork after a restore. It now keeps it. The sync also records our system's highest ticket number at AEAT (`AeatDirectBackend::highestNumberAtAeat`); the next ticket and rectificativa number (`sql_lite::nextTicketNumber`) start above it, and the main window warns when the database is behind AEAT. Found on the way: `MainWindow`'s pointer members had no default, and the new number read crashed the window before Verifactu was set up; they now start as nullptr. Tests: `test_laterSyncKeepsWhatAeatAlreadyAnswered` (mutation-checked), `test_firstSyncNetworkFailureGeneratesNothing`, `nextTicketNumber`.
- [x] **A production switch for the direct client** (compliance auditor, must-fix 2 of the 12.0 review): production could only be set by editing `verifactu.aeat_direct_production` in the settings file, so choosing *Directa con la AEAT* in Configuración would have registered real sales in pre-production only. The direct section now has *Enviar a la AEAT real (PRODUCCIÓN, con efectos tributarios)*, off by default; turning it on asks for confirmation (default No). The connection is no longer labelled "(en pruebas)". The self-test still always uses pre-production. Test: `test_directProductionNeedsConfirmation` (mutation-checked). Switching the shop itself is the 12.0 decision.
- [x] **Rectificativas and late outcomes with the direct client** (compliance auditor, must-fix 1 and should-fix 4 of the 12.0 review): with the direct client a NETWORK_ERROR / PENDING answer means the record is stored and will be sent, but Rectificar factura wrote the placeholder row ERROR and re-enabled the form. A second R5 could be issued, Reintentar would resend it as a plain F2, and when AEAT later accepted it the original (substitution) kept counting as income. Now: such a rectificativa is PENDIENTE and the ticket is cleared (`RectifyInvoiceDialog::queuedWithoutAnswer`). An accepted substitution settled later marks its original RECTIFICADA (`applySettledVerifactuResult`, `rectifiedTicketOf`). Recogida's Reintentar and the startup recovery never resend a rectificativa. The backend reports every definitive outcome on `recordSettled`, also when a dialog waited (it may have closed); a cancellation already marked returns 2 and stays silent. Tests: `test_settledRectificativa`, the already-marked cancellation, `recordSettled` with a waiter, `test_rectifyDirectQueuedWithoutAnswer`; each guard mutation-checked.
- [x] **Direct AEAT client: investigation done, ready for the 12.0 decision** (`feature/aeat-direct-investigation`): the report's phases are complete - built, run end to end against AEAT pre-production with the owner's certificate (11/12-10-2026), and adapted to AEAT's answers of 13-10-2026 (own chain per system and issuer, the factura printed at issue, refusals resent then rectified, the declaración responsable per art. 15, QR 30-40 mm). What is left needs the owner or AEAT and is in the tracker: the producer's signature data, AEAT's written answer on production, and switching the shop's connection in the 12.0 release.
- [x] **A refused invoice: resend, then rectify** (AEAT, 13-10-2026: a refused record is resent corrected with the same number and `Subsanacion = S` + `RechazoPrevio = S`; if the amount or date change, a rectificativa follows, because the refused record exists in the SIF): the direct client already does the first part (a resend after a refusal is a new record flagged `Subsanacion` / `RechazoPrevio`, chained after the refused one; `test_rejectionThenNewRecord`). Rectificar factura answered a refused (ERROR) invoice with "no hay nada que rectificar". It now gives the path: resend it from Recogida (Verifactu… → Reintentar), then rectify it there once AEAT accepts it. Test in `test_cancelAndRectify_offeredOnlyForSentInvoices`.
- [x] **Declaración responsable per art. 15 of the Orden** (AEAT, 13-10-2026: the producer signs it, even for own use): new `ProducerDeclaration` (`src/verifactu/producerdeclaration.*`) builds the title and items a) to l) in order with their descriptions, the producer and code from `AeatRecord::laIdealSystem` (so the declaración and the records name the same producer), the version, and an annex with the single-operator scope (Req. 5). Ayuda -> Acerca de Verifactu shows it and saves it as PDF (15.3); with the IreneSolutions connection it notes their own declaración (15.4). The producer's postal address and the date and place of signature (`ProducerDeclaration::currentSignature`) are still to be filled in by Germán: until then they read "(pendiente)" and the window warns. It named the shop as producer and missed most items. Test: `test_aeat_record::test_producerDeclaration` (order of the items, producer, pending and signed).
- [x] **Direct client: a PENDIENTE invoice is reprinted with its QR; no factura when a garment was not stored** (compliance auditor, October 2026): `Imprimir::resolveQrCode` required a CSV, so a reprint before AEAT answered had no QR although the customer's copy had one. With the direct client it now draws the queued record's QR (`qrBeforeReply`, the same record, date and amount) and prints the leyenda, like the copy at issue; the gateway still has none before its CSV. Combined with it: Cobrar printed the factura even when a garment could not be stored as paid (locked or paid meanwhile), so the printed garments and total disagreed with the invoice's QR. `PayDialog::printPartialFactura` now prints nothing then, and Recogida's red message says so. Found on the way: the direct-client e2e tests cleared the fake-endpoint override while a backend was alive, so a queued record could have been sent to the real AEAT test environment. The e2e suite now keeps a dead local endpoint for its whole run. Tests: reprint with the direct client and the gateway, the no-print case; both mutation-checked.
- [x] **Printed QR 30-40 mm and the leyenda in the body font** (Orden HAC/1177/2024 arts. 21.1 and 20.1.b, confirmed October 2026): the QR was scaled smoothly to 192 dots (about 24 mm, blurred modules) and the leyenda printed in Font B. New `TicketRenderer::printableQr` reads the module grid back from any QR image - the gateway's pixmap or the direct client's - (bounding box, module size from the finder pattern's top band, nearest valid size 21 + 4k, sampled at module centres) and redraws it at the largest whole number of dots per module that keeps the symbol within 320 dots, plus the 4-module quiet zone; it fits the 58 mm roll. The leyenda prints in Font A on two lines. Tests: synthetic grid from a crisp and a smoothly scaled source, a real AEAT URL from `AeatQr` (`test_e2e_app`), unreadable input, the leyenda's font.
- [x] **Direct client: its own chain, never IreneSolutions'** (AEAT, 13-10-2026: one chain per SIF and obligado tributario; a restored DB on a new PC is the same SIF and continues it): the chain sync keeps only La Ideal's own records - the local store, and AEAT's registrations whose `SistemaInformatico` is ours (`MostrarSistemaInformatico = S`, `IdSistemaInformatico` LI + producer NIF) - and starts with `PrimerRegistro` when there are none. It runs at every start: two years back the first time (required), this month and the previous one afterwards, falling back to the local chain when AEAT cannot be reached, so a database restored from an older backup continues where AEAT is. The gateway hand-over (`continueChainFromAeat`, `setGatewayRecordXmls`, `seedChain`, `AeatStore::seedChainHead`, `sql_lite::verifactuStoredRecordXmls`) is gone. Found on the way and fixed with it: every record named the issuer as the producer in `SistemaInformatico`; now `AeatRecord::laIdealSystem` (Germán Bravo López, 75929840D, LI) for the app and the self-test. Tests: other systems ignored, new DB of the same system, restored backup, own cancellation as head, fallback without AEAT, producer; the fake AEAT server returns `SistemaInformatico`. Removing the system filter fails `test_chainIgnoresOtherSystems`.
- [x] **Direct AEAT client: the factura prints at once, with the QR** (`feature/aeat-direct-investigation`): in the pre-production runs AEAT's `TiempoEsperaEnvio` (60 s) made the 3 s / 5 s wait miss the reply, so a QR-less recibo printed. The QR is now drawn from the record once it is generated and queued (`VerifactuIntegration::qrBeforeReply` → `AeatDirectBackend::queuedRecordQr`: pending, this issuer, same date and amount), printed with the VERI*FACTU leyenda (`Imprimir::verifactuIssued`) while the rows stay PENDIENTE. Paid at reception: factura with QR for the client + recibo with IMPORTE PAGADO for the shop (was two facturas); Cobrar: one factura. A refusal after that print stays in the result panel. The self-test's cancellation settled after its dialog closed no longer shows a false "No se pudo marcar como anulada" (`applySettledVerifactuCancellation` → -1 only when the database says no ticket). Reviewed by the compliance auditor (should-fix items done); tests in `test_e2e_app`, `test_e2e_aeat_direct`, `test_sql_lite`, each guard mutation-checked.
- [x] **Direct AEAT client, implemented on `feature/aeat-direct-investigation`** (not merged; research): the phased plan of `docs/modules/verifactu/aeat-direct-investigation.md` built up to the proof of concept. `VerifactuBackend` interface with the gateway and a new `AeatDirectBackend` behind it (chosen in Configuración, gateway by default); records built and hashed by the app (`AeatHash`, `AeatRecord`, official vectors and AEAT's own XSDs in a new `aeat_xsd` ctest), chain head and outbox in two new tables (`AeatStore`), flow control, resend / rejection rules, local QR (vendored qrcodegen), the owner's certificate through the Windows crypto API and WinHTTP (Qt's Schannel cannot read PKCS#12 - found by the tests), the hand-over from the gateway via AEAT's chain tip (the gateway's chain does not follow the local ticket order - found in the shop DB), settings and a self-test against AEAT pre-production. 8 new ctest entries plus a fake AEAT SOAP server; key rules mutation-checked. Not run against AEAT: needs the owner's certificate.

### Post-11.1 development — October 2026 (shipped in 11.2)
- [x] **Open garments could not be voided or edited in the shop (since 11.0)**: Anular prendas on ticket 31163 answered "0 prenda(s) anuladas ... se han cobrado, enviado o bloqueado" for an unpaid, unlocked garment. The guard of the amount writers (`kAmountEditableWhere`, and the split's own) tested `COALESCE(edit_lock, 0) = 0`: the shop DB stores `edit_lock` as TEXT `'0'`, and `COALESCE` drops the column's affinity, so SQLite compared text with the integer 0 and nothing matched. Every write behind it was refused: voiding a garment, the m2 size / price, quantity and service edits, the split, and the start-up rounding of three-decimal amounts. Now one shared guard, `kUnlockedWhere` = `COALESCE(edit_lock, 0) IN (0, '0')`: only an explicit 0 is unlocked, and a blank or odd value stays locked. On a copy of the shop DB the garment is voidable and all 15,057 locked rows stay protected. The first 11.2 start rounds the 2 open three-decimal rows (24602, 31267), which no invoice covers. Release review: Verifactu auditor PASS. The test schema declared `edit_lock INTEGER`, so the suite could not see it; it is now TEXT as in the shop (with the old guard, `sql_lite` and `e2e_app` fail). Files: `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.cpp`, `tests/support/testschema.h`.
- [x] **Next ticket number from a numeric maximum** (found while sweeping for the same TEXT-column pitfall): `readMaxValueInColumnFromTable` ran `MAX(n_recibo)` on a TEXT column, which is alphabetical. It was right only because every number has 5 digits. Past 99999, or with any shorter number such as "9999", the next ticket would repeat an invoice number. Now `MAX(CAST(... AS INTEGER))`; the Envíos pendientes list is ordered numerically too. Test in `test_sql_lite` (fails with the old query). Swept the rest: comparisons on the column itself (`edit_lock = 0`), `MAX(edit_lock)` read with `toInt()`, `toBool()` of "0" and `SUM(importe)` are type-safe.

### Post-11.0 development — October 2026 (shipped in 11.1)
- [x] **Imprimir printed twice on Enter** (reported by the shop on 11.0): Herramientas → Imprimir recibo / factura ran the whole print again for the same ticket when the number was confirmed with Enter. The redesign connected the field's `returnPressed` to the print *and* made Imprimir the dialog's default button; `QLineEdit` emits `returnPressed` and leaves the key to the dialog, which pressed the default button as well. The same pattern hit the other ticket fields: in Añadir prendas Enter searched and then pressed Añadir prenda (the search had just cleared the garment, so it only showed "Formulario incompleto" over the search result), in Anular prendas it also pressed Anular seleccionadas, and Anular / Rectificar factura searched twice. New `UiKit::onEnter(lineEdit, action)` runs the action and consumes the key; the five fields use it. Test: `test_enterInNumberFieldRunsOneAction` (fails for each dialog with the old wiring, and when the key is not consumed). From the 11.1 release review: the action runs once per press (an auto-repeated Enter does nothing) and only for acceptable input, the numeric keypad's Enter counts, and in Anular / Rectificar factura no button takes Enter any more (`setAutoDefault(false)`), so Enter in another field neither resets the form through Buscar nor sends the rectificativa (`test_enterInCancelAndRectifyDialogs`). Each fails its test when removed.
- [x] **One Enter rule for every dialog** (from the same review, done in 11.1 instead of filed): `UiKit::setUpDialog` installs a dialog-wide filter - Enter in a field presses the enabled primary button (`primaryButton` marks it), or nothing when the dialog has none; a focused button still presses itself; a held key never repeats. Before, Qt pressed whichever button it had made the default, e.g. the first row's Reintentar in Envíos pendientes or Buscar in Anular / Rectificar factura. Those two dialogs now use `setUpDialog`, so their per-button `setAutoDefault(false)` lines went. Test: new `test_uikit` suite (each rule fails it when removed), plus `test_enterInCancelAndRectifyDialogs`.

### Post-10.13 development — October 2026 (shipped in 11.0)
- [x] **Open non-blocking issues closed before 11.0** (the five audit notes and the start-up repairs' cost): `voidGarmentRow` re-checks in SQL that the garment is open (`kAmountEditableWhere`) and that one row changed, and Anular prendas reports any it could not void; Cobrar stores a garment as paid after AEAT's reply only if it is still unlocked and unpaid, logs and counts the others (`PayDialog::unstoredGarments`) and Recogida shows them in red instead of "cobrado"; the split relink migration logs every row it relinks, since an old separate payment (Verifactu off, before 8.5) could match it - no rule tells the two apart, and the four shop tickets it relinks were checked by hand on a copy of the DB; Contabilidad never locks a quarter whose PDF was not written, and `ReportHtml::writePdf` removes the previous file first and accepts only a non-empty file written now (a stale PDF used to count as success); a gastos row locked by Contabilidad is read-only in Listado (proxy `flags`) and cannot be deleted; the start-up data repairs stay at every launch but are cheap: the split relink, nearly all of their time, is one self-join with a temporary index instead of three correlated subqueries per candidate (~50 ms instead of ~0.75 s on a copy of the shop DB, same four rows). A run-once flag (`PRAGMA user_version`) was tried and dropped after the compliance re-check: an older version opening the DB after the upgrade (rollback, an old exe) writes the old split shape again, and a flag would never repair it. Tests: `test_voidGarmentRow_setsAnuladoAndScopesByHash`, the relink log in `test_migrateDatabase_relinksSplitOffPaidGarment`, `test_payDialog_reportsAGarmentLockedDuringTheSubmit`, `test_contabilidad_unwrittenReportDoesNotLock`, `test_listado_buttonsAndMessagesInWindow`; each fix fails its test when removed. Then, at the shop owner's request, the gap this work found: in Listado de gastos an open row's date could be typed into a closed quarter, adding to its filed gastos. `MySortFilterProxyModel` now runs an `editValidator` before writing (signal `editRefused`), and Listado refuses a `fecha` in a closed quarter or not in dd-mm-aaaa. For that check `readLockForQuarter` leaves an already open connection open - closing it under the live `QSqlTableModel` broke the table (the mutation that restores the close fails the test). Test: `test_listado_gastosDateNeverMovesIntoAClosedQuarter`.
- [x] **Release 11.0 review and compliance gate**: `/code-review medium` on PR #56 - Recogida reports refused writes (a Separar on a locked row, a negative price) instead of a success; Cobrar stores the same cent amount it sends to AEAT; the split checks its transaction and re-checks the row; a negative `importe` is refused by every amount writer; Añadir prendas and Recogida use the shared `garmentUnmeasured`. The `verifactu-compliance-auditor` run on `10.13..develop` then found two more, fixed before the merge: the redesigned **Imprimir** window stays open, and kept the first QR it fetched, so the next factura printed from it (another ticket, or the next partial-payment invoice) carried that QR - it now drops it at every print (`test_imprimirDialog_qrNotReusedForTheNextTicket`); and a ticket save that failed half-way left the first garments stored, which a second Guardar would duplicate under the same number and the start-up recovery would send to AEAT for part of the ticket - `saveTicket` now inserts all rows in one transaction (`sql_lite::insertGarmentRows`, `test_mainWindow_failedInsertStopsTheSave` with the second insert refused). Each guard fails its test when removed; the auditor's non-blocking notes are in the tracker.
- [x] **Main window redesigned with UiKit** (the last window; `mainwindow.ui` removed - no window uses a `.ui` form any more): heading with the shop name and quick buttons (Recogida de prendas, Añadir prendas, Factura de gastos), explanation, Cliente and Ticket groups, the Prendas table with Añadir fila and a read-only total, Limpiar / **Guardar ticket**, result panel. The 25 menu actions are built in code with their former names, texts and shortcuts. Pagado is a checkbox with a SÍ / NO label instead of the red / green button. Every message box of the window (validation, client-name note, Verifactu at start-up, failed backup, the Archivo / Herramientas / Ayuda tools) became result-panel text; the log action links the file. Fixed on the way: picking a garment before its quantity raised a "Cantidad vacía" box - it now counts 1; prices were recalculated on the table's current row instead of the edited one; an unmeasured m2 garment was priced as one m2 (and only names starting with "Alfombra" could be saved at 0) - it is 0,00 until measured and any "m2" garment can be saved like that; the total could be typed by hand; saving checked one month's lock instead of the quarter (`quarterIsClosed`). The save result says what was printed. Test: `test_mainWindow_entryFlowAndMessages` (and the two MainWindow scenarios read the panel). From the `verifactu-compliance-auditor` run on it: a ticket marked paid with an unmeasured m2 garment is refused (it would have reached AEAT at 0, frozen), and Cobrar refuses such a garment too (`test_payDialog_refusesUnmeasuredM2Garment`); one shared rule `sql_lite::garmentUnmeasured` replaces the two that differed (MainWindow, Recogida re-pricing, Cobrar); slots without a garment are no longer stored; the amount sent to AEAT is the sum of the stored rows; negative typed prices are refused. Each guard fails its test when removed.
- [x] **Formulario facturas: suppliers and services in alphabetical order**: both combo boxes were filled in table order; they now go through the new `UiKit::sortedNames` (Spanish collation, case-insensitive, accented names in place). Covered in `test_facturas_saveAndRefusals`.
- [x] **Recogida: labels of a voided garment**: "Anulada" is green (as the table shows cancelled rows), and a locally voided garment no longer shows a red NO / En tienda nor a pickup date - it was never paid nor collected. The payment / pickup labels are now set for every selected row (a checkbox only signals a change, so a row could inherit the previous row's labels), and an empty pickup date shows today instead of the previous row's date. Covered in `test_recogida_voidGarmentsFromTheWindow`.
- [x] **Recogida: Añadir prendas button; Herramientas grouped by function** (requested by the shop): Recogida's Ticket group has **Añadir prendas…**, opening the Añadir nuevas prendas window on the selected ticket (`AddGarment::loadTicket`); a garment added as paid is sent to AEAT from Recogida (`retryVerifactuSubmit(ticket, 0)`), and on close Recogida refreshes and reports how many were added (`AddGarment::garmentSaved`). The menu entry stays. Herramientas now reads Recogida (Recogida de prendas, Añadir nuevas prendas, Imprimir) · Verifactu (Anular / Rectificar factura, Exportar registros AEAT) · Gastos (Formulario facturas) · Contabilidad (Generar / Revertir) · copia de seguridad. Also: the table refresh that follows an AEAT reply no longer overwrites Recogida's last message. Test: `test_recogida_addGarmentFromTheWindow`.
- [x] **Recogida: Anulación date and Anular prendas button** (requested by the shop): the Estado y fechas group shows the read-only **Anulación** date under Recogida (with a red "Anulada"), and an empty payment or anulación date now reads "-" instead of today. The Ticket group has **Anular prendas…**, which opens the void dialog with the selected ticket loaded (`VoidGarmentsDialog::loadTicket`) and reports the result in Recogida; the Herramientas → Anular prendas menu entry was removed. `VoidGarmentsDialog` moved from `src/app/` to `src/recog_prendas/` (Recogida's library cannot reach the app's) and took the UiKit style; its destructive-action confirmation stays a question. Test: `test_recogida_voidGarmentsFromTheWindow`.
- [x] **Recogida de prendas redesigned with UiKit** (requested after the shop approved the new style): built in code, `recog_prendas.ui` removed. Heading and explanation, a Búsqueda group, the table taking the spare height, then Prenda seleccionada / Estado y fechas / Ticket groups and a result panel. The red / green Pagado and Estado buttons became a read-only Pagada checkbox and the Recogida checkbox, each with a coloured label; Pagar todo is the bold **Cobrar…**; the Separar prendas input box became a count field beside the button; the ten message boxes (search, nothing to charge, unpaid reprint, split, AEAT query, CSV adopted) are written in the result panel, while AEAT replies stay in the status bar. The Verifactu details and AEAT comparison windows take the same style. Widgets keep the former object names (slots still connect by name; the e2e bench is unchanged apart from the split count field and the adopted-CSV message). `updateDb` no longer reads button texts (PKU writes `Recogido` / `En tienda` explicitly), the unused second-copy prompt of `printFactura` was dropped, and the quantity column header ("Cant." was set on the size column twice) is fixed.
- [x] **UI style standardisation (UiKit)**: the redesigned Generar contabilidad became the style of every window except MainWindow and Recogida de prendas (deferred, customised). **Centralised**: new `src/uikit/` library (`setUpDialog`, `heading`, `introPanel`, `ResultPanel`, primary / secondary buttons, `dateEdit`, `closeRow`, coloured result phrases, file links) and one PDF writer `ReportHtml::writePdf` (+ the open switch the tests use) replacing the Contabilidad / GenListado copies. **Redesigned in code** (the `.ui` forms of Facturas, Añadir nuevas prendas and Generar listado deleted; the hand-copied form code of Imprimir, Insertar elemento and Listado replaced): every outcome is shown in a result panel instead of message boxes, fonts follow the app font, one bold primary action per window, windows stay open after acting. Notable behaviour changes: Imprimir asks the full-invoice address / DNI, the partial-payment choice and the shop copy in the window; Añadir nuevas prendas uses Pagada / Recogida checkboxes and only opens its form after a successful search; Listado has action buttons and a per-table explanation; Exportar registros AEAT is its own window (`AeatExportDialog`) defaulting to `<reports>/AEAT/`; listados are always regenerated; Facturas, Añadir prendas and Cobrar check the whole quarter's lock (`quarterIsClosed`). Fixes found on the way: Facturas wrote through the default connection and never reported a failed insert; the test schema's `gastos` columns were not in the shop's order; `insertNewItemToTable` now reports failure. Each window has an e2e scenario that drives it by object name, reads its result panel and asserts no pop-up appears; the coding-guidelines skill now asks new windows to use UiKit.
- [x] **Generar / Revertir contabilidad redesigned** (requested by the shop): the dialog is now built in code like Rectificar factura and `contabilidad.ui` is gone. It has an explanation panel, a Periodo group (fields sized to their content), an Opciones group, Comprobar bloqueo and a bold primary Generar contabilidad / Revertir contabilidad on one row, and a result panel that replaces every pop-up. Results are coloured green / amber / red and link to the generated PDF, and the comma-decimal warning is in the panel too. Revert mode sets its own title, text and button. `generateContabilidad()` returns the written file and the invalid-amount count instead of showing message boxes. `test_contabilidad_generateLockThenRevert` now drives the buttons by object name, reads the result panel and asserts no pop-up appears; it also covers the revert texts and "nothing to revert".
- [x] **Generar contabilidad: gastos subtotal, detail on demand, dialog kept open, lock check** (requested by the shop): **(1)** the gastos table reads IVA 21 %, IVA 10 %, a new **Subtotal con IVA** column (21 % + 10 %), Sin IVA, Total. **(2)** The dialog stays open after each report or revert (window-modal over MainWindow, no minimise / help button, Generar / Cerrar buttons); only Cerrar closes it. **(3)** New **Incluir detalle de tickets** checkbox (off by default): the per-ticket annex is only added when ticked, and that version is written to its own `_detalle.pdf` file (title "· Detalle"), so the short summary and the detailed report are both kept (`Contabilidad::reportRelativePath`). **(4)** New **Comprobar bloqueo** button: tells whether the selected quarter / month's quarter / each quarter of the year is locked, has no records, or is open (`Contabilidad::lockStatusMessage`). `Contabilidad::lastReportHtml()` is a new test seam, since the PDFs are compressed. Tests: `test_gastosTable_columnsAndSubtotal`, `test_reportRelativePath`, `test_contabilidad_generateLockThenRevert` extended; each fails against a build without its change.
- [x] **Amounts of invoiced garments frozen; amounts stored in cents**: ticket 30837 (AEAT: 3 Mantel at a hand-entered 10.00 each = 30.00) was re-priced to 24 + 12 by the old Separar prendas when the customer collected in two days; the shop restored 20 + 10 by hand. Separar prendas already keeps the total (previous entry); now the `sql_lite` amount writers (`updateTicketSizeAndPrice`, `updateGarmentQtyAndImporte`, `updateGarmentServiceAndImporte`) also refuse any row that is paid or `ENVIADA` / `ERROR` / `ANULADA` / `RECTIFICADA`, so no caller can change the amount of an invoice AEAT holds - only Anular / Rectificar factura can. Ticket 30723 showed m2 garments stored with three decimals (Recogida's size edit wrote `QString::number(price)`): 28.405 + 22.515 + 9.50 locally against AEAT's 60.43. New `roundToCents` / `moneyText` round half away from zero with a tolerance for binary error, and every writer of `importe` stores through them; `garmentImporte` rounds its result and the float list price, and Recogida's size edit uses it. Of the 42 three/four-decimal rows in the shop, the open (unpaid) ones are rounded by `migrateDatabase` the way they will be charged, and PayDialog reads amounts through `moneyText`; paid ones keep what was stored, and a split of one keeps the exact remainder instead of re-rounding it. From the `verifactu-compliance-auditor` run: the freeze also honours `edit_lock`, and an m2 size of 0 is not priced. Tests: `test_moneyText_roundsHalfAwayFromZero`, `test_amountWriters_roundAndRefuseInvoicedRows`, `test_garmentImporte`, `test_migrateDatabase_roundsOpenAmountsToCents`, the split of a 22.515 row, `test_recogida_m2SizeStoredInCents`; each fails without its fix.
- [x] **Verifactu review follow-ups** (the open items left by the `verifactu-compliance-auditor` reviews of the 10.13 export and cancellation work), checked first against a copy of the shop DB: **(1) Separar prendas on a paid garment** inserted the split-off row with empty `verifactu_*` columns and seq 0, outside the invoice AEAT registered, and re-priced both rows from the price list. It happened in the shop: four tickets (30492, 30572, 30837, 30990) had an export / reprint total below AEAT's. The split is now `sql_lite::splitGarmentRow`, which copies the whole row (seq, estado, CSV, id, payload) and divides the `importe` in proportion to the cent; `migrateDatabase` relinks existing split rows to the one `ENVIADA` invoice with the same payment date, garment and service (cancelled / ambiguous matches only logged), which restores three of the four to AEAT's total; 30837 was also re-priced and is filed separately. **(2)** Export: invoices AEAT holds without a readable payment date (none today) are counted and reported in the result message. **(3)** Export: a payload that cannot be inlined (not well-formed, a DOCTYPE - which `QXmlStreamReader` tolerates inside an element - or an undeclared prefix) is written as escaped text with `payloadComoTexto="1"`. **(4)** A cancellation without a date (pre-10.12; none in the shop) is written after its `<Registro>` with `sinFecha="1"` instead of never. **(5)** `fechaExpedicion` is added to `<Registro>` / `<Anulacion>` when the payload's first `FechaExpedicionFactura[Anulada]` differs from the payment date (an invoice registered under its reception date). **(6)** `CancelInvoiceDialog` reads all events of a ticket in one query (`sql_lite::submittedInvoiceEvents`) and keeps the reception date once. **(7)** The test suites build `ingresos` through `migrateDatabase` (`tests/support/testschema.h`), checked against `INGRESOS_COL_*`, instead of three hand-copied schemas. **(8)** `updateTicketVerifactuFields` returns the rows changed; a reply for an already settled invoice is logged and reported as ignored, and Recogida no longer queries AEAT for it. **(9)** The one-off query found no `(n_recibo, seq)` group mixing `ENVIADA` and `PENDIENTE` rows. From the `verifactu-compliance-auditor` run on this work: Anular factura dates an invoice over all its paid rows (as retry and query do), the relink only takes seq-0 rows (a payment made while Verifactu was off is its own event), `splitGarmentRow` refuses a row locked by Contabilidad itself, and a reply whose local write fails no longer shows "enviado". Each fix has a test that fails without it.

### Post-10.12 development — October 2026 (shipped in 10.13)
- [x] **Release 10.13 review fixes** (from `/code-review medium` on PR #55 and the release `verifactu-compliance-auditor` run): **(1)** Añadir nuevas prendas checked for paid garments only at search time while the number stayed editable: retyping an already-sent ticket after searching an unpaid one would add a paid garment to it and - since this release - submit invoice `<n>` again; a duplicate rejection would then have rewritten the sent rows to `ERROR`, wiping their CSV / payload / Huella (auditor: blocking). Now the save is refused unless the number is the one searched, the paid check is repeated at save, and `updateTicketVerifactuFields` never rewrites a row already `ENVIADA` / `ANULADA` / `RECTIFICADA`. **(2)** The AEAT export filtered dates row by row before grouping, so a legacy invoice paid over several days appeared in two periods with partial amounts; it now filters on the event's earliest `fecha_pago` / latest `fecha_anulacion` (`HAVING`), the latter by date rather than text. **(3)** A ticket with several cancelled payment events merged into one regularisation kept whichever row's payment date came first, so the period ticket count depended on row order; it keeps the latest. **(4)** When the reception-date retry of a cancellation also failed, the first (real) rejection was lost; the dialog now shows both. Also `kEarliestFechaPago` reuses `kFechaPagoIso`, and three doc drifts were fixed (AddGarment submit path, Req. 1 insert paths, `NETWORK_ERROR` at save stays `PENDIENTE`). Each fix has a test that fails without it. Smaller findings were filed in the "Verifactu review follow-ups" tracker row.
- [x] **Añadir nuevas prendas: a garment added as paid is submitted to AEAT at once**: the open item asked whether a paid garment added with AddGarment could collide with an invoice already registered under seq 0. It cannot: AddGarment refuses any ticket that already has a paid garment, so such a garment is always the ticket's first payment event (seq 0, InvoiceID = ticket number, its payment date), exactly what the startup recovery would submit. The real gap was timing: nothing submitted it when saved; it sat `PENDIENTE` until the next startup's recovery dialog and an operator's "Reintentar". Now `AddGarment` emits `paidGarmentSaved` after inserting a paid row and MainWindow submits it through `verifactuSubmitInvoice` like a paid ticket on save; the reply is applied by the usual handler and an unanswered submission stays `PENDIENTE` for the recovery. Test: `test_e2e_app::test_addGarmentPaid_submittedAtOnce` (one Create with the ticket number, today's date and the garment's total; row `ENVIADA` in seq 0; the unpaid garment untouched); it fails without the connection. Files: `src/add_garment/add_garment.{h,cpp}`, `src/app/mainwindow.cpp`, `tests/test_e2e_app.cpp`, `docs/modules/add_garment.md`.
- [x] **AEAT's cancellation record is stored and exported**: the `/Cancel` reply carries AEAT's own cancellation record in `Return.Xml` (parsed into `VerifactuResult::rawXml` like a `/Create` reply), but it was thrown away, so the Hacienda export could only show a cancellation as a date on the original invoice. New column `ingresos.verifactu_cancel_xml` (added by `migrateDatabase`, hidden in Listado and Recogida); `markInvoiceSeqCancelled` takes the reply's XML and stores it on the cancelled event's paid rows (a later call without one never erases it). Exportar registros AEAT now writes a `<Registro>` in the period an invoice was issued and, for a cancellation, an `<Anulacion invoiceId fechaPago fechaAnulacion>` in the period it was made with that record inlined (`sinPayload="1"` for cancellations made before this change); the original invoice is no longer repeated in the cancellation's period. (The envelope element is `Anulacion`, not `RegistroAnulacion`, because AEAT's own payload root has that name.) A substituted invoice gets no `<Anulacion>`: its rectificativa is a `<Registro>` of its own. Tests: `test_markInvoiceSeqCancelled_stampsTheCancelledEvent` (stored, never erased), `test_cancelInvoice_markedAnulada` (the dialog stores the reply), `test_aeatExport_oneRegistroPerPaymentEvent` (issue vs cancellation periods); the dialog test fails if the XML is not passed.
- [x] **Anular factura sends each invoice's own date**: `CancelInvoiceDialog` sent every payment event's cancellation with `InvoiceDate` = the ticket's earliest reception date, but AEAT keys an invoice on (emisor, InvoiceID, FechaExpedicion) and every submission path issues it under the payment date (`fecha_pago`). For a ticket paid at pickup on a later day (the usual case) or any partial-payment event `<n>-<seq>`, the cancellation named a date AEAT never registered, and would probably have been rejected as not existing. Found by the compliance review of the AEAT export fix. Now each event's date comes from `verifactuEventFor` (the same rule as retry and query) and is shown in a new "Fecha factura" column; an event without a readable payment date is refused locally with a pointer to "Consultar en AEAT". Following the compliance review: the earliest payment date is now taken by date, not as `dd-MM-yyyy` text (`MIN` over the text put 05-03 before 28-01), in `verifactuEventFor`, the startup recovery and the AEAT export alike; this matters for pre-10.9 seq-0 invoices paid garment by garment on different days, which AEAT registered under the first payment. And because 8.0-10.8 resubmissions used the reception date, a rejected cancellation of a seq-0 invoice is retried once with the reception date (only one invoice can have that InvoiceID, so it cannot reach another). Tests: `test_cancelInvoice_markedAnulada` (ticket received 20 days ago, paid 10 days ago and partly paid again today: each Cancel's InvoiceID and date, both events ANULADA), `test_cancelInvoice_olderDataDates` (earliest date, the reception-date retry, the local refusal), `test_verifactuEventFor` (date vs text order); each fails with the old behaviour. The review also raised an unconfirmed AddGarment / seq-0 question, filed as an open item. Files: `src/app/cancelinvoicedialog.{h,cpp}`, `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.cpp`, `tests/test_e2e_app.cpp`, `tests/test_sql_lite.cpp`.
- [x] **Exportar registros AEAT: one record per invoice (payment event)**: found by the dead-code pass. The Hacienda export (Verifactu Req. 7, Art. 14.1 RD 1007/2023) wrote one `<Registro>` per **garment row**, so a multi-garment invoice repeated its XML payload once per garment. It used the bare `n_recibo`, so the partial-payment invoices `<n>-<seq>` could not be told apart, filtered by reception date instead of the date the invoice was issued under, and wrote one garment's amount. Now `sql_lite::aeatExportRecords` returns one record per paid `(n_recibo, verifactu_invoice_seq)` event with a stored payload (any estado: AEAT keeps cancelled and rectified invoices), filtered by `fecha_pago`, with the literal InvoiceID, the event total, CSV and estado. The XML writer moved out of the menu slot into `src/app/aeatexport.{h,cpp}` (`writeAeatExportXml`). The `<Registro>` attributes are now `nRecibo`, `invoiceId`, `fechaPago`, `importe`, `csv`, `estado`, plus `fechaAnulacion`, `rectifica` / `tipoRectificacion` and `sinPayload` when they apply. Following the compliance review, the export also includes invoices known only by their CSV (recovered with "Consultar en AEAT", which stores no payload) and invoices from an earlier period cancelled inside the range, never merges two submissions sharing a seq (grouped by CSV too), and reports a failed query or write instead of producing an empty file. The review also found that Anular factura sends the reception date rather than the invoice's payment date, and that the cancel reply's XML is not stored; both filed as open items. Tests: `test_sql_lite::test_aeatExportRecords_onePerPaymentEvent` (grouping, totals, legacy id, CSV-only invoice, cancellation in the period, rectificativa link, unpaid / unconfirmed / out-of-range rows excluded, inclusive range) and `test_e2e_app::test_aeatExport_oneRegistroPerPaymentEvent` (records + writer as the menu runs them; the document parses, each payload appears once). Both fail with the old per-row grouping, without the CSV-only case, and without the cancellation-date case; the unit test also fails with the reception-date filter.
- [x] **Dead-code cleanup pass; dead-code report and guidelines-audit doc retired**: ran the `dead-code-finder` agent over `src/` and removed what production never reaches. Removed: Recogida's per-garment payment writes (`updateDb` `PAY_YES` / `PAY_NO`, unreachable because `pb_payment` is never enabled; payments only go through PayDialog) with `hasPendingSubmit` and the then test-only `sql_lite::updateTicketPayment` / `ticketVerifactuEstado` and their tests; `PayDialog::onSelectionChanged` (never connected); `VerifactuInvoice` getters with no caller and the never-set buyer fields (their `BuyerID` JSON branch could not fire; `isValid` still refuses any type other than F2 / R1-R5, same message); `VerifactuTaxItem`'s unused `TaxType` and never-set description; `VerifactuResult::isError` and the never-read `rawResponse`; `EscPosBuilder::raw`; the date-range filter of `MySortFilterProxyModel` (never wired); the write side of `FilterWidget`'s two properties; `RecogPrendas::columnClickedCell` and `RectifyInvoiceDialog::m_loadedCSV` (written, never read). `VerifactuManager::getValidationUrl` / `getLastError` and `BackupManager::backupDirectory` became private (internal callers only). Fixed on the way: `RecogPrendas::rowClickedCell` was uninitialised and could be read before any row was clicked (now `-1`); a comment in `verifactutypes.h` sat above the wrong declaration. Kept on purpose and now listed in the agent's exclusions: the test hooks (`setEndpointOverride`, `setOpenGeneratedReports`, `loadFrom`, `setBackupEnabled`, the DPAPI wrappers, `EscPosBuilder::columns`). Payloads for the invoices the app issues (F2, R1-R5) are unchanged; checked by the `verifactu-compliance-auditor`. The pass also found that **Exportar registros AEAT** is not grouped by payment event; that is a real bug, filed as an open tracker item rather than folded into the cleanup. `docs/dead_code_report.md` is gone (all its items are resolved; the agent now reports to the main agent and no snapshot is kept). Likewise `docs/coding_guidelines_audit.md` moved into the skill as `.claude/skills/coding-guidelines/audit.md`: the grep checklist, the severity tiers and the accepted Tier 2 / Tier 3 exceptions (without line numbers); the dated findings snapshot was dropped, since open items live in the tracker. The `guidelines-auditor` agent, the release skill, `INDEX`, `README`, `ai_agent_instructions` and the MkDocs nav point to the new place.
- [x] **E2E coverage: Recogida late-reply adoption and the AEAT comparison dialog**: these two screens were checked only by the smoke test's real-AEAT blocks, which were dropped when the smoke test stopped contacting AEAT. Their logic was already covered; Recogida's own wiring was not: taking over PayDialog's in-flight request, chaining a duplicate rejection into the AEAT query, the comparison dialog and its Actualizar rules, and which Verifactu buttons each row offers. `test_e2e_app` gains three scenarios on the real `RecogPrendas` with the fake server. (1) Pay-all → Cobrar with a Create reply delayed 7 s: PayDialog gives up at 5 s leaving `PENDIENTE`, then the late reply makes the row `ENVIADA` with its CSV and the status bar offers the factura with QR. (2) An `ERROR` row → Reintentar answered "duplicado" → automatic query → comparison dialog (match, AEAT's CSV, Actualizar enabled) → adopt → `ENVIADA` with AEAT's CSV. (3) Consultar en AEAT when nothing may be adopted (not found, different amount, already `ENVIADA` = informational), plus the button rules (Reintentar only on `ERROR`, neither on an unpaid row). New `tests/support/modaldriver.h` operates dialogs opened with `exec()` (one-shot match + action per dialog); stable object names were added to the Verifactu and comparison dialogs in `recog_prendas.cpp`, with no behaviour change. Four mutations were each caught (no takeover of the request, no query after a duplicate, Actualizar enabled on any found record, Consultar on unpaid rows). The suite now takes about 15 s. The smoke test's "not covered" note and the tracker row are gone.
- [x] **Smoke test rewritten: shorter, and it never contacts AEAT**: the old checklist sent smoke tickets to the real IreneSolutions endpoint with whatever service key was configured. `TEST_ENDPOINT` and `PROD_ENDPOINT` are the same URL, so with the shop's key they landed in the shop's own AEAT records. That happened in the blocks marked *registers at AEAT* and, silently, in any paid save or Cobrar made while online. Their numbers would also collide with real tickets the shop issues later, because the copied DB's next number is the real one. Now the setup backs up the settings, empties `ingresos` / `gastos` in the backup copy, and points the settings at the copy with an empty `verifactu.service_key`, so every submit / cancel / rectify / QR / query path is skipped locally. The first launch must show "No se pudo inicializar Verifactu", or the run stops. A log check (no `Submitting invoice` / `Server response`, etc.) repeats at the end, and the settings are restored afterwards. Seeds are five integer-numbered tickets plus one gasto, chosen so the Q3 / Q4 2026 reports have known figures, a regularisation included. Everything the e2e bench and unit suites check by themselves was dropped: migrations, saves, voids, cancel / rectify guards, recovery listing, offline PENDIENTE, accounting totals. What is left is start-up on a real schema, wiring, printing, PDF rendering, the Listado lock, backup, language and installer: 5 blocks and 25 checks instead of 7 blocks and 48. `test_e2e_app` gained `test_cancelAndRectify_offeredOnlyForSentInvoices` (Anular disabled on `PENDIENTE`, unpaid tickets refused) to replace two manual rows. The dropped real-AEAT screens (Recogida late-reply adoption, the comparison dialog) became an open tracker item. The release skill and the production-switch row now say the smoke test never contacts AEAT. Files: `docs/testing/smoke_test.md`, `tests/test_e2e_app.cpp`, `docs/testing/e2e.md`, `docs/testing/README.md`, `.claude/skills/release/SKILL.md`, `docs/progress_tracker.md`.
- [x] **Application-level test bench, phase 2: MainWindow, the app dialogs, Imprimir and Contabilidad at screen level**: phase 1 covered PayDialog and the Verifactu client; the app's own windows were compiled into the executable and could only be checked by hand. `src/app/` now builds a `laideal_app` static library (MainWindow + Cancel / Rectify / Void / Pending dialogs) and `laideal.exe` is just `main.cpp` + resources linking it (same exe path, installer and CI unchanged). New suite `tests/test_e2e_app.cpp` (nine scenarios, ~6 s, every CI run) drives the windows as an operator would and asserts on the DB, on the requests the fake AEAT received and on the pop-ups: MainWindow save of an unpaid and a paid ticket (incl. the 3 s print-after-submit wait and the reqId correlation) and the no-client refusal; Anular prendas; Anular factura and its closed-quarter refusal; Rectificar por sustitución with the before-payment and closed-quarter date refusals; the startup Envíos pendientes recovery and its retry; an Imprimir reprint of one payment event (paid rows of that seq only, QR requested under its InvoiceID and payment date, none once ANULADA); and Contabilidad Trimestral generate + lock + revert writing the PDF. Each scenario was proven against a mutated build. Test seams added: `Contabilidad::setOpenGeneratedReports(false)` (skips opening the PDF viewer), stable object names on the dialog widgets, `ModalAutoCloser` answers Yes/No confirmations with Yes, the fake server's GetQrCode returns a real image, and a shared `tests/support/e2efixture.h`. Same safety rules as phase 1: local fake server only (the suite refuses to start otherwise), throwaway DB and settings. This absorbs the two parked Backlog "Test coverage" rows (MainWindow async-AEAT wiring, Imprimir seq filter / QR gating), and retires smoke-test checks A5–A7, B1 and G10. See `docs/testing/e2e.md`.
- [x] **Testing documentation moved to `docs/testing/`**: requested before phase 2 of the test bench, so test knowledge sits in one organised place instead of a long section of `architecture.md`. It now has four pages. **`README.md`** covers the three layers (unit suites, end-to-end bench, manual smoke test), running locally and in CI (JUnit results, the step-summary script), the conventions (real libs, pure cores, throwaway state only, application type, prove each test can fail, 120 s timeouts, the UAC naming rule), the patterns table, adding a suite and the CI toolchain. **`unit_suites.md`** has every suite in an index table and then grouped by module, refreshed for this cycle's tests: listings, regularisations, migrations, ticket counts, language and IVA. **`e2e.md`** has the bench's safety rules, `FakeVerifactuServer` / `ModalAutoCloser` usage, the scenario table, how to add a scenario and the phase 2 plan. **`smoke_test.md`** was moved from `docs/` with `git mv`. `architecture.md` keeps a short Testing section that links there. All live references were updated: the `test-engineer` agent, the `/tackle-issue` and `/release` skills, `ai_agent_instructions.md`, `INDEX.md`, `docs/README.md`, the tracker, `.gitignore`. The MkDocs site gains a Testing section, plus the previously unlisted Completed milestones page. `docs.yml` needed no change, since it already triggers on `docs/**` and `mkdocs.yml`, and the site was built locally with no new warnings.
- [x] **Application-level test bench, phase 1: fake Verifactu server + end-to-end payment / AEAT scenarios**: the unit suites tested logic and `sql_lite` in isolation, while the flows across objects were covered only by the manual smoke test: payment -> AEAT submission -> reply -> estado/QR -> reconciliation -> Contabilidad. The user chose these design points. **Seam**: the in-process `VerifactuConfig::setEndpointOverride(baseUrl)` redirects every endpoint (Create / Cancel / GetFilteredList / GetQrCode). Only code linked into a test can call it, so no setting, environment variable or registry value can divert real invoices. It also matters because TESTING and PRODUCTION are the same hard-coded URL, so there is no real sandbox. **`tests/support/FakeVerifactuServer`** is a minimal HTTP/1.1 server on 127.0.0.1 (random port). It records every request (endpoint + JSON) and answers with a queued scripted reply or a default "accepted" reply (CSV, ValidationUrl, Huella XML, a real PNG QR). Replies can be delayed or dropped, and the builders follow the captured fixtures in `test_verifactu_response`. **`tests/support/ModalAutoCloser`** closes and records any `QMessageBox` (user choice: auto-dismiss and record), so a pop-up never blocks the run headless. **`test_e2e_verifactu`** (runs on every CI run, offscreen, about 9 s) drives the real `PayDialog` as Cobrar does, with the real client and network stack, against a seeded throwaway DB and a throwaway settings file with printing off. It never touches the real database, the user's settings or the AEAT, and refuses to start unless the endpoint is local. Six scenarios: accepted; two payment events as invoices `n` and `n-1`; no reply within 5 s -> PENDIENTE -> retry answered "already exists" -> the queried CSV is adopted (ENVIADA); AEAT rejection -> ERROR; a payment into a closed quarter is refused, nothing sent; paid Q1 -> Q1 closed -> cancelled in Q2 -> Q1 unchanged, Q2 regularised, the remainder charged as `n-1`. The last scenario immediately found a real bug in 10.12's ticket count, fixed in its own commit just below. **Phase 2** (move MainWindow + the app dialogs into a static lib and add screen-level scenarios) stays Open in the tracker. Files: `src/verifactu/verifactuconfig.{h,cpp}`, `tests/support/{fakeverifactuserver.h,fakeverifactuserver.cpp,modalautocloser.h}`, `tests/test_e2e_verifactu.cpp`, `tests/CMakeLists.txt`, docs.
- [x] **Contabilidad ticket count: only a same-period cancellation offsets a ticket (bug found by the new e2e test bench)**: 10.12's `netTicketCount` netted a ticket's income against every regularisation of the period, regardless of which payment was being cancelled. A ticket with a new sale in Q2 (its remaining garment charged as `<n>-1`) and the cancellation of its Q1 payment in Q2 netted below zero in Q2, so it was not counted even though Q2 had a real sale. The first end-to-end scenario written for the test bench (paid Q1 -> Q1 closed -> cancelled at AEAT in Q2 -> remainder charged in Q2) caught it. `netTicketCount` now takes the period and lets a regularisation offset only when the cancelled payment's `fecha_pago` lies in that same period; `figuresFromDetails` passes the report period through, and `yearTicketCount(details, year)` the calendar year. The user's rule is unchanged: paid and cancelled in the same period is not counted, and paid in Q1 / cancelled in Q2 is counted in Q1. Amounts were never affected, only the "Número de tickets" figure. Tests: `test_netTicketCount` gains the Q1-payment / Q2-sale case, and existing fixtures carry the payment date. Files: `src/contabilidad/{contabilidad.h,contabilidad.cpp}`, `tests/test_contabilidad.cpp`, `docs/modules/contabilidad.md`.
### Post-10.11 development — October 2026 (shipped in 10.12)
- [x] **10.12 release gate: fixes from the PR review and the compliance gate**: a medium `/code-review` of the commits after the pre-release audit, and the new `verifactu-compliance-auditor` release gate, confirmed the closed-quarter fix and found the following, all fixed before merging. **Rectify**: the red "AEAT accepted but the local RECTIFICADA mark failed" message was immediately overwritten by the green success text. `applyRectificationResult` now returns whether the local mark succeeded, and the success text is shown only when it did (both reviewers flagged this). **Cancel**: after a failed local write the event is shown ANULADA, so its button cannot send a second cancellation to AEAT. A quarter closed while the request was in flight moves the stamped date to today. The new message uses `tr()`, like its neighbours. The event list groups only `pagado = 'SI'` rows, like `verifactuEventFor`, so a part-paid seq-0 invoice no longer shows as SIN COBRAR with its Anular button disabled. **Marks**: `markInvoiceSeqCancelled` / `markTicketRectified` return false when they match no paid row, instead of reporting a silent success. **Data repair**: a `migrateDatabase` step returns to `SIN COBRAR` the unpaid garments that pre-10.12 cancellations had marked `ANULADA`, leaving local voids alone. A gate re-check showed that unpaid `RECTIFICADA` rows are not provably safe to re-open: before `dc87a5e` a substitution could have been submitted with their amounts included. They are only logged for manual review. The same re-check made a failed UPDATE of the new rectificativa row count as a failed local write too. Without it the release note's "they can still be charged" would not hold for existing data; the note now says so in both languages. **Ticket count**: only a positive net counts, so a by-differences credit note is not a ticket, while a comma-flagged real sale still is. `yearTicketCount` now reuses `netTicketCount`. Dismissed with reasons: the "last payment includes cancelled seqs" case is unreachable, because the rectify dialog refuses multi-seq tickets and accepts only ENVIADA ones; the CI regex's `r?` is intentional, to stop at the legacy `r7.1`-style headers. Tests: marks fail on no paid row, unpaid-remainder repair (incl. a local void left alone), credit note and comma-only ticket counts. An existing fixture got realistic amounts, because a zero-amount ticket correctly no longer counts. Verified non-vacuous. Verifactu doc drift flagged by the auditor fixed too. A one-off, idempotent `migrateDatabase` backfill moves the stamped date of existing voids (estado `Anulado`, `pagado != 'SI'`, `ANULADA`) into `fecha_anulacion` and empties both fields; AEAT-cancelled paid rows are not touched. **AEAT cancellation / substitution rectification**: by user decision, `markInvoiceSeqCancelled` / `markTicketRectified` now record `fecha_anulacion` on every row they mark, not only on rows of an already-closed quarter, still without overwriting an existing date. The Contabilidad rule becomes uniform: a paid row stays income in its payment period and is subtracted in the period of its `fecha_anulacion`. Within the same period the two net out, matching the old result. The one visible change is a sale cancelled in a later quarter while its own quarter was still open: it now shows +original / −cancellation quarter instead of silently leaving the original quarter. Pre-10.12 cancellations without a date stay excluded as before. Voided garments are unpaid, so they are never income nor a regularisation. All verified non-vacuous by restoring the old rules. - [x] **Closed quarters no longer change after an AEAT cancellation or rectification; the change is counted where it happens**: neither `CancelInvoiceDialog` nor `RectifyInvoiceDialog` respected the accounting lock. Cancelling at AEAT, or substituting via a rectificativa, a garment from an already-closed quarter set `ANULADA` / `RECTIFICADA`, the garment silently dropped out of its original quarter, and regenerating that filed quarter gave different figures. The rectificativa row itself was inserted unlocked (`edit_lock = 0`) and could be dated inside a closed month. Following the user's choice ("count where it happens", the usual fiscal treatment), a new `ingresos.fecha_anulacion` column (`migrateDatabase`, `INGRESOS_COL_FECHA_ANULACION` = 26, hidden in Recogida) is stamped only on rows whose quarter is already closed: the cancellation date, or the rectificativa's date. The DB writes moved out of both dialogs into the tested seams `sql_lite::markInvoiceSeqCancelled` / `markTicketRectified`, with the same seq/ticket scoping, still only after AEAT success, and never overwriting an existing date. `kIngresosIncomeWhere` keeps a stamped row as income in its closed quarter, so the filed report is reproducible. The new `regularizationsBetweenDates` (and `QuarterlyDetails::regularizations`) lists it in the period of `fecha_anulacion`, where `figuresFromDetails` subtracts it (`PeriodFigures::ingRegularizacion`). The Ingresos table then shows period income, the regularisation and the net total, and the annex adds an "Anulaciones y rectificaciones de periodos anteriores" table with negative amounts. Rows from open quarters behave as before, and pre-existing cancellations are untouched. **Guards**, the first two raised by the `verifactu-compliance-auditor` review: the rectificativa date must be in an open quarter, and a cancellation is refused while today's quarter is closed. Both use the new `sql_lite::quarterIsClosed`, which checks the whole quarter across `ingresos` and `gastos`, because the month-level `readLockForMonthAndYear` reads a row-less month (e.g. August) as open. Tests: `markInvoiceSeqCancelled` stamps only closed rows of the right seq; `markTicketRectified` uses the rectification date; a Q1-closed ticket cancelled in Q2 stays in Q1 income and is a Q2 regularisation (incl. annual bucketing); `quarterIsClosed` covers a row-less month and gastos-only; no overwrite; `figuresFromDetails` nets regularisations; the regularisation table renders. All verified non-vacuous. - [x] **Contabilidad figures now come from the detail rows (single source of truth), closing the annex reconciliation gaps**: the Contabilidad summary used to be computed by separate SQL helpers (`totalPriceBetweenDates`, `countOperationsBetweenDates`, plus the grouped `annualAccountingByQuarter` for the annual report), while the 10.11 detail annex listed rows through other queries. Four gaps followed, all fixed. **(c)** Every mode now fetches the period's detail rows once (`incomeTicketsBetweenDates` / `expensesBetweenDates`; annual `annualDetailsByQuarter`, one scan per table bucketed by quarter), and the new pure static `Contabilidad::figuresFromDetails()` derives the `PeriodFigures` from those same rows. A summary can no longer disagree with its detail, and Mensual/Trimestral drop from 6 + 2 queries to 2. **(a)** A comma-decimal `importe` is now flagged rather than silently read as 0 or 10. The collectors count it (`IncomeTicketDetail::invalidAmounts`, `ExpenseDetail::invalidAmount`) and never sum it; the detail marks the ticket `*` or the gasto `**` with a note; and `generateContabilidad()` raises the clean-up-tool error dialog once per report. **(d)** Because every mode reads the same collectors, the annual report treats comma amounts exactly like the quarterly one; previously its SQL `SUM()` handled them differently and without the dialog. **(b)** Each detail table ends with a note that per-line base/IVA are rounded to cents while the totals are unrounded. The annual consolidated ticket count is now `Contabilidad::yearTicketCount()`, a distinct `n_recibo` over the year's buckets, replacing the short-lived `ingTicketsYear` field. **Removed as dead code**: `totalPriceBetweenDates`, `countOperationsBetweenDates`, `annualAccountingByQuarter` / `QuarterlyAccountingTotals`, and `Contabilidad::getTotalIncome` / `computeFigures`, which had no other callers. Their tests were rewritten against the listings, plus new `figuresFromDetails`, `yearTicketCount`, flagging and rendering tests, verified non-vacuous by breaking the sin-IVA bucket and the comma skip. A rate change would therefore have silently altered every regenerated past report, along with every ticket, factura and AEAT submission made afterwards. The user decided the ingresos rate will not change from 21 %, so it is now the internal constant `AppSettings::kIngresosIvaRate`, exposed as the static `AppSettings::ivaRate()` so no call site had to change (Contabilidad, PayDialog, Recogida retry, MainWindow, Imprimir, RectifyInvoiceDialog, TicketRenderer data). The "Tipo de IVA (%)" field was removed from Configuración → General, `setIvaRate` is gone, and `applyDefaults()` no longer writes a default `taxes.iva_rate`. For backwards compatibility an existing key in a user's JSON is left untouched (older app versions still read it) but ignored. Gastos keep their per-invoice `iva` column. Test `test_ivaRateIsFixedAndIgnoresJson` checks a JSON value of 10 is ignored. The amounts were correct; only the count was inflated. `annualAccountingByQuarter` now also returns `ingTicketsYear`, a year-level distinct count under the shared `kIngresosIncomeWhere` predicate, and the consolidated summary uses it. The per-quarter sections keep their own counts, where the ticket correctly appears in each. Test `test_annualTicketCountIsDistinctOverYear` checks it equals `countOperationsBetweenDates` over the year. It now reads both tables and merges them in the pure static `Contabilidad::combinedLockState(ingresosLock, gastosLock)`: no data only when both are empty, locked if either is, else open. `updateLock()` already locked and unlocked both tables by month, so closing and reverting a gastos-only quarter work unchanged. The Mensual and Anual "Contabilidad cerrada / no cerrada" labels use the same merge, and the empty-period message now says "ni de gastos". Test `test_combinedLockState`. The user decided to keep stamping both: a voided garment carrying a `fecha_pago` with `pagado = 'NO'` and estado `Anulado` reads unambiguously as "closed, never paid" throughout the app. The side effects are already handled. Every total skips `ANULADA` rows (`garmentExcludedFromTotals`, `kIngresosIncomeWhere`), so a Pago-date search lists a voided garment on its void date without adding it. The month lock covers it harmlessly, because `readLockForMonthAndYear` / `readLockForQuarter` use `MAX(edit_lock)` and a voided row cannot mask a locked sibling. Closed without a code change.
