### Post-9.0 bug fixes — June 2026
- [x] **Startup backup moved off the GUI thread + single-query client phone lookup**: two of the three perf findings from the 9.0 review. (1) `BackupManager::performBackup()` (`VACUUM INTO` + `PRAGMA integrity_check`, seconds on a large DB) ran on the GUI thread ~3 s after launch and froze the UI for its duration; it now runs on a `QThread::create` worker (safe — it uses its own dedicated DB connections and touches no GUI), with the `Result` marshalled back to the main thread via `QMetaObject::invokeMethod(this, …, Qt::QueuedConnection)` for the success status-bar / failure message-box. (2) `RecogPrendas::updateRowClickedToFields` looked up `tel_fijo` and `movil` with two separate `selectFromWhereLike` calls (two DB open/close + two SELECTs) on every row click; new `sql_lite::readClientPhones(db, client)` returns both in one `SELECT tel_fijo, movil … WHERE nombre = :n`. The third finding (annual Contabilidad report's ~24 full-table scans) was deliberately deferred — see Open Non-Blocking — because it rewrites the quarterly tax aggregation and needs verification against real data. Files: `src/app/mainwindow.cpp`, `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.h/.cpp`, `src/recog_prendas/recog_prendas.cpp`, `docs/modules/sql_lite.md`, `docs/architecture.md`.
- [x] **PayDialog AEAT timeout now records `PENDIENTE`, not `Error` (and recovery is scoped so it can't mis-resubmit)**: when the 5 s bounded wait expired, `PayDialog::persistPayment` ran a `NETWORK_ERROR` result through `updateTicketVerifactuFields`, which wrote `verifactu_estado = Error`. A slow-but-accepted AEAT submission was therefore recorded as a failure, and `PendingSubmitsDialog` (which only re-surfaces `NULL`/empty/`PENDIENTE`) never offered to reconcile it, so a later manual retry risked an AEAT 8003 duplicate. Now a `NETWORK_ERROR`/`PENDING` outcome (timeout or transport failure — an *unknown*, not a definitive rejection) is persisted as `PENDIENTE` via the new `PayDialog::markPendingVerifactu(seq)`, which keeps the `<n_recibo>-<seq>` InvoiceID and leaves CSV/XML empty; a genuine AEAT `ERROR` reply still writes `Error`. Because `PendingSubmitsDialog` re-submits a whole ticket as a bare `n_recibo` (it is seq-unaware), its query was scoped with `AND verifactu_invoice_seq = 0` so a partial-pay (`seq>0`) `PENDIENTE` row is never auto-retried with the wrong amount/InvoiceID — full seq-aware recovery is filed as a new Open Non-Blocking item. Income totals are unaffected (`PENDIENTE`, like `Error`, is still counted). Files: `src/recog_prendas/pay_dialog.h/.cpp`, `src/app/pendingsubmitsdialog.cpp`, `docs/modules/verifactu/README.md`.
- [x] **Reprinted factura QR now uses the submitted payment date (`fecha_pago`), not `fecha_recepcion`**: `Imprimir::resolveQrCode()` rebuilt the QR's `FechaExpedicion` from row 0's `fecha_recepcion`. For a partial payment (PayDialog submits to AEAT with `fecha_pago`) or a late pickup payment (retry submits with the payment date), payment day ≠ reception day, so a reprint produced a QR whose date — and thus the AEAT cotejo URL — did not match the registered invoice. Now reads the first paid row's `fecha_pago`, which equals the date submitted to AEAT on every path: PayDialog/retry submit the payment date, and save-time submissions store `fecha_pago = fecha_recepcion` (mainwindow binds `:fecha_pago` to `de_date_recep`), so the common counter-sale case is unchanged. Scans for the first non-empty `fecha_pago` because row 0 can be an unpaid garment in the legacy unfiltered reprint path. Files: `src/imprimir/imprimir.cpp`. (Note: the QR is regenerated via the AEAT GetQrCode service rather than rendered from a stored URL because there is no local QR encoder; storing/rendering the cotejo URL locally remains a possible future simplification.)
- [x] **Trimestral accounting lock now reads the whole quarter, not just its last month**: `Contabilidad::on_bb_ok_cancel_accepted()` decided whether a quarter was already done/locked by calling `readLockForMonthAndYear(db, "ingresos", sb_trim*3, year)` — i.e. only the quarter's third month. A quarter with income in its first month(s) but nothing paid in the last month read back as "no data" (status 2), hit the default branch ("no hay registros"), and `updateLock()` was never invoked, so the quarter stayed editable after the operator closed contabilidad — AEAT-reported income could then be altered. New `sql_lite::readLockForQuarter(db, table, quarter, year)` does one pass over the quarter's three months (`substr(dateCol,4,2) IN (m1,m2,m3) AND substr(dateCol,7,4)=year`): `COUNT(*)=0` → 2 (no data), else `MAX(edit_lock)` → 1 locked / 0 open. Used both for the lock decision and for the Annual report's per-quarter "Contabilidad cerrada/no cerrada" label (which had the same last-month-only bug). As part of the change the lock read moved inside the `Trimestral` branch (it was computed unconditionally at the top, producing a meaningless `month=sb_trim*3` up to 36 in Mensual mode) and the dialog's stay-open-on-revert decision now uses an explicit `keepDialogOpen` flag instead of re-testing the now-scoped `editLock`. Files: `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.h/.cpp`, `src/contabilidad/contabilidad.cpp`, `docs/modules/{contabilidad,sql_lite}.md`.

### Post-8.4 development — May 2026
- [x] **Pickup-all (`pb_pku_all`) scoped to the clicked ticket (closes the name-search blast-radius bug)**: `RecogPrendas::on_pb_pku_all_clicked()` used to loop `sqlQueryModel->rowCount()` and fire `updateDb(PKU_YES)` per source row. On a name search the model holds the whole `ingresos` table (`SELECT *`), so the loop marked **every** ticket in the DB as Recogido — the same blast-radius pattern that was fixed for pay-all in 8.5 via `PayDialog`. Now reads the clicked row's `n_recibo` (`isCellClicked` guard) and runs a single prepared `UPDATE ingresos SET fecha_recogida = :fr, estado = 'Recogido' WHERE n_recibo = :n` bounded to that one ticket, then refreshes via `on_pb_search_clicked()`. Pickup never carried the Verifactu duplicate-`InvoiceID` consequence (no AEAT submission), which is why it was non-blocking. Shipped in commit `9f8d217`; this entry reconciles the tracker (the item had a dangling "tracked in Open Non-Blocking" reference but the fix had already landed). Files: `src/recog_prendas/recog_prendas.cpp`, `docs/modules/recog_prendas.md`.
- [x] **`db` handle privatised + constructor-injected across all dialogs (extends the Contabilidad #9 fix)**: following the Contabilidad cleanup, the same anti-encapsulation pattern (`QSqlDatabase db;` as a public member assigned after construction, e.g. `ui_x->db = db;`) was removed from every other dialog opened from `MainWindow`. `db` is now a **private** member set through the constructor (`explicit Foo(const QSqlDatabase &database, QWidget *parent = nullptr)`), so callers write `new Foo(db, this)` instead of `new Foo(this)` + a separate `->db = db`. Classes converted: `Imprimir`, `Listado`, `RecogPrendas`, `Facturas`, `AddGarment`, `GenListado`, `InsertNewItem`, `PayDialog`, `CancelInvoiceDialog`, `RectifyInvoiceDialog`. All nested instantiations updated too (RecogPrendas→`PayDialog`/`Imprimir`, PayDialog→`Imprimir`, Listado→`InsertNewItem`/`GenListado`). Verified safe: every constructor-invoked `setupUi()`/`initialSettings()`/`buildUi()` is db-free (db is only touched in event handlers), so injecting the handle earlier is behaviour-preserving; and there were no external *reads* of another object's `db` (every prior occurrence was a `db = db` assignment), so privatising breaks nothing. In each header `db` was placed after the `ui` member so the constructor init-list order matches declaration order (no `-Wreorder`). `MainWindow::db` stays public — it is the owner that creates the connection. The other public flags/pointers flagged in audit item #9 (`qrCode`, `isRecibo`, `revertirOn`, `m_verifactu`, …) were left as-is — out of scope. Release build links clean (30/30). Files touched: headers + cpps of all ten classes above, `src/app/mainwindow.cpp`, `src/listado/listado.cpp`, `src/recog_prendas/recog_prendas.cpp`, `src/recog_prendas/pay_dialog.cpp`; docs `docs/modules/{imprimir,add_garment,recog_prendas,facturas,listado}.md`, `docs/coding_guidelines_audit.md` (item #9 marked resolved for `db`).
- [x] **Contabilidad / sql_lite review fixes (re-verified `todo.bak` findings)**: an earlier opencode review of `contabilidad` had logged 10 issues; re-checking against the post-rewrite code, two were already resolved (the malformed `<th<'>` HTML — the table builders now go through `ReportHtml::tableOpen()`; and the missing `revertirOn` guard for Mensual/Anual — `resetAllContents()` now forces `cb_config` to Trimestral and disables it in revert mode, so the unguarded branch is unreachable). The rest were fixed: **(#1 SQL injection / guideline)** `totalPriceBetweenDates`, `countOperationsBetweenDates`, `readLockForMonthAndYear` and the lock-update helper now use `prepare()` + `bindValue()` named params instead of `+` string concatenation (table/column names stay interpolated — still validated against `"ingresos"`/`"gastos"` — since SQL can't bind identifiers). **(#2 misnamed)** `updateLockInIngresos` → **`updateLockForMonth`**: the name no longer hides that it UPDATEs both `ingresos` *and* `gastos`. **(#3 leak + modality)** `Contabilidad` changed from `QMainWindow` to `QDialog` (the `.ui` lost its `centralwidget` wrapper) and now sets `WA_DeleteOnClose`, so the non-modal `new Contabilidad(...)` + `show()` instances self-delete instead of leaking until MainWindow dies. **(#6 dead code)** removed the three no-op `ui->sb_trim->minimum()` calls. **(#7 readability)** `static_cast<int>(!revertirOn)` replaced with a named `const int lockValue = revertirOn ? 0 : 1`. **(#8 hardcoded 2019)** the year-selector lower bound now comes from `readMaxNMinYearInColumnFromTable(db, false, "fecha_pago", "ingresos")` (falls back to the current year when there is no data). **(#9 public `db`)** the `db` handle is now a private member injected via the constructor (`Contabilidad(const QSqlDatabase &, QWidget *)`) instead of a public field assigned after construction. **(#10 text-coupled constants)** the `C_MENSUAL/C_TRIMESTRAL/C_ANUAL` `#define`s (compared against combobox text) replaced by a `ConfigMode { Mensual=0, Trimestral=1, Anual=2 }` enum compared via `currentMode()` (the combobox index), so renaming an item can't silently break the logic. Release build links clean. Files touched: `src/contabilidad/contabilidad.h/.cpp/.ui`, `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.h/.cpp`, `src/app/mainwindow.cpp`, `src/recog_prendas/pay_dialog.cpp` (comment), `docs/modules/contabilidad.md`, `docs/modules/sql_lite.md`, `docs/architecture.md`, `.claude/commands/coding-guidelines.md` (stale `C_TRIMESTRAL` example).
- [x] **Dead-code cleanup in `src/verifactu/` (19 unused methods removed)**: closes the Open Non-Blocking entry. Removed `VerifactuConfig::getEmitterCompanyName()`, `getSystemDeveloper()`, and the `companyName`/`developer` parameters from `setEmitterData`/`setSystemData` (the members were never read outside their own setters); thinned `VerifactuTaxItem` by 6 accessors (`setTaxType`, `setOperationType`, `setDescription`, `getTaxType`, `getTaxRate`, `getOperationType`); thinned `VerifactuInvoice` by 12 items (`setBuyerNIF/Name`, `setTotalAmount`, `setTotalTaxAmount`, `getInvoiceType`, `getDescription`, `getBuyerNIF/Name`, `getTotalTaxAmount`, `clearTaxItems`, the member `operationTypeToString` overload, `taxTypeToString`). All dead as of the async refactor that removed `createAndSubmitInvoice` — codebase is self-contained with no external linkers. Updated call sites: `verifactuintegration.cpp`, `mainwindow.cpp`. Snapshot refreshed via the `dead-code-finder` subagent. Files touched: `src/verifactu/verifactuconfig.h/.cpp`, `src/verifactu/verifactutaxitem.h`, `src/verifactu/verifactuinvoice.h/.cpp`, `src/verifactu/verifactuintegration.cpp`, `src/app/mainwindow.cpp`, `docs/dead_code_report.md`, `docs/progress_tracker.md`.
- [x] **Verifactu ServiceKey encrypted at rest (Windows DPAPI, per-user)**: closes the "ServiceKey stored in plaintext JSON" Open Non-Blocking entry. The AEAT API credential was kept as plaintext under `verifactu.service_key` in `~/.laideal_settings.json`. Now `AppSettings::setVerifactuServiceKey()` encrypts via Windows DPAPI `CryptProtectData` (scoped to the current Windows user, `CRYPTPROTECT_UI_FORBIDDEN` so no prompt) and stores `dpapi:v1:<base64>`; `verifactuServiceKey()` transparently `CryptUnprotectData`s on read, so every caller (`VerifactuIntegration`, `SettingsDialog`) still sees plaintext and nothing else changed. Chosen over a master password (the shop runs unattended at the counter) and over an app-embedded key (not real security). **Migration**: `encryptSecretsAtRest()` runs in `load()` (both the new-file and existing-file branches) and re-encrypts any value lacking the `dpapi:v1:` marker — covering both legacy plaintext already in the JSON and a key just imported from `~/.verifactu_key`; the existing-file branch then `save()`s immediately so the plaintext does not linger on disk. **Fallbacks** (key never lost): `CryptProtectData` failure stores plaintext rather than dropping the key; `CryptUnprotectData` failure (file copied to another Windows user/machine — DPAPI is user+machine bound) returns empty so "Probar conexión" fails clearly and the operator re-enters it. Non-Windows builds (none shipped) compile via a passthrough. Logs were already redacted (`verifactumanager.cpp`). Tradeoff documented: the encrypted key is **not portable** across Windows accounts/machines and must be re-entered after a reinstall or migration. Files touched: `src/appsettings/appsettings.h/.cpp`, `src/appsettings/CMakeLists.txt` (link `crypt32` on Windows). Docs: `docs/architecture.md` (AppSettings + Known Issues), `docs/INDEX.md` (glossary).
- [x] **Size-dependent garment price fixed for comma-decimal input (m² cortinas)**: closes the "Price calculation for size-dependent garments (cortinas) may be incorrect" Open Non-Blocking entry. Root cause: both `MainWindow::setGarmentPrice` and `AddGarment::setGarmentPrice` parsed the user-entered size (and quantity) with `QString::toFloat()`, which is C-locale only (dot decimal), while every **storage** site normalises with `.replace(",",".")` before binding `:size` / `:importe`. So a Spanish-entered size like `2,6` parsed as `0.0` and the size factor was silently dropped: in `MainWindow` the importe fell back to `quantity × unitPrice` (too low), and in `AddGarment` (which multiplies unconditionally when the field is non-empty, no `!= 0` guard) it became `0.0 × price = 0,00` — yet the size was stored correctly as `2.6`, so the saved/printed importe did not match the entered size. Fix: normalise both inputs with `.trimmed().replace(",", ".")` before `toFloat()` at both price-calc sites (mirroring the existing save-time convention), and switch the size test to a parsed `sizeValue != 0.0f` so non-numeric input no longer multiplies. No schema change. The DB unit price itself is out of scope (kept clean by the existing `Herramientas` comma-cleanup tool / `updateComasInDecimalData`). Files touched: `src/app/mainwindow.cpp` (`setGarmentPrice`), `src/add_garment/add_garment.cpp` (`setGarmentPrice`). Docs: `docs/modules/add_garment.md` (price-calc note).
- [x] **Investigated & discarded "clients missing from Listado but present in MainWindow combobox"**: no reproducible mechanism remains, so the entry was removed from Open Non-Blocking Issues (to be reopened only if observed again). The combobox is populated solely from `clientes.nombre` via `readColumnFromTable` (`mainwindow.cpp:216`, `SELECT nombre FROM clientes`) and `Listado` (`tableName == "clientes"`) shows the same `clientes` table via `QSqlTableModel`. `Listado::populateTable()` drains `fetchMore` for every non-`ingresos` table (`while (model->canFetchMore()) model->fetchMore()`), which deterministically loads **all** `clientes` rows — this replaced an older flaky "scroll the view to the bottom until rowCount stops growing" hack (commit b39cda7 "fix ingresos table view"). The 256-row `QSqlTableModel` lazy batch was the only plausible original cause (same family as the RecogPrendas name-search 256-row bug) and it is now force-drained. The issue's "encoding differences" hypothesis is refuted: both paths read `clientes.nombre` through the same QSQLITE driver (`SELECT nombre …` vs `SELECT * …`), so a row cannot decode into one result set and disappear from the other; the default (empty) `MySortFilterProxyModel` filter hides nothing. No code change. Files touched: docs only (`docs/progress_tracker.md`, `docs/architecture.md`).
- [x] **Report visualisation: shared professional A4 style + IVA settlement / net result / counts / annual consolidated**: closes the "Improve report visualisation" Open Non-Blocking entry for the two A4 PDF surfaces (the thermal-ticket half stays open, re-scoped, since `imprimir.cpp` is a 58 mm Excel/cscript path where the A4 redesign does not apply). **Shared styling, no drift**: the three report generators (`Contabilidad::generate*`, `GenListado::generate_html_prendas_table`, `generate_html_gastos_table`) each hand-rolled their own `<!DOCTYPE>…<style>…<body>` + business header, with an undefined `text-small` CSS class, a hardcoded "Granada", and malformed tags (`<th<'>`, `<td colspan='6',>`). Lifted the common scaffolding into a new tiny static lib `src/reporthtml/` (`ReportHtml::documentOpen(title, subtitle)` / `documentClose()` / `formatEuro(value)`): one "clean professional" stylesheet (Arial, slate `#2c3e50` headings, soft `#b6bec7` grid, grey `#e8ecf0` header row, `#f6f7f9` zebra, bold accent total rows), a business header band (name / address / city / NIF from `verifactuNif()` / phone, issue date right-aligned) and a generation-timestamp footer. Everything stays table- and inline-style based because `QTextDocument` renders only a subset of HTML/CSS (no flexbox). Currency now prints with Spanish grouping via `QLocale(Spanish, Spain)` — `1.234,56 €` instead of `1234.56 €`. **New contabilidad figures** (all computed from existing data, no schema change): a per-period **Liquidación de IVA** block — IVA repercutido (ingresos) − IVA soportado (gastos) = **Resultado IVA**, labelled "a ingresar"/"a compensar" by sign (the modelo-303 figure previously computed by hand); **Resultado del periodo** = base ingresos − base gastos (beneficio/pérdida); the explicit period range (`Periodo: dd-MM-yyyy a dd-MM-yyyy`) and operation counts (distinct paid tickets for ingresos, invoice rows for gastos) via a new `countOperationsBetweenDates()` in `sql_lite` that mirrors the estado/date filters of `totalPriceBetweenDates`; and, for the annual report, a **Resumen anual consolidado** that sums the four quarters. Refactors that fell out: the four-way quarter/month date switch duplicated inside `getTotalIncome` is now a single `periodRange()` helper reused by the counts and the period subtitle; the figures are computed once into a `PeriodFigures` struct (with `resultadoIva()` / `resultadoPeriodo()` / `accumulate()` helpers) and shared by the ingresos table, gastos table and summary, replacing the old per-table recomputation and the dead `createHtmlHeader()` / `createHtmlTables()`. The listados keep their columns but adopt the shared header/footer/stylesheet, right-aligned euro cells, and the malformed group-total `colspan` rows are fixed. Files touched: `src/reporthtml/` (new: `reporthtml.h/.cpp`, `CMakeLists.txt`), `CMakeLists.txt` (add_subdirectory), `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.h/.cpp` (`countOperationsBetweenDates`), `src/contabilidad/contabilidad.h/.cpp` (+ `CMakeLists.txt` link), `src/listado/genlistado.cpp` (+ `CMakeLists.txt` link). Docs: `docs/modules/contabilidad.md`, `docs/INDEX.md`, `docs/architecture.md`.
- [x] **Tag-triggered release workflow — `release.ps1` pipeline reproduced in CI and fully automated**: new `.github/workflows/release.yml` (runs on push of an `X.Y` tag) reproduces every step of `releases\release.ps1` on a `windows-latest` runner — validate tag == `CMakeLists.txt` version, install Qt 6.4.3 MinGW + Ninja + Inno Setup, configure/build (CMake + Ninja, Release), stage `laideal.exe`, `windeployqt`, zip into `X.Y.zip`, compile the Inno Setup installer (`laideal_setup_X.Y.exe`), extract this version's `releases_notes.txt` section, and publish a GitHub Release with both artifacts attached + the section as the release body. So cutting a release is now just `git tag X.Y && git push origin X.Y` — no local `release.ps1` run or manual `gh release create` needed (`release.ps1` stays as the offline/local fallback). **Toolchain parity, no separate pin**: `jurplel/install-qt-action@v4` with `arch: win64_mingw` runs `aqt install-qt … --autodesktop`, which auto-downloads `MinGW-w64 11.2.0` (the exact toolchain `release.ps1` pins at `C:\Qt\Tools\mingw1120_64`) and puts it on PATH, so the build and `windeployqt` use the same compiler runtime DLLs the libraries were built against — confirmed in the run log (`Finished installation of MinGW-w64-x86_64-11.2.0-…-runtime.7z`). An initial attempt to pin it explicitly with `tools: 'tools_mingw1120'` failed (`aqt`: not a valid tool name) and was redundant, so it was removed. **Hardening added over the plain script translation** (caught during a max-effort review of the new file): (1) the `windeployqt` step now asserts the runtime DLLs were actually deployed (`libgcc_s_seh-1.dll`, `libstdc++-6.dll`, `libwinpthread-1.dll`, `Qt6Core.dll`, `platforms\qwindows.dll`) — `ci.yml` only builds the bare exe and never runs `windeployqt`, so this is the only place that guards the packaging step from shipping an installer that crashes on launch on a clean machine; (2) `Test-Path` existence checks after build (exe) and after Inno (installer), mirroring `release.ps1`; (3) the release-notes extraction now **throws** when `releases_notes.txt` has no section for the tag, or the section is empty, instead of silently publishing a release with a blank body that the in-app updater would then show users; (4) the publish step is idempotent — if a release already exists for the tag (re-run after a transient failure, or a moved tag) it is deleted first (the git tag is kept) so `gh release create` always succeeds. The tag glob `'[0-9]+.[0-9]+'` is valid GitHub filter syntax (`+` = one-or-more), the bare `X.Y` tag (no `v`/`r` prefix) matches `Updater::currentVersion()` / `tag_name`, and the `laideal_setup_X.Y.exe` asset name matches the updater's `kInstallerAssetPrefix` — so the auto-published release is consumable by the in-app updater unchanged. **Validated end-to-end** via a throwaway `0.1` tag (on a temp commit bumping `CMakeLists.txt`/notes to `0.1`, never merged): the full pipeline ran green in 3m38s and published `0.1.zip` (22 MB) + `laideal_setup_0.1.exe` (17.6 MB) with the correct notes body; `0.1` chosen because the updater is downgrade-safe (`0.1 ≤ 8.4` → no update offered) so no user was affected during the brief window it was `/releases/latest`. Test release + tag deleted afterwards, restoring `8.4` as Latest. Files touched: `.github/workflows/release.yml`. Docs: root `README.md` (Development workflow step 6 + Release procedure now lead with the tag-push automation, `release.ps1` documented as the local fallback), `docs/INDEX.md` (CI/CD workflow files), `docs/ai_agent_instructions.md` (release-pipeline note).
- [x] **GitHub Actions CI/CD pipeline**: new `.github/workflows/ci.yml` — Windows runner that builds on every push/PR (Qt 6.4.3 MinGW via `jurplel/install-qt-action` + CMake + Ninja), with a test placeholder and artifact upload of `laideal.exe` on push branches. Closes the Open Non-Blocking entry. Files touched: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`.

- [x] **`CancelInvoiceDialog` per-seq cancellation**: closes the Open Non-Blocking entry of the same name and removes the 8.5 partial-pay guard that previously blocked the dialog whenever any row of the searched ticket carried `verifactu_invoice_seq > 0`. The dialog now lists every `(n_recibo, verifactu_invoice_seq)` AEAT submission as one row in a `QTableWidget` (InvoiceID, importe, estado, CSV, per-row "Anular" button). Single-event legacy tickets render the same uniform table with one row (seq=0) and behave identically to before. **AEAT side**: each cancel sends the literal `verifactu_invoice_id` stored at submit time — when that column is empty (8.0-8.4 rows or pre-Phase-G PayDialog events) the dialog reconstructs the same string AEAT would have under (seq=0 → bare `n_recibo`, seq>0 → `<n>-<seq>`). **Local side**: the post-success UPDATE is now scoped `WHERE n_recibo=:n AND verifactu_invoice_seq=:seq`, so the other payment events of the same ticket stay ENVIADA. Only events whose `verifactu_estado=ENVIADA` are cancellable; ANULADA / RECTIFICADA / ERROR / PENDIENTE rows show the button greyed out. Single in-flight cancellation is enforced — the dialog disables all action buttons while a request is pending and re-enables them on reply. The header (cliente + fecha_recepcion) is rendered once above the table since it is invariant per ticket. No schema change. Public surface of the dialog (`db` + `m_verifactu` + `exec()`) is unchanged; `MainWindow::on_actionAnular_factura_verifactu_triggered` keeps working as-is. Files touched: `src/app/cancelinvoicedialog.h/.cpp`.
- [x] **RecogPrendas "Recoger todo" blast-radius bug closed**: closes the Open Non-Blocking entry of the same name. The legacy `on_pb_pku_all_clicked` iterated `sqlQueryModel->rowCount()` source rows and called `updateDb(PKU_YES)` per row. On a name-search (`SELECT * FROM ingresos`) the source model held the entire table, so pressing "Recoger todo" marked every ticket in the database `estado=Recogido` + `fecha_recogida=today` — same blast-radius pattern that was fixed for `pay_all` in 8.5 via PayDialog. Closed here with the same scope strategy but a much smaller change since pickup has no per-garment choice / AEAT submit / print: read the clicked row's `n_recibo` from the source model (no proxy mapping needed — `rowClickedCell` is already a source row thanks to the 8.4 coord-space fix) and run **one** `UPDATE ingresos SET fecha_recogida=:fr, estado='Recogido' WHERE n_recibo=:n` then re-fire the search to refresh the view. No new dialog, no schema change. `fecha_recogida` is bound from `ui->de_date_pickup->date()` to match the legacy semantics where the operator could pick a different pickup date in the form. Files touched: `src/recog_prendas/recog_prendas.cpp`.
- [x] **`hash` column: UUID-based generator + maintenance action regenerates legacy duplicates instead of just listing them**: closes the Open Non-Blocking entry "Manual `hash` column — review necessity, fix duplicates". **Why the dialog was reporting "wrong" duplicates**: the legacy `SELECT n_recibo, COUNT(*) FROM ingresos GROUP BY hash HAVING COUNT(*) > 1` returned ONE arbitrary `n_recibo` per duplicate group, so when ticket 21484 shared a hash with ticket 17510 the operator saw `21484` listed but checking 21484's own rows showed unique hashes within that ticket — the collision was cross-ticket, not within-ticket; ticket numbers appearing twice in the list (`21484, 21484`) just meant that ticket participated in two distinct collision groups. The collisions themselves are legacy data from the older `rand()`-era `genHash16()`. **The hash IS still required**: it is the per-row addressing key used by `PayDialog::persistPayment` (WHERE n_recibo=:n AND hash=:h) and seven RecogPrendas operations (Entregar / Separar / Pagar / etc.), plus every INSERT site, so dropping it in favour of SQLite's `rowid` would be a wider refactor than this task warranted. **Changes**: (1) `genHash16()` now returns `QUuid::createUuid().toString(QUuid::Id128).left(16)` — 16 hex chars from a cryptographic UUID, ~2^64 entropy, no realistic birthday-collision and no manual retry loop; (2) `on_actionCrear_hash_en_ingresos_triggered()` rewritten as three phases — **A** fills `hash IS NULL OR hash = ''` rows keyed by `rowid` (not the legacy all-fields equality, which mass-stamped identical garment rows with one hash); **B** detects every collision group `GROUP BY hash HAVING COUNT(*) > 1`, keeps the first row's hash (ordered by `rowid`), regenerates the rest via `UPDATE ... WHERE rowid = :rid`; **C** defensive re-check, surfaces remaining groups in a warning if Phase B didn't converge. Dialog now reports actionable counts: filled / collision groups / rows regenerated, instead of the opaque ticket list. Idempotent — a clean DB returns `0 / 0 / 0`. The action is kept as an operator escape hatch even though the schema is now self-healing. Files touched: `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.cpp` (`genHash16` body + swap `<QRandomGenerator>` for `<QUuid>`), `src/app/mainwindow.cpp` (`on_actionCrear_hash_en_ingresos_triggered` rewrite).
- [x] **Pending Verifactu submissions across app restarts: operator-surfaced recovery dialog**: closes the Open Non-Blocking entry of the same name. Until now `MainWindow::m_pendingSubmits` (reqId → ticketNum) lived only in RAM, so an app close or crash mid-submission left the row at `verifactu_estado='PENDIENTE'` with no recovery path — and a naive auto-resubmit risks AEAT duplicate-InvoiceID if the original POST had actually reached AEAT before the reply was lost. **Resolution path picked**: operator-surfaced recovery rather than auto-reconcile (the alternative, auto-reconcile via lookup, was rejected because the Irene Solutions Kivu API has no confirmed idempotent lookup-by-InvoiceID endpoint that returns the AEAT-side CSV). New module `src/app/pendingsubmitsdialog.{h,cpp}` — a modal `QDialog` opened from `MainWindow`'s startup via `QTimer::singleShot(4000)` so it lands after the updater + backup prompts have settled. `loadPending()` SELECTs distinct `n_recibo` with at least one row whose `verifactu_estado` is NULL/empty/`PENDIENTE`, aggregates `fecha_recepcion` / `cliente` / SUM(`importe`) per ticket, and returns false (so the dialog never opens) when there is nothing pending. **Two gates protect against surfacing pre-Verifactu legacy tickets**: (1) operator toggle `verifactu.pending_recovery_enabled` (default true) — when off, `loadPending()` returns false unconditionally; (2) floor date `verifactu.pending_recovery_floor_date` (ISO `yyyy-MM-dd`, default `2026-09-01` matching the planned PROD cutover) — added to the SQL as `substr(fecha_recepcion,7,4)||'-'||substr(fecha_recepcion,4,2)||'-'||substr(fecha_recepcion,1,2) >= :floor` since the DB stores dd-MM-yyyy and the substr-rebuild gives a lexicographically-comparable ISO key. Both settings are user-configurable in the Verifactu tab of `SettingsDialog` (`QCheckBox` + `QDateEdit` with `dd-MM-yyyy` display, calendar popup, persisted as ISO in `~/.laideal_settings.json`). Each row shows three per-row buttons: **Reintentar** emits `retryRequested(ticketNum, date, total)` → `MainWindow::verifactuSubmitInvoice(...)` which already registers the new `reqId` in `m_pendingSubmits` so `onVerifactuRequestFinished` patches the row when AEAT replies; **Marcar como error** runs a single UPDATE setting `verifactu_estado='Error'` + `verifactu_error='Pendiente sin reconciliar tras cierre - revisar en sede AEAT'` (no AEAT call, no duplicate-InvoiceID risk); **Posponer** just drops the row from the table — `verifactu_estado` stays PENDIENTE and the dialog will resurface it on the next launch. Files touched: `src/app/pendingsubmitsdialog.h/.cpp` (new), `src/app/CMakeLists.txt` (add to executable sources), `src/app/mainwindow.cpp` (`#include` + deferred-launch hook + `retryRequested` connect → `verifactuSubmitInvoice`), `src/appsettings/appsettings.h/.cpp` (two new accessors + defaults), `src/appsettings/settingsdialog.h/.cpp` (two new controls under the Verifactu tab). Docs: `docs/architecture.md` (new module box under MainWindow's children), `docs/INDEX.md` (new file entries).
- [x] **Drop the legacy `VerifactuConfig` INI cache**: closes the Open Non-Blocking entry of the same name. Pre-8.5 builds wrote a `QSettings` INI copy of NIF/name/serviceKey/environment to `<AppDataLocation>/verifactu_config.ini` on every Verifactu submit, but `VerifactuIntegration::loadEmitterConfiguration()` always overwrote the in-memory state from `~/.laideal_settings.json` (`AppSettings`) at startup, so the INI was never read back. The file was therefore a stale plaintext copy of the same credentials already kept in the JSON — a credential-footprint smell with no functional purpose. **Changes**: `VerifactuConfig` is now a pure in-memory holder. Dropped `save()`, `load()`, the `m_settings` member, the `<QSettings>` include, and the `configPath` constructor parameter. Constructor performs a one-time delete of the legacy INI at `<AppDataLocation>/verifactu_config.ini` if it exists, so existing installs are cleaned up the next time the app starts (idempotent — once gone, the file-exists check no-ops). `VerifactuManager` ctor signature loses its `configPath` arg; the sole caller in `VerifactuIntegration::initialize()` updated. The lingering `m_manager->getConfig()->save();` call in `loadEmitterConfiguration()` is gone since there is no more persistent target. Net: zero credential bytes written by Verifactu code outside of `~/.laideal_settings.json`. Side-benefit: removes the `VerifactuConfig::load()` row called out as dead in the open "Dead-code cleanup in `src/verifactu/`" tracker entry. Files touched: `src/verifactu/verifactuconfig.h/.cpp`, `src/verifactu/verifactumanager.h/.cpp`, `src/verifactu/verifactuintegration.cpp`. Docs: this entry; `docs/modules/verifactu/README.md` Internal note removed.

New `verifactutaxitem.h` and `verifactutaxitem.cpp` carry only the `VerifactuTaxItem` class; `verifactuinvoice.h` now declares just `VerifactuInvoice` and `#include`s the new header at the top so existing transitive consumers (`verifactumanager.{h,cpp}`, `verifactuintegration.{h,cpp}`, `mainwindow.cpp`, `rectifyinvoicedialog.cpp`, `pay_dialog.cpp`, `sql_lite.cpp`) keep working without edits. `verifactuinvoice.cpp` likewise slimmed to `VerifactuInvoice`-only implementation; the `#include <cmath>` and the tax-item-specific `qDebug` include moved to the new `verifactutaxitem.cpp`. `src/verifactu/CMakeLists.txt` adds the two new files to `PROJECT_SOURCES` (alphabetical order: taxitem before invoice, mirroring header dependency). No public API change, no behaviour change. Same one-class-per-header direction as the 8.4 split of `verifactutypes.h` out of `verifactumanager.h`. - [x] **Verifactu Req. 5: close per-user trazabilidad (Art. 8.1) by stating the monoperador scope in the declaración responsable**: removes the only remaining PARTIAL row from `docs/modules/verifactu/verifactu-requirements.md` (Req. 5) and the matching entry from Open Non-Blocking Issues. The issue text offered two closures — full per-operator-stamping (new `operador TEXT` column on `ingresos`, login prompt at startup, stamp on each write) vs. single-operator declaration — and we picked the second because La Ideal is run by a single clerk and the regulator accepts "implicit identification by Windows session" when the scope is documented. Added a new paragraph to the `Acerca de Verifactu...` dialog body in `mainwindow.cpp` declaring (a) the system is used in a monoperador installation, (b) the single operator is identified implicitly by the Windows session, (c) under that scope Art. 8.1 is satisfied, and (d) incorporating a second clerk requires enabling per-event identification first — so the operator is aware that hiring re-opens the requirement. `verifactu-requirements.md` §5 flipped PARTIAL → COVERED with the new wording; §8 cross-references the monoperador clause; the audit table at the bottom now shows all ten requirements COVERED. No schema change, no UI prompt, no new module. Files touched: `src/app/mainwindow.cpp` (HTML body of the declaración), `docs/modules/verifactu/verifactu-requirements.md`.
- [x] **Verifactu Req. 4: automated SQLite backup + 4-year retention + in-app manual trigger**: closes the Open Non-Blocking entry "Verifactu Req. 4 — automated SQLite backup + 4-year retention (Art. 8.2.c RD 1007/2023)" and flips that requirement from PARTIAL to COVERED in `docs/modules/verifactu/verifactu-requirements.md`. New module `src/backup/backup_manager.{h,cpp}` exposes `BackupManager::performBackup()` (synchronous), `needsBackup(minIntervalSeconds=24*3600)`, and `pruneOldBackups()`. The snapshot uses SQLite `VACUUM INTO 'target'` on a dedicated connection (`laideal_backup_snapshot`), which produces a transactionally-consistent point-in-time copy without taking exclusive access on the live DB — so the operator's current session is not interrupted. Each snapshot is then re-opened read-only on a second dedicated connection (`laideal_backup_verify`) and `PRAGMA integrity_check` must return `ok` before the file is counted toward the retention window; a corrupt copy is deleted on the spot. Filenames follow `laideal_yyyy-MM-dd_HHmmss.db` (same `.db` extension as the live DB so the operator manages a uniform set of files) and sort lexicographically by time, which keeps the pruner trivial. Backups live in a `backups/` subdir next to the live DB — derived from `QFileInfo(dbPath()).absolutePath() + "/backups"` — so the operator manages one location and the snapshots stay isolated from the live file. Retention policy: keep every backup from the last 30 days, then one per calendar month for 4 years, then delete (matches the tax prescription period in Ley 58/2003 LGT). **AppSettings** gains `backup.enabled` (default true) and `backup.last_time` (ISO timestamp persisted after each success); the directory is *not* a separate setting — it follows `dbPath()`. **MainWindow** instantiates `m_backupManager` in the constructor; if `needsBackup()` is true it schedules `performBackup()` via `QTimer::singleShot(3000)` so the snapshot runs after the first paint and doesn't delay the UI. The auto path is silent on success (status-bar 8 s message) and shows a `QMessageBox::warning` only on failure — since the regulatory requirement is durable storage, a failed snapshot is worth surfacing. **Herramientas → Hacer copia de seguridad ahora...** is a new `QAction` in the menubar (`actionHacer_copia_de_seguridad`) that runs the same `performBackup()` under a `WaitCursor` and reports the resulting path + size in a `QMessageBox` so the operator can grab the file for offsite copies. No installer / Task Scheduler changes were needed: the shop opens the app daily, so an in-app daily backup covers the prescription window equivalently, and the manual trigger handles ad-hoc offsite workflows. Files touched: `src/backup/backup_manager.h/.cpp` (new), `src/backup/CMakeLists.txt` (new), `CMakeLists.txt` (add_subdirectory), `src/app/CMakeLists.txt` (link `backup`), `src/appsettings/appsettings.h/.cpp` (new backup keys + default opt-in), `src/app/mainwindow.h/.cpp` (include + member + startup hook + slot), `src/app/mainwindow.ui` (Herramientas menu entry + action). Docs: `docs/architecture.md` (new module box), `docs/INDEX.md` (new files), `docs/modules/backup.md` (new module reference), `docs/modules/verifactu/verifactu-requirements.md` (Req. 4 → COVERED).
- [x] **Partial-payment dialog (PayDialog) — operator picks a subset of garments and Verifactu submits one invoice per payment event**: closes the long-standing tracker entry "Per-payment Verifactu InvoiceID seq (restore per-garment payment)" and the 8.5 Blocking Issue for "Pagar todo on name search marks every ticket paid". v8.0 disabled per-garment payment as a workaround for AEAT duplicate-InvoiceID rejection; v8.5 restores it with the proper mechanism. **Schema**: `migrateDatabase()` adds `verifactu_invoice_seq INTEGER DEFAULT 0` to `ingresos`. Constant `INGRESOS_COL_VERIFACTU_INVOICE_SEQ = 24` in `ingresos_schema.h`. Legacy rows (8.0-8.4) keep seq=0, matching the bare-InvoiceID convention they were submitted under. **sql_lite helpers**: `nextVerifactuInvoiceSeq(db, ticketNum)` returns `MAX(verifactu_invoice_seq)+1` across rows of that ticket that have a non-empty `verifactu_estado` (so it skips never-submitted rows), and `updateTicketVerifactuFieldsForSeq(db, ticketNum, seq, result)` patches AEAT metadata scoped by `(n_recibo, seq)` instead of `n_recibo` alone. **Imprimir** gains an optional `int invoiceSeq = -1` member; when `>=0`, `getTicketInfo()` filters `WHERE n_recibo=? AND verifactu_invoice_seq=?`, the factura header prints `Nº: <n_recibo>-<seq>`, and the reprint QR path uses the same seq'd InvoiceID for `generateQRAsync` — so printed receipt and AEAT-side invoice are always aligned. **PayDialog** (`src/recog_prendas/pay_dialog.{h,cpp}`) is a modal `QDialog` opened from `RecogPrendas::pb_pay_all` with the clicked ticket pre-loaded: every unpaid row of that `n_recibo` appears as a `QTableWidget` line with a per-row checkbox pre-checked. Live "Total seleccionado (N prendas)" label updates as boxes are unticked. "Cobrar" reads `nextVerifactuInvoiceSeq`, builds `InvoiceID = "<n_recibo>-<seq>"`, fires `submitSimplifiedInvoiceAsync` with a 5 s bounded wait via `QEventLoop` + `QTimer::singleShot`, UPDATEs the chosen rows `pagado='SI', fecha_pago=:date, verifactu_invoice_seq=:seq`, calls `updateTicketVerifactuFieldsForSeq` for the AEAT metadata on the matched rows, and prints the partial factura via `Imprimir` with `invoiceSeq` set so only the paid subset is on the print. AEAT-not-configured and 5 s-timeout paths still persist the payment locally and print a paid recibo (no QR). **RecogPrendas** `on_pb_pay_all_clicked` was rewritten: instead of looping `sqlQueryModel->rowCount()` source rows and pressing `on_pb_payment_toggled(true)` per row (the v8.5 blocking bug: on name-search the source model held the full table, so the loop marked every ticket in the DB paid), it now takes the clicked row's `n_recibo`, opens `PayDialog`, waits for it to close, and re-runs the search. `pb_print` was updated to read the clicked row's `verifactu_invoice_seq` and forward it to `Imprimir` via `printFactura(ticketNum, askSecondCopy, invoiceSeq)` so a reprint covers exactly that payment event's rows. **CancelInvoiceDialog** gets a guard: if the queried ticket has any row with `verifactu_invoice_seq > 0`, the dialog blocks the cancel and tells the operator to use the AEAT sede electrónica — its current `UPDATE WHERE n_recibo = X` would mark every payment event's rows ANULADA in one shot and lose the per-seq alignment, so full per-seq cancellation is deferred to 8.6 (filed in Open Non-Blocking Issues). `pku_all` left unchanged - same blast-radius pattern but no Verifactu corruption pressure, also filed in Open Non-Blocking. Files touched: `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.h/.cpp`, `src/sql_lite/ingresos_schema.h`, `src/imprimir/imprimir.h/.cpp`, `src/recog_prendas/pay_dialog.h/.cpp` (new), `src/recog_prendas/CMakeLists.txt`, `src/recog_prendas/recog_prendas.h/.cpp`, `src/listado/listado.cpp` (hide the new column), `src/app/cancelinvoicedialog.cpp`. Six commits in sequence (Phase A→F): f8ebbce, b964c60, 6430528, da0cef7, 7a64e2c, plus this docs commit.

### 8.4 release — May 2026
- [x] **Split `verifactutypes.h` out of `verifactumanager.h`**: the previous milestone moved `updateTicketVerifactuFields()` into `sql_lite.cpp` and added `#include "../verifactu/verifactumanager.h"` to bring in `VerifactuResult` + `VerifactuEstado` + the helpers. That header transitively pulls `<QNetworkAccessManager>` (because it declares the REST client), so the `sql_lite` static lib - which links only Core / Gui / Sql, not Network - failed to compile (`fatal error: QNetworkAccessManager: No such file or directory`). Lifted the lightweight types (the `VerifactuEstado` enum, `verifactuEstadoToString/FromString`, the `VerifactuResult` POD) into a new `verifactu/verifactutypes.h` that depends only on `<QString>` + `<QPixmap>`. `verifactumanager.h` now includes that header instead of carrying the types; `sql_lite.cpp` includes the small header so it never sees Network. Same one-class-per-header direction as the open Tier-1 task for `verifactuinvoice.h`. Files touched: `src/verifactu/verifactutypes.h` (new), `src/verifactu/verifactumanager.h`, `src/verifactu/CMakeLists.txt`, `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.cpp`.
- [x] **`RecogPrendas`: fix latent proxy/source row-coord bug under header sort + set `table_name` so date / `importe` sort correctly**: `recog_prendas.ui` already had `sortingEnabled=true` on the table view and `on_pb_search_clicked` already called `sortByColumn(0, DescendingOrder)`, so header-click sorting was nominally working - but two flaws made it silently wrong. **(1) Coordinate-space bug**: `on_tableView_clicked(const QModelIndex &index)` received a proxy-space index from the view, stored `index.row()` as `rowClickedCell`, and the rest of the class then consumed that value as a *source-model* row via `sqlQueryModel->index(rowClickedCell, ...)`. Whenever the desc-by-n_recibo default sort actually reordered rows (i.e. searches that span multiple tickets - typically name search), every click that wasn't on the first visible row hit the wrong DB record - so `pb_state` (Entregar) could mark the wrong ticket as picked up, `pb_separ_garm` could split the wrong garment, etc. Same-ticket searches were immune because all rows share the same n_recibo so the sort was a no-op. Factored row storage into a new private helper `selectSourceRow(int sourceRow, int sourceCol)`; `on_tableView_clicked` now maps via `proxyModel->mapToSource(index)` before delegating. The pay-all / pku-all loops, which previously fake-called `on_tableView_clicked` with raw `sqlQueryModel->index(row, 0)` source indices (an existing layering oddity that happened to work because of the bug above), now call `selectSourceRow(row, 0)` directly - same outcome, less magic. **(2) `table_name` was never set on the proxy**: `MySortFilterProxyModel::lessThan()` keys on `table_name` (`"ingresos"` / `"gastos"` / `"prendas"`) to pick the date or numeric comparator; `Listado` sets it but `RecogPrendas` never did, so clicking a date column header sorted lexicographically (`"31-01-2026" < "02-02-2026"` reverses by day, not chronology) and `importe` sorted as strings (`"100" < "9"`). Added `proxyModel->table_name = "ingresos";` right after the proxy is constructed. **Defensive**: `MySortFilterProxyModel *proxyModel` was an uninitialised pointer member; changed to `= nullptr` so the `proxyModel ? mapToSource : index` guard in the click handler is safe before the first search. The `sortByColumn(0, ...)` call now uses `INGRESOS_COL_N_RECIBO` instead of a bare literal. Files touched: `src/recog_prendas/recog_prendas.h/.cpp`.
- [x] **Consolidate `ingresos` column-index constants into `sql_lite/ingresos_schema.h`**: three parallel macro sets indexed the same 24 columns of `ingresos`: `INGRESOS_IDX_*` in `mysortfilterproxymodel.h` (used by `mysortfilterproxymodel.cpp`, `mainwindow.cpp`, `listado.cpp` — half-Spanish names ID/IMPORTE/PAYED/CANTIDAD/PRENDA/SERVICIO/OBSV), `TABLE_*` in `imprimir.h` (used by `imprimir.cpp`, `add_garment.cpp` — English-ish names TICKET/PRICE/IS_PAYED/QUANTITY/GARMENT/SERVICE/OBSERV), and a near-duplicate `TABLE_*` in `recog_prendas.h` (with `TIMESTAMP` instead of `TS` and a comment leaning on imprimir.h for `TABLE_HASH`). The previous Verifactu milestones in the tracker explicitly note new columns being added to all three blocks for each schema change — exactly the drift hazard that produced the v8.0 `verifactu_xml`/`verifactu_hash` silent-drop bug. Lifted into a single canonical set in a new tiny header `sql_lite/ingresos_schema.h` (no QtSql dep, so light consumers like `tableview` can include it without picking up the SQL link): `INGRESOS_COL_<UPPER_DB_COLUMN_NAME>` (`INGRESOS_COL_N_RECIBO=0` ... `INGRESOS_COL_VERIFACTU_RECTIFICATION_TYPE=23`). `sql_lite.h` re-includes it so existing SQL consumers keep working transparently. The name = uppercase SQL column avoids the English-vs-Spanish bikeshed (DB names are what they are) and makes a grep for any column hit both the schema and the C++ constant. All ~80 call sites renamed (mainwindow.cpp, listado.cpp, mysortfilterproxymodel.cpp, imprimir.cpp, recog_prendas.cpp, add_garment.cpp). Headers (`mysortfilterproxymodel.h`, `imprimir.h`, `recog_prendas.h`) gain a one-line breadcrumb pointing at `sql_lite.h` so future readers know where the constants live. `TABLE_TICKET_*` in `mainwindow.h` was **not** touched — those index the in-memory `QTableWidget` used for ticket entry, a distinct concept from the `ingresos` DB row layout. The mechanical rename was driven by a regex script with `\b` boundaries so `TABLE_TICKET_QNTY` etc. were correctly skipped. Docs updated: `docs/modules/imprimir.md`, `docs/modules/recog_prendas.md` (column-index tables rewritten to point at `INGRESOS_COL_*`). Files touched: `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.h`, `src/tableview/mysortfilterproxymodel.h`, `src/imprimir/imprimir.h`, `src/recog_prendas/recog_prendas.h`, `src/app/mainwindow.cpp`, `src/listado/listado.cpp`, `src/tableview/mysortfilterproxymodel.cpp`, `src/imprimir/imprimir.cpp`, `src/recog_prendas/recog_prendas.cpp`, `src/add_garment/add_garment.cpp`, `docs/modules/imprimir.md`, `docs/modules/recog_prendas.md`.
- [x] **Consolidate `updateTicketVerifactuFields()` into `sql_lite`**: `MainWindow::updateTicketVerifactuFields()` and `RecogPrendas::updateTicketVerifactuFields()` were byte-identical (same UPDATE on `ingresos`, same bindings, same logging shape) — exactly the drift hazard flagged in the previous milestone where the RecogPrendas copy missed the xml/hash columns for weeks. Lifted into a single free function `updateTicketVerifactuFields(QSqlDatabase &db, const QString &ticketNum, const VerifactuResult &result)` in `sql_lite.h/.cpp`, alongside the other ingresos-touching helpers (`updateLockInIngresos`, `insertNewItemToTable`, etc.). Both call sites now invoke the free function with their member `db`. `sql_lite.h` forward-declares `VerifactuResult` so consumers do not need to include `verifactumanager.h` transitively; the implementation includes it. The rectifyinvoicedialog helper `applyRectificationResult()` was intentionally **not** folded in — its WHERE clause has the extra `AND verifactu_rectifies_n_recibo = :orig` guard and a different success-state mapping, so it is a sibling helper, not a duplicate. `docs/architecture.md` data-flow box updated to point at `sql_lite::updateTicketVerifactuFields`. Files touched: `src/sql_lite/sql_lite.h/.cpp`, `src/app/mainwindow.h/.cpp`, `src/recog_prendas/recog_prendas.h/.cpp`, `docs/architecture.md`.
- [x] **Save-time print: bounded 3 s wait for AEAT so the factura carries the QR when possible, otherwise degrade to a paid recibo**: previously the `isPaid` branch of `on_bb_save_reset_clicked` always called `printFra()` immediately after firing the async `verifactuSubmitInvoice()`, so the printed factura simplificada always went out without QR / CSV - the AEAT reply was still in flight and the DB row was still PENDIENTE. The Verifactu legend gated on `verifactu_estado = ENVIADA` (line 415 of `imprimir.cpp`) was likewise never printed at save time. New flow: `verifactuSubmitInvoice` now returns the `reqId` (was `void`); the save handler opens a local `QEventLoop` with a 3 s `QTimer::singleShot` timeout and listens on `VerifactuIntegration::requestFinished` filtered by that `reqId`. The existing `onVerifactuRequestFinished` slot fires first (FIFO connect order) and UPDATEs the row to `ENVIADA` / `ERROR` + CSV / Huella before the local lambda runs, so by the time the lambda quits the loop the DB is consistent. If the reply arrived AND `result.isSuccess() && !result.qrCode.isNull()`, we call the new `printFra(const QPixmap &qrCode = QPixmap())` overload - it forwards the pixmap into `Imprimir::qrCode` so `resolveQrCode()` short-circuits on its `if (!qrCode.isNull()) return qrCode` early-out, skipping a second `generateQRAsync` round-trip. Otherwise (timeout, success-without-QR, or error) we call `printRecibo()`, which - because the ticket is paid - already stamps "Importe pagado" on the customer copy via `createTicketExcel(true, ui->pb_payment->isChecked())`. The async submit is still alive in `m_pendingSubmits` and `onVerifactuRequestFinished` will eventually patch the row even if it missed the 3 s window; the customer can reprint a Verifactu-complete copy via RecogPrendas at pickup time once `ENVIADA` lands. Picked 3 s because the median Irene Solutions round-trip from local logs is around 1-2 s; longer waits start to feel laggy at the counter. Files touched: `src/app/mainwindow.h/.cpp` - new `+#include <QEventLoop>`, `verifactuSubmitInvoice` signature change (`void` → `QString`), `printFra` gains a default `QPixmap` parameter, save handler rewritten with the bounded wait. `docs/architecture.md` Data Flow updated.

### Post-8.2 development — May 2026
- [x] **Ayuda → Notas de la versión: in-app version history viewer**: complements the updater dialog (which only shows the *new* version's body during an upgrade) by letting the user browse the full history of what shipped at any time, offline. `releases_notes.txt` is now bundled into the exe as a Qt resource (`resources/laideal.qrc`, alias `:/docs/releases_notes.txt`) — the same file Inno Setup already shows at install time (`InfoBeforeFile=..\releases_notes.txt`) and that `gh release create --notes-file` reuses for the GitHub release body, so all three surfaces are guaranteed to stay in sync without any deploy-time copy step. New menu entry in `Ayuda` between "Buscar actualizaciones..." and the separator. Handler opens a small modal `QDialog` with a read-only `QTextEdit` in the system fixed font (so the tab-indented release notes layout is preserved), sized 680×560, with cursor anchored at the top so the newest release is what the user sees first. No network involved — the dialog works on offline machines. Files touched: `resources/laideal.qrc` (new `/docs` prefix), `src/app/mainwindow.ui` (new `actionNotas_de_la_version` + menu wiring), `src/app/mainwindow.h/.cpp` (new slot, +4 includes).
- [x] **In-app updater (Ayuda → Buscar actualizaciones + startup check)**: end-user upgrade no longer requires manually downloading the installer from GitHub and uninstalling the prior version. New `src/updater/` module with two classes: `Updater` (async `QNetworkAccessManager` client against `https://api.github.com/repos/germanbravolopez/laideal/releases/latest`, compares the returned `tag_name` to the compile-time `PROJECT_VERSION` macro via the same `target_compile_definitions(... PROJECT_VERSION="${PROJECT_VERSION}")` trick the Verifactu module already uses, picks the `laideal_setup_X.Y.exe` asset out of `assets[]`, and emits `updateAvailable / noUpdateAvailable / checkFailed`) and `UpdaterDialog` (shows current vs. new version plus the GitHub release body, on "Actualizar ahora" collapses to a progress bar, downloads the .exe to `%TEMP%` via the same `Updater`, then `QProcess::startDetached(installer, {}); qApp->quit();` so Inno Setup can replace the running files). The dialog is modal but the network is fully async so the UI never blocks. `MainWindow` ctor instantiates one `Updater`, wires the three signals, and — gated on `AppSettings::checkUpdatesOnStartup()` (default `true`) — fires `checkForUpdates(silentOnNoUpdate=true)` via `QTimer::singleShot(1500, ...)` so the main window is fully shown first. Menu action `Ayuda → Buscar actualizaciones...` uses `silentOnNoUpdate=false` so the user always sees an outcome. AppSettings gained `checkUpdatesOnStartup() / setCheckUpdatesOnStartup(bool)` reading/writing `updater.check_on_startup` in `~/.laideal_settings.json`, and `SettingsDialog`'s General tab gained a "Buscar actualizaciones al iniciar la aplicación" checkbox. Inno Setup (`releases/laideal.iss`) declares `CloseApplications=yes`, `RestartApplications=yes`, `CloseApplicationsFilter=*.exe` so a running app is auto-closed and relaunched (the updater's own `qApp->quit()` usually wins the race; the Inno flags are the safety net). The repo was made **public** so unauthenticated GitHub API calls resolve `/releases/latest` (private repos return 404). Files touched: `src/updater/` (new dir, 5 files: `CMakeLists.txt`, `updater.h/.cpp`, `updaterdialog.h/.cpp`), `CMakeLists.txt` (add_subdirectory), `src/app/CMakeLists.txt` (link `updater`), `src/app/mainwindow.h/.cpp` (new fields + slots + startup-check timer), `src/app/mainwindow.ui` (Ayuda menu entry + new QAction), `src/appsettings/appsettings.h/.cpp` (new key + default seeding in `applyDefaults`), `src/appsettings/settingsdialog.h/.cpp` (checkbox on General tab), `releases/laideal.iss` (3 new keys). Limits: only checks `releases/latest` (no pre-release / multi-version paths); the GitHub release body is shown verbatim as plain text (no markdown rendering); if the installer fails to launch (`QProcess::startDetached` returns false) the dialog surfaces the local path so the user can run it by hand; download timeout uses Qt's default (no explicit deadline). Known UX caveat: an in-place upgrade keeps the user's `~/.laideal_settings.json`, `~/.laideal.log` and the SQLite database (all under `%USERPROFILE%`, outside Inno's `{app}` scope) — no migration risk for end-user data.
- [x] **Repo switched from private to public** (`gh repo edit --visibility public`): required for the in-app updater above — the GitHub Releases REST API returns 404 to anonymous callers on private repos, so the updater could not see `/releases/latest`. Pre-flight grep confirmed no NIF / ServiceKey / .env / .key / .cfg files were ever committed; the "secret"-looking matches in the tree were source-code variable names and dialog labels, never literal values. The Verifactu ServiceKey lives only in `~/.laideal_settings.json` on the customer machine.

### Post-8.0 maintenance — May 2026
- [x] **`RecogPrendas` "Pagar todo" now auto-prints the factura and offers a second copy; print no longer closes the window**: previously the operator had to click "Pagar todo" (which marks every row of the ticket as paid and fires the async Verifactu submission), then separately click the print button to send the factura to the thermal printer. The print button also unconditionally closed the RecogPrendas window, which made it awkward to print a second copy for the establishment - the operator had to reopen RecogPrendas, search the ticket again and reprint. New flow: at the end of `on_pb_pay_all_clicked()`, the ticket number is captured **before** `resetAllContents()` clears `le_nr_ticket` (when no row was originally clicked the loop leaves it on the last paid row), and the factura is generated + sent to the printer via a new private helper `printFactura(ticketNum, askSecondCopy)`. When `askSecondCopy=true`, the helper then opens a `QMessageBox::question` "¿Imprimir una segunda copia de la factura?" (Yes / No, defaulting to No) and re-runs `printTicket()` on the same Excel file if the operator picks Yes. After the print returns, `resetAllContents()` clears the form so the operator can move on to the next client without reopening the window. `on_pb_print_clicked()` is refactored to call the same helper with `askSecondCopy=false` and then `resetAllContents()` (replacing the previous `this->close()`, so the standalone print button no longer dismisses the window either). Both copies use the same Excel parameters (`createTicketExcel(copyForClient=false, addPayedInfo=false)`) so the output is identical - "second copy" just reprints the same file rather than rendering a different variant. The `AppSettings::enablePrinting()` guard wraps both the first print and the second-copy dialog, so testing-mode runs still generate the Excel but skip the printer and the dialog. Caveat: when "Pagar todo" is pressed on a multi-ticket search (e.g. name search returning all of a client's tickets), only the last paid ticket gets printed - separate `printFactura` calls per `n_recibo` would be needed to cover that, deferred until the multi-ticket case is actually exercised. A `qDebug` was added at the top of `printFactura` showing `ticket` and `askSecondCopy` so the operator can confirm the flow ran via `~/.laideal.log`. Files touched: `src/recog_prendas/recog_prendas.h/.cpp`.
- [x] **`RecogPrendas` name search silently dropped tickets past row 256** (no log was registered because the function had no entry-time `qDebug`): the name-search branch did `db.open() / SELECT * FROM ingresos / setQuery() / db.close()` and then, ~30 lines later in the common post-search section, ran `while (sqlQueryModel->canFetchMore()) sqlQueryModel->fetchMore();` to drain the rest. But `QSqlQueryModel` lazy-fetches in 256-row batches and `fetchMore()` needs the underlying SQLite statement handle to still be valid, which `db.close()` had already invalidated. So `canFetchMore()` returned `false` immediately and the proxy filter only ever saw the first 256 rows of `ingresos` - any client whose receipts landed past that point would have some tickets in the result and some silently missing, regardless of whether the search term was a partial or complete name. Fix: pulled the `fetchMore()` loop **inside** the name branch, ordered as `setQuery -> fetchMore-until-done -> db.close()`, and dropped the now-redundant common loop. Added three `qDebug` lines so the search is now visible in `~/.laideal.log`: (1) at function entry, showing the input and the date-mode combobox value; (2) after the SELECT, showing how many source rows were loaded and the normalized filter; (3) at the end, showing `proxyMatched / sourceLoaded`. The same `db.close()` pattern exists in the phone / ticket / date branches but doesn't trigger the bug there because each of those returns a small result set (single client, single ticket, single day) that fits in one 256-row batch. Files touched: `src/recog_prendas/recog_prendas.cpp`.
- [x] **Streamlined release pipeline: one PowerShell command builds + deploys + installs**: the previous flow required opening the Qt Creator IDE, building Release manually, closing Qt Creator, opening a special "Qt command prompt" shortcut (`cmd.exe /K C:\Qt\6.4.3\mingw_64\bin\qtenv2.bat`) with admin rights, and running `deploy_laideal_run_in_qt_cmd.bat` which chained three other batch scripts (`zip_folder.bat`, `find_replace.bat`, `create_installer.bat`). The deploy script searched for Qt Creator's specific shadow-build folder (`build-laideal-Desktop_Qt_*_MinGW_64_bit-Release`), so the README's `cmake -B build` instructions actually produced an exe in a folder the deploy script could not find - a hidden disconnect that broke the documented build path. Replaced by a single `releases\release.ps1 <version>` invocation (with `releases\release.bat <version>` as a thin cmd.exe wrapper) that: (1) prepends Qt + MinGW to `PATH` itself so the Qt cmd shortcut is no longer required, (2) cross-checks the supplied version against `project(laideal VERSION X.Y ...)` in `CMakeLists.txt` and aborts on mismatch, (3) refuses to overwrite an existing zip/installer/staging dir, (4) configures CMake (Release, MinGW Makefiles) into `build-release/`, (5) builds with `-j`, (6) stages the exe into `releases\<version>\` and runs `windeployqt --release --no-translations --no-system-d3d-compiler`, (7) zips into `releases\old_releases\<version>.zip` via .NET `System.IO.Compression.FileSystem`, (8) compiles the Inno Setup installer via `ISCC.exe /DMyAppVersion=<version> laideal.iss`, (9) deletes the staging dir. The `laideal.iss` template was rewritten to use ISCC preprocessor `#define MyAppVersion` (with `#ifndef` guard so it still compiles standalone with a 0.0 default), and all paths inside it switched from absolute to relative-to-iss (`..\License.txt`, `..\releases_notes.txt`, `..\icon\lavadora.ico`, `setup_outputs`, `{#MyAppVersion}\*`) so the installer recipe is portable to any clone of the repo, not just this developer's machine. The four obsolete batch scripts were deleted (`deploy_laideal_run_in_qt_cmd.bat`, `zip_folder.bat`, `find_replace.bat`, `create_installer.bat`) - the brittle `find_replace.bat` sed-replace of "1.0" in the iss is now redundant since ISCC handles substitution natively. `.gitignore` updated: dropped the now-unused `laideal_upd.iss` entry, added `build-release` alongside `build`. README's "Build" section now shows the explicit `-G "MinGW Makefiles" -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release` flags (so a fresh CMake configure picks the right toolchain even on machines with Visual Studio installed), and the "Release procedure" collapsed from 12 manual steps to 5 (bump version + commit, run script, tag, push, gh release). Files touched: `releases/release.ps1` (new), `releases/release.bat` (new), `releases/laideal.iss` (rewritten), `.gitignore`, `README.md`; deleted `releases/deploy_laideal_run_in_qt_cmd.bat`, `releases/zip_folder.bat`, `releases/find_replace.bat`, `releases/create_installer.bat`.

## Archive (Entries older than 6 months / no longer actionable)

_Pre-8.0 development history. The `feature/add_mdiago_verifactu` entries below are the original Verifactu build-out, all shipped in 8.0; moved here from Completed Milestones on 2026-09-14 to keep the active section readable._

### Pre-release bug fixes — May 2026 (`feature/add_mdiago_verifactu`)
- [x] **Code-review fixes for v8.0 release** (twelve findings from the branch review): (1) **ServiceKey log leak** [release-blocker]: `VerifactuManager::submitInvoiceAsync` was dumping the full `/Create` JSON payload - including `ServiceKey` - to `~/.laideal.log` on every paid ticket save and every "Probar conexion". Now redacted to `[redacted, N chars]` before logging. Other request-emitting paths (cancel / QR / testConnection) were already safe (they log endpoint URLs only). (2) **`Listado` allowed inline edit of `ingresos`** via `QSqlTableModel::OnFieldChange` - including `verifactu_csv`/`verifactu_estado`/`importe`/etc. - which contradicts Verifactu Req. 1 (Art. 8.1 RD 1007/2023). Added `setEditTriggers(NoEditTriggers)` on the `ingresos` table view; all changes must now flow through RecogPrendas / Cancel / Rectify which keep AEAT and the accounting lock in sync. (3) **`insertNewItemToTable` built SQL with raw quote concatenation** of user values - a client named `O'Brien` would have crashed, and a hostile input could have injected DDL. Replaced with `q.prepare("... VALUES (?, ?, ...)")` + positional `addBindValue` so values are bound by the driver, never interpolated. (4) **`AppSettings::save()` was non-atomic**: a crash mid-write produced a zero-byte JSON and the next launch saw "first run" (DB path lost, Verifactu credentials lost). Now writes to `<path>.tmp` first, flushes, then `QFile::rename` over the target - previous file survives a crash. (5) **Operator-precedence ambiguity in `MySortFilterProxyModel::lessThan`**: the `&&`/`||` chain happened to parse correctly but triggered `-Wparentheses` and was fragile to refactors. Each `table && col` clause is now its own paren group. (6) **`RectifyInvoiceDialog` n_recibo race**: max(n_recibo)+1 was allocated at submit time and INSERTed only on AEAT reply, leaving a seconds-long window where a regular `saveTicket` could re-use the same number. Now eagerly INSERTs a PENDIENTE placeholder row at submit time (same pattern as `MainWindow::saveTicket`); `applyRectificationResult` patches it to ENVIADA/ERROR on reply. (7) **Huella regex was too permissive**: `<[^>]*Huella[^>]*>` would also have matched a hypothetical `<HuellaPrevia>`. Tightened to `<(?:\w+:)?Huella(?:\s[^>]*)?>` so only the exact element (with optional namespace prefix) matches. (8) **`Imprimir::resolveQrCode` checked only row-0 CSV**: a ticket with mixed row states (e.g. some PENDIENTE / RECTIFICADA / ANULADA / ERROR) could still print a QR claiming AEAT validity. Now aggregates across all rows: emits a QR only when at least one row has a CSV AND no row is in a state that contradicts the claim. (9) Annotated the magic `10` in `filterAcceptsRow` - it caps the search to user-facing columns so the hidden verifactu_* metadata never bleeds into the visible filter. (10) `testConnection` reply now held via `QPointer`: defensive against the dialog being closed mid-loop. (11) Documented the intentional NULL verifactu_* columns in the `SEPARATE_GARM` INSERT (split rows must not re-submit to AEAT - would trigger duplicate-InvoiceID). (12) Tier-1 coding-guidelines cleanups: last `SIGNAL`/`SLOT` macro converted to pointer syntax in `tableview.cpp`; Spanish locals `estado`/`cliente`/`fecha`/`estado` renamed to `state`/`client`/`date`/`verifactuState` in `cancelinvoicedialog.cpp`, `imprimir.cpp`, `recog_prendas.cpp`; `add_sufix_to_filename` typo fixed to `add_suffix_to_filename` in `genlistado.h/.cpp`. The remaining Tier-1 item - splitting `verifactuinvoice.h` into two headers - stays in Open Non-Blocking Issues.
